# Kollink 招募與機會頁 Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** 品牌可以用四步驟表單刊登招募（草稿、發佈、編輯、關閉、刪除草稿）並在詳情頁錄取或婉拒報名者；創作者在 `/creator` 依「適合我／全部／內容領域／現金／互惠」瀏覽開放中的招募、看詳情（逐項顯示是否符合條件）、報名與撤回，並在「我的報名」看到結果。

**Architecture:** 資料層（`20261124000500_briefs.sql`）先檢查 `briefs` 必須是空的才繼續，把 `cover_url` 改名為 `cover_path`、加 `published_at` 與 CHECK；寫入全部改走 SECURITY DEFINER 函式（`save_brief`、`close_brief`、`delete_brief`、`apply_to_brief`、`withdraw_application`、`decide_application`），讀取用 RLS（`can_view_brief` definer helper，避免 `briefs` 與 `brief_applications` 的 policy 互相遞迴）加上只給創作者的 `list_opportunities`／`get_opportunity`、只給招募主的 `list_brief_applicants`、`brief_application_counts`。「今天」一律是 `today_tw()`（台灣日期），TS 端 `todayTw()` 用同一條規則並測試同一個跨日邊界。封面放新的公開 bucket `brief-covers`。頁面都在 `<Suspense>` 內 `requireRole` 後以使用者自己的 client 讀取；表單是單一 `<form>`、四步用 `hidden` 切換、步驟用 `history.replaceState` 寫進 `?step=`；機會列表的「載入更多」沿用廣場的 Server Action 模式。

**Tech Stack:** Next.js 16.4.6（App Router、`cacheComponents`、Server Actions、`useActionState`）、React 19.3、TypeScript、Tailwind v4、Supabase CLI 3.2（Postgres 17、Storage）、pgTAP、Vitest、lucide-react。

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-24-briefs-design.md`（含使用者決定；視覺沿用 `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-24-ui-refresh-design.md`；延後事項與既有決定見 `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-11-22-foundation-followups.md`）

## Global Constraints

- Next.js 16 與訓練資料不同：寫程式前先讀 `node_modules/next/dist/docs/` 對應文件（本計畫用到的已列在下方「設計決定」）。`middleware.ts` 已改名為 `proxy.ts`。
- `cacheComponents` 已開啟：讀 `cookies()`、`headers()`、`searchParams`、`params` 的元件必須在 `<Suspense>` 內；不要在 layout 最外層 await session（`node_modules/next/dist/docs/01-app/02-guides/authentication-with-cache-components.md`）。不可使用 `export const runtime`、`export const dynamic`。`todayTw()` 會讀現在時間：伺服器元件裡只能在 `requireRole()`（內部已 `await connection()`）或 `await searchParams` 之後呼叫；client 元件不自己算「今天」，由伺服器當 prop 傳下去。
- 金鑰：`NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL`、`NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY`（瀏覽器可見）、`SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY`（只能出現在 import `server-only` 的檔案，絕不加 `NEXT_PUBLIC_` 前綴）。
- 讀取與寫入一律用**使用者自己的** client（`src/lib/supabase/server.ts`；瀏覽器上傳用 `src/lib/supabase/client.ts`），不可使用 `createAdminClient()`（E2E 的本機種子資料除外，那是在終端機用 SQL／Storage API 建立，不是 app 程式碼）。
- SQL 函式一律 `set search_path = ''`，物件名稱寫完整 schema；`RETURNS TABLE` 的 plpgsql 函式加 `#variable_conflict use_column`，欄位一律加表別名。
- 權限寫在 migration（followups「資料表權限寫在 migration」：雲端不會自動授權、本機會）：資料表先 `revoke all ... from anon, authenticated, service_role` 再明確 `grant`；新函式先 `revoke execute ... from public, anon, authenticated, service_role` 再 `grant` 給需要的角色。只給 definer 函式內部用的 helper 不 grant 給任何 API 角色。
- 寫入一律經由 definer 函式：`briefs`、`brief_applications` 不給任何 API 角色 INSERT／UPDATE／DELETE（anon 連 SELECT 都沒有）。
- 錯誤碼：身分不符 `42501`（`not authenticated`、`not a brand`、`not a creator`、`creator not approved`、`not brief owner`）；找不到 `P0002`；狀態不允許 `55000`；格式 `22023 invalid <欄位>`；重複報名 `23505 already applied`。TS 端只透過 `src/lib/briefs/errors.ts` 轉成中文。
- 文字欄位：JS 端 `cleanText`／`cleanTextWithin` 與 SQL 端 `clean_text` 清理、`has_bidi` 拒絕，兩邊字元集合不變（見 `src/lib/validation/onboarding.ts` 註解）。NUL（U+0000）：Postgres text 存不進去（PostgREST 直接回 22P05，資料庫函式根本收不到），所以招募與報名表單在 JS 端用 `hasBadChar`（= bidi 或 NUL）擋下；其他既有表單維持原狀，記在 followups。
- 清單以外的值要能容忍（followups「4. 創作者廣場、5. 招募與機會頁」）：網址的 `?tab=`、`?cat=`、`?step=` 任何值都不能讓頁面或資料庫報錯；表單裡資料庫既有的清單外的值（合作形式、內容領域、地區）照樣顯示、可以取消。白名單只在 UI 端。
- 每個讀取使用者資料的頁面與 Server Action 都要呼叫 `requireRole("brand")` 或 `requireRole("creator")`（layout 的 Gate 不算數）。
- Server Action 是公開的 POST 端點：參數一律當成 `unknown` 檢查型別與範圍；id 一律用 `isUuid` 檢查（`node_modules/next/dist/docs/01-app/02-guides/server-actions.md`「Security」）。
- `supabase gen types` 產生的 RPC 回傳型別把 `RETURNS TABLE` 的欄位都標成非 null，實際上可能是 null：mapper 一律用自己定義、欄位可為 null 的 Row 型別（`src/lib/briefs/view.ts`），`data` 先轉成 `unknown` 再轉型。
- 介面文字為繁體中文；產品名稱只從 `src/lib/site.ts` 的 `SITE_NAME` 取得。
- 顏色只用 `src/app/globals.css` 的 token class：`bg-bg`、`bg-panel`、`bg-surface`、`border-line`、`divide-line`、`text-ink`、`bg-ink`、`text-muted`、`text-on-ink`、`text-primary`、`bg-primary`、`bg-primary-soft`、`text-success`、`bg-success-soft`、`text-danger`、`bg-danger`、`border-danger`、`ring-ink`、`accent-ink`；圓角 `rounded-card`、`rounded-btn`、`rounded-nav`、`rounded-input`。不可用 `text-white` 或其他色碼。
- 手機 390 寬不可出現整頁水平捲軸：可左右捲動的容器加 `relative`（裡面有 `sr-only` 文字時，否則 sr-only 的絕對定位會撐出整頁捲軸）；長文字加 `break-words`／`min-w-0`。
- 確認一律用頁面內的確認區塊（`ConfirmAction`），不可用瀏覽器的 `confirm()`。
- 不新增 npm 套件。圖示用已安裝的 `lucide-react`，`strokeWidth={1.5}`、`aria-hidden="true"`。
- 只動**本機** Supabase：不可執行 `supabase link`、`db push --remote`、`supabase config push`，也不可連到正式專案。
- 不可同時跑兩個 `next build`／`next dev`／`next start`（共用 `.next`）；同一時間只能有一個 agent 在跑這些指令。只有 Task 10 會啟動伺服器（一個 dev server、一個 port），用完要關掉。
- 每個 task 結束時：`npm run lint && npm run typecheck && npm test` 全過；有動到 SQL 的 task 另跑 `npx supabase test db` 全過。畫面元件沒有單元測試環境（vitest 是 node 環境），由 typecheck、lint、Task 10 的 build 與 E2E 驗證。
- Commit 訊息用中文寫「計畫 5 Task N：重點」，結尾加你所在環境指定的 Co-Authored-By 歸屬行（寫實際執行的模型）。
- 前置：Docker Desktop 已啟動，`npx supabase start` 已執行，`.env.local` 已設定（見 README「本機開發」）。

## 設計決定

讀過的文件：
- `node_modules/next/dist/docs/01-app/03-api-reference/03-file-conventions/page.md`（`searchParams` 是 Promise，Request-time API，要在 `<Suspense>` 內 await）
- `node_modules/next/dist/docs/01-app/03-api-reference/03-file-conventions/dynamic-routes.md`（「Without `generateStaticParams`」：params 是 runtime data，必須在 `<Suspense>` 內讀）
- `node_modules/next/dist/docs/01-app/02-guides/server-actions.md`（「A single response carries data and UI」：`revalidatePath`／`redirect` 會在同一個回應帶回新畫面，`redirect` 之後的程式不會執行，revalidate 要放在 redirect 前；沒有呼叫時只回傳值；「Security」：action 是公開的 POST）
- `node_modules/next/dist/docs/01-app/02-guides/forms.md`（`useActionState`：action 第一個參數是前一次的 state，`pending` 用來停用按鈕）
- `node_modules/next/dist/docs/01-app/01-getting-started/04-linking-and-navigating.md`、`node_modules/next/dist/docs/01-app/02-guides/single-page-applications.md`（「native History API」：`window.history.replaceState(null, '', url)` 會整合進 router，不重新向伺服器要資料）
- `node_modules/next/dist/docs/01-app/03-api-reference/04-functions/not-found.md`（「Calling `notFound()` after streaming has started」：在 `<Suspense>` 內呼叫時回應碼仍是 200，Next 會加 `noindex`；畫面是 not-found UI）
- `node_modules/next/dist/docs/01-app/03-api-reference/04-functions/use-router.md`（`router.bfcacheId`，廣場已用來重設結果區）
- `node_modules/next/dist/docs/01-app/03-api-reference/04-functions/revalidatePath.md`

決定：
1. **台灣日期與測試用的時間旅行。** SQL 的 `today_tw(p_now timestamptz default now())` 是純計算；所有 policy 與函式一律**不帶參數**呼叫，所以呼叫者無法影響截止日判斷。pgTAP 用 `today_tw('2026-11-24 15:59:59+00')`／`'… 16:00:00+00'` 驗證跨日邊界，用 `today_tw()`、`today_tw() - 1` 當 fixture 的截止日驗證「當天可以、隔天不行」。不用 GUC（例如 `set kollink.now`）：GUC 可以被任何連線設定，等於讓正式環境多一個可被濫用的「現在時間」開關。TS 端 `todayTw(now = new Date())` 以 `Intl` 的 `Asia/Taipei` 計算，測試同一組 UTC 邊界。
2. **migration 的保護。** `20261124000500_briefs.sql` 開頭若 `briefs` 有任何一列就 `raise exception` 停下來（spec：改名前確認正式資料庫沒有招募資料）。Task 1 用 psql 在 rollback 的 transaction 裡實際驗證這段會擋下來。
3. **草稿可以缺必填。** spec「草稿只檢查格式，不檢查必填」→ `title`、`description`、`deadline` 改成可為 null；`briefs_published_complete` CHECK 保證非草稿一定齊全；`status` 預設改成 `draft`；`(status = 'draft') = (published_at is null)`。截止日的「今天到 90 天內」只在發佈與修改發佈中的招募時檢查（舊草稿的過期截止日不會讓它無法再存草稿）。
4. **預算 0 等於沒填。** `save_brief` 把 0 轉成 null（CHECK 保證存進去的預算是 1..10,000,000），所以「現金」篩選、「預算或產品至少一項」、酬勞文字三者對「0」的解讀一致。
5. **可見性用 definer helper `can_view_brief`。** `briefs_select` 需要知道「我報名過這則」，而 `brief_applications_select` 又要查 `briefs`；直接寫 `exists` 會造成 policy 互相遞迴。helper 以 definer 讀 `brief_applications`，同一個 helper 也給 `brief_application_counts` 用，條件只寫一份。品牌看不到其他品牌的招募（spec YAGNI）。
6. **機會詳情用 `get_opportunity(p_id)`。** spec 列的 `list_opportunities` 只列開放中的招募，但詳情頁要能顯示「報名過的已關閉／已截止招募」與四項是否符合，所以另寫一個只給創作者的 definer 函式，和列表共用 `match_brief(briefs, creator_profiles)`（四項規則只寫一份，回傳 composite type `brief_match`）。草稿與看不到的招募回傳 0 列 → 頁面 `notFound()`。
7. **「內容領域」篩選只列明確包含該領域的招募**（「不限」的招募不算），清單外的領域不報錯、只是沒有結果。不認得的 `p_filter` 當成「適合我」。
8. **報名者列表一次回傳全部欄位。** `list_brief_applicants` 回傳報名（留言、報價、狀態、時間）與創作者公開資料（名稱、頭像、粉絲、互動率、地區），品牌不必另外 select `brief_applications`。篩選（全部／待處理／已錄取／已婉拒）只在畫面上（client state），不寫進網址；已撤回的只出現在「全部」。
9. **人數。** `brief_application_counts` 的 `applicant_count` 只算 `applied`、`accepted`；`pending_count`（`applied`）只有招募主看得到，其他人是 null。
10. **四步驟表單。** 四步的欄位都在同一個 `<form>`，非目前步驟用 `hidden`，所以 FormData 永遠含全部欄位、伺服器能完整驗證。步驟存在 React state，變更時 `history.replaceState` 寫進 `?step=`（重新整理停在同一步；不產生歷史紀錄，也不觸發伺服器渲染）。「下一步」只驗證這一步；「存成草稿」與「發佈」送出前前端先驗證，有錯就跳到有錯的第一步；伺服器回傳欄位錯誤時同樣跳過去。**新**草稿存檔後 redirect 到 `/brand/briefs/<id>/edit?step=<目前步驟>&saved=1`（之後重新整理會從資料庫讀回來）；**既有**草稿存檔留在原頁、回傳 `{ saved: true }`。發佈 redirect 到 `/brand/briefs/<id>`。發佈中的招募只有「儲存變更」（intent=publish），不能存回草稿。
11. **封面。** 公開 bucket `brief-covers`，路徑 `<uid>/cover-<uuid>.<ext>`；上傳在瀏覽器用品牌自己的 client（同 `creator-media`）。換掉這次上傳的檔案時盡力刪除；已存進資料庫的舊封面不刪；刪除草稿時 `delete_brief` 回傳封面路徑，Server Action 盡力刪檔。其他孤兒檔交給共用的清理排程（待辦）。
12. **機會頁網址。** `/creator`（適合我）、`?tab=all|cash|product|applied`、`?cat=<內容領域>`（cat 優先）。「載入更多」是 `loadMoreOpportunitiesAction(queryString, offset)`（一次 20 則），結果區以 `key={`${router.bfcacheId}|${queryKey}`}` 重設，與廣場相同。「我的報名」一次列最多 100 筆，不分頁（YAGNI）。
13. **404。** 頁面在 `<Suspense>` 內呼叫 `notFound()`：回應碼是 200 + `noindex`、畫面是 not-found UI（與 `/c/[handle]` 相同的已接受取捨）。網址的 id 不是 UUID 時直接 `notFound()`，不送進資料庫。
14. **未審核創作者。** 可以瀏覽與看詳情；報名按鈕停用並顯示「審核通過後即可報名」；Server Action 先擋，資料庫 `apply_to_brief` 再擋（`42501 creator not approved`）。
15. **`cleanText` 不能加參數。** 它常被當成 `.map(cleanText)` 用（map 會把索引傳進第二個參數），所以 2000 字的說明改用新的 `cleanTextWithin(v, maxRaw)`。

---

### Task 1: 資料庫：招募欄位、可見性、寫入函式、機會與報名者函式

**Files:**
- Create: `supabase/migrations/20261124000500_briefs.sql`、`supabase/tests/11_briefs.test.sql`
- Modify: `supabase/tests/02_briefs_messaging_rls.test.sql`、`src/lib/supabase/database.types.ts`（產生）

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `public.my_role()`、`public.is_admin()`、`public.clean_text(text)`、`public.has_bidi(text)`、`public.check_text_list(text[], text, integer, integer)`、`public.check_media_path(uuid, text, text, text)`（既有 migration）。
- Produces（後面的 task 依賴這些名稱與型別）：
  - `briefs`：`cover_url` → `cover_path text`（null 或 `<brand_id>/cover-<檔名>`）、新增 `published_at timestamptz`；`title`、`description`、`deadline` 可為 null（只有草稿）；`status` 預設 `draft`。
  - `today_tw(p_now timestamptz default now()) → date`（authenticated、service_role 可執行）。
  - `save_brief(p_id uuid, p_title text, p_description text, p_collab_types text[], p_categories text[], p_min_followers integer, p_region text, p_budget_min_twd integer, p_budget_max_twd integer, p_includes_product boolean, p_deadline date, p_cover_path text, p_publish boolean) → uuid`，全部參數有預設值。
  - `close_brief(p_id uuid) → void`；`delete_brief(p_id uuid) → text`（被刪草稿的封面路徑或 null）。
  - `apply_to_brief(p_brief_id uuid, p_message text default null, p_proposed_price integer default null) → uuid`；`withdraw_application(p_id uuid) → void`；`decide_application(p_id uuid, p_accept boolean) → void`。
  - `brief_application_counts(p_ids uuid[])` → 列 `(brief_id uuid, applicant_count bigint, pending_count bigint)`。
  - `list_opportunities(p_filter text default 'match', p_category text default null, p_limit integer default 20, p_offset integer default 0)` → 列 `(id, title, collab_types, categories, min_followers, region, budget_min_twd, budget_max_twd, includes_product, deadline, cover_path, brand_id, brand_name, brand_logo_path, applicant_count, followers_ok, region_ok, category_ok, collab_ok, is_match, has_applied, my_status, total_count)`。
  - `get_opportunity(p_id uuid)` → 0 或 1 列 `(id, title, description, status, collab_types, categories, min_followers, region, budget_min_twd, budget_max_twd, includes_product, deadline, cover_path, brand_id, brand_name, brand_logo_path, applicant_count, followers_ok, region_ok, category_ok, collab_ok, is_match, my_application_id, my_status, my_message, my_proposed_price_twd)`。
  - `list_brief_applicants(p_brief_id uuid)` → 列 `(id, creator_id, handle, display_name, avatar_path, follower_count, engagement_rate, region, message, proposed_price_twd, status, created_at)`。
  - 錯誤訊息（`errcode`）：`not authenticated`／`not a brand`／`not a creator`／`creator not approved`／`not brief owner`（42501）、`brief not found`／`application not found`（P0002）、`brief not editable`／`brief not open`／`brief not draft`／`brief closed`／`application not pending`（55000）、`invalid <title|description|collab_types|categories|region|min_followers|budget_min_twd|budget_max_twd|reward|deadline|cover_path|message|proposed_price_twd|accept>`（22023）、`already applied`（23505）。

- [ ] **Step 1: 寫失敗的測試 `supabase/tests/11_briefs.test.sql`**

測試先在自己的 transaction 裡刪掉本機既有的招募（被合作案引用的改成草稿），結束時 rollback 還原；所有 fixture 都在這個 transaction 內，不會影響 `01`、`05` 這些會數列數的舊測試。

```sql
begin;
create extension if not exists pgtap with schema extensions;
select plan(138);

-- 本機資料庫可能有 E2E 留下的招募：在這個 transaction 裡先刪掉（結束時 rollback 還原），
-- 讓計數只看本檔的 fixture。被合作案引用的招募刪不掉，改成草稿（創作者看不到）。
update public.briefs b set status = 'draft', published_at = null
 where exists (select 1 from public.collaborations c where c.brief_id = b.id);
delete from public.briefs b where not exists (select 1 from public.collaborations c where c.brief_id = b.id);

-- ── B. 權限：直接寫入一律被拒絕；anon 沒有任何權限 ──
select is(has_table_privilege('anon', 'public.briefs', 'select,insert,update,delete'), false, 'anon 對 briefs 沒有任何權限');
select is(has_table_privilege('anon', 'public.brief_applications', 'select,insert,update,delete'), false, 'anon 對 brief_applications 沒有任何權限');
select is(has_function_privilege('anon', 'public.save_brief(uuid, text, text, text[], text[], integer, text, integer, integer, boolean, date, text, boolean)', 'execute'),
  false, 'anon 不能呼叫 save_brief');
select is(has_function_privilege('anon', 'public.list_opportunities(text, text, integer, integer)', 'execute'),
  false, 'anon 不能呼叫 list_opportunities');

set local role authenticated;
select set_config('request.jwt.claims', '{"sub":"00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000601","role":"authenticated"}', true);
select throws_ok(
  $$insert into public.briefs (brand_id, title) values ('00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000601', '直接新增')$$,
  '42501', null, '品牌不能直接新增招募');

select throws_ok(
  $$delete from public.briefs where id = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f004'$$,
  '42501', null, '品牌不能直接刪除招募');
select throws_ok(

  '42501', null, '品牌不能直接改報名狀態');
select set_config('request.jwt.claims', '{"sub":"00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000611","role":"authenticated"}', true);
select throws_ok(
  $$insert into public.brief_applications (brief_id, creator_id) values ('00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f001', auth.uid())$$,
  '42501', null, '創作者不能直接新增報名');

-- ── E. 撤回 ──
select set_config('request.jwt.claims', '{"sub":"00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000612","role":"authenticated"}', true);
select throws_ok(
  $$select public.withdraw_application('00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006a005')$$,
  'P0002', 'application not found', '不能撤回別人的報名');
select set_config('request.jwt.claims', '{"sub":"00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000611","role":"authenticated"}', true);
select lives_ok(
  format('select public.withdraw_application(%L)',
    (select id from public.brief_applications where brief_id = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f002' and creator_id = auth.uid())),
  '創作者可以撤回自己待回覆的報名');
select is(
  (select status from public.brief_applications where brief_id = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f002' and creator_id = auth.uid()),
  'withdrawn', '撤回後狀態是 withdrawn');
select throws_ok(
  format('select public.withdraw_application(%L)',
    (select id from public.brief_applications where brief_id = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f002' and creator_id = auth.uid())),
  '55000', 'application not pending', '已撤回的不能再撤回');
select throws_ok(
  $$select public.withdraw_application('00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006a005')$$,
  '55000', 'application not pending', '已錄取的不能撤回');
select throws_ok(
  $$select public.apply_to_brief('00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f002')$$,
  '23505', 'already applied', '撤回後不能再報名同一則');

-- ── F. 錄取與婉拒 ──
select set_config('request.jwt.claims', '{"sub":"00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000602","role":"authenticated"}', true);
select throws_ok(
  $$select public.decide_application('00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006a003', true)$$,
  'P0002', 'application not found', '別的品牌不能錄取');
select set_config('request.jwt.claims', '{"sub":"00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000601","role":"authenticated"}', true);
select lives_ok(
  format('select public.decide_application(%L, true)',
    (select id from public.brief_applications where brief_id = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f001' and creator_id = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000611')),
  '招募主可以錄取');
select is(
  (select status from public.brief_applications where brief_id = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f001' and creator_id = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000611'),
  'accepted', '錄取後狀態是 accepted');
select throws_ok(
  format('select public.decide_application(%L, false)',
    (select id from public.brief_applications where brief_id = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f001' and creator_id = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000611')),
  '55000', 'application not pending', '錄取後不能再改');
select lives_ok(
  $$select public.decide_application('00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006a003', false)$$,
  '截止後招募主仍可以婉拒');
select is(
  (select status from public.brief_applications where id = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006a003'),
  'rejected', '婉拒後狀態是 rejected');
select throws_ok(
  $$select public.decide_application('00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006a003', null)$$,
  '22023', 'invalid accept', '必須指定錄取或婉拒');
select set_config('request.jwt.claims', '{"sub":"00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000611","role":"authenticated"}', true);
select throws_ok(
  $$select public.decide_application('00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006a061', true)$$,
  'P0002', 'application not found', '創作者不能錄取');

-- ── G. 報名人數 ──
select set_config('request.jwt.claims', '{"sub":"00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000602","role":"authenticated"}', true);
select is(
  (select applicant_count || ':' || pending_count from public.brief_application_counts(array['00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f006'::uuid])),
  '1:1', '人數只算 applied 與 accepted；招募主看得到待處理人數');
select is(
  (select count(*)::int from public.brief_application_counts(array['00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f001'::uuid])),
  0, '看不到的招募不回傳人數');
select set_config('request.jwt.claims', '{"sub":"00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000611","role":"authenticated"}', true);
select is(
  (select applicant_count || ':' || coalesce(pending_count::text, 'null')
     from public.brief_application_counts(array['00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f006'::uuid])),
  '1:null', '創作者看得到人數，看不到待處理人數');
select is(
  (select count(*)::int from public.brief_application_counts(array['00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f004'::uuid])),
  0, '創作者看不到草稿的人數');
select is(
  (select applicant_count::int from public.brief_application_counts(array['00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f001'::uuid])),
  1, '已錄取也算在人數裡');

-- ── H. 機會列表 ──
-- 小美（已審核）
select is((select count(*)::int from public.list_opportunities('all')), 7, '全部：開放中未截止的 7 則');
select is((select total_count::int from public.list_opportunities('all') limit 1), 7, 'total_count 是總數');
select is((select id from public.list_opportunities('all') limit 1), '00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f002'::uuid, '依截止日由近到遠');
select is((select count(*)::int from public.list_opportunities('all', null, 2, 0)), 2, 'p_limit 限制筆數');
select is((select total_count::int from public.list_opportunities('all', null, 2, 0) limit 1), 7, '分頁時 total_count 仍是總數');
select is((select count(*)::int from public.list_opportunities('all', null, 20, 6)), 1, 'p_offset 跳過前面的');
select is(
  (select array_agg(id order by deadline) from public.list_opportunities('match')),
  array['00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f002', '00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f001']::uuid[],
  '適合我：只列四項都符合的');
select is((select count(*)::int from public.list_opportunities()), 2, '預設是適合我');
select is((select count(*)::int from public.list_opportunities('bogus')), 2, '不認得的篩選當成適合我');
select is(
  (select array[followers_ok, region_ok, category_ok, collab_ok]::text from public.list_opportunities('all')
    where id = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f009'), '{f,t,t,t}', '粉絲數低於門檻：只有粉絲不符合');
select is(
  (select array[followers_ok, region_ok, category_ok, collab_ok]::text from public.list_opportunities('all')
    where id = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f010'), '{t,f,t,t}', '粉絲剛好等於門檻算符合；地區不同不符合');
select is(
  (select array[followers_ok, region_ok, category_ok, collab_ok]::text from public.list_opportunities('all')
    where id = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f011'), '{t,t,f,t}', '內容領域沒有交集不符合');
select is(
  (select array[followers_ok, region_ok, category_ok, collab_ok]::text from public.list_opportunities('all')
    where id = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f012'), '{t,t,t,f}', '合作形式沒有交集不符合');
select is(
  (select is_match from public.list_opportunities('all') where id = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f012'),
  false, '任一項不符合 is_match 就是 false');
select is(
  (select array_agg(id order by deadline) from public.list_opportunities('cash')),
  array['00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f006', '00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f001']::uuid[],
  '現金：有預算的');
select is((select count(*)::int from public.list_opportunities('product')), 5, '互惠：提供產品的');
select is(
  (select array_agg(id) from public.list_opportunities('category', '美妝')),
  array['00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f001']::uuid[], '內容領域：只列明確包含的（不限的不算）');
select is((select count(*)::int from public.list_opportunities('category', '不存在的領域')), 0, '清單以外的內容領域不報錯，只是沒有結果');
select is((select count(*)::int from public.list_opportunities('category', null)), 0, '內容領域篩選沒給領域時沒有結果');
select is(
  (select has_applied::text || ':' || my_status from public.list_opportunities('all') where id = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f001'),
  'true:accepted', '帶出自己是否報名與狀態');
select is(
  (select has_applied from public.list_opportunities('all') where id = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f009'),
  false, '沒報名的是 false');
select is(
  (select applicant_count::int from public.list_opportunities('all') where id = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f006'),
  1, '列表的人數只算 applied 與 accepted');
select is(
  (select brand_name || ':' || brand_logo_path from public.list_opportunities('all') where id = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f001'),
  '品牌甲:00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000601/logo.png', '帶出品牌名稱與 Logo 路徑');
-- 阿明：沒填想接的合作 → 合作形式不檢查
select set_config('request.jwt.claims', '{"sub":"00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000612","role":"authenticated"}', true);
select is(
  (select collab_ok from public.list_opportunities('all') where id = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f012'),
  true, '創作者沒填想接的合作時不檢查合作形式');
select is(
  (select is_match from public.list_opportunities('all') where id = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f006'),
  true, '阿明符合 f06');
-- 新人（未審核、沒填地區）
select set_config('request.jwt.claims', '{"sub":"00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000613","role":"authenticated"}', true);
select is((select count(*)::int from public.list_opportunities('all')), 7, '未審核創作者也可以瀏覽機會');
select is(
  (select region_ok from public.list_opportunities('all') where id = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f001'),
  false, '創作者沒填地區時，限定地區的招募不符合');
select set_config('request.jwt.claims', '{"sub":"00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000601","role":"authenticated"}', true);
select throws_ok($$select * from public.list_opportunities('all')$$, '42501', 'not a creator', '品牌不能讀機會列表');

-- ── I. 機會詳情 ──
select throws_ok($$select * from public.get_opportunity('00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f001')$$, '42501', 'not a creator', '品牌不能讀機會詳情');
select set_config('request.jwt.claims', '{"sub":"00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000611","role":"authenticated"}', true);
select is(
  (select description || ':' || is_match::text || ':' || my_status from public.get_opportunity('00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f001')),
  '說明:true:accepted', '詳情帶出說明、是否符合、自己的報名');
select is((select count(*)::int from public.get_opportunity('00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f003')), 0, '沒報名過的已截止招募不回傳');
select is((select count(*)::int from public.get_opportunity('00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f004')), 0, '草稿不回傳');
select is(
  (select status from public.get_opportunity('00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f005')),
  'closed', '報名過的已關閉招募仍可以看詳情');

-- ── J. 報名者列表 ──
select throws_ok($$select * from public.list_brief_applicants('00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f001')$$, '42501', 'not brief owner', '創作者不能讀報名者');
select set_config('request.jwt.claims', '{"sub":"00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000602","role":"authenticated"}', true);
select throws_ok($$select * from public.list_brief_applicants('00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f001')$$, '42501', 'not brief owner', '別的品牌不能讀報名者');
select set_config('request.jwt.claims', '{"sub":"00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000601","role":"authenticated"}', true);
select is(
  (select handle || ':' || display_name || ':' || follower_count || ':' || status
     from public.list_brief_applicants('00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f001')),
  'bf_mei:小美:5000:accepted', '招募主讀得到報名者的公開資料與狀態');

-- ── K. 新增與修改招募 ──
select isnt(public.save_brief(p_title => E'  只有案名​ '), null, '草稿只要格式正確，不檢查必填');
select is(
  (select status || ':' || coalesce(published_at::text, 'null') from public.briefs where title = '只有案名'),
  'draft:null', '案名經過 clean_text；草稿沒有 published_at');
select lives_ok(
  $$select public.save_brief(p_title => '新品開箱', p_description => '說明', p_collab_types => array['IG 貼文'],
    p_budget_min_twd => 0, p_budget_max_twd => 5000, p_deadline => public.today_tw() + 7, p_publish => true)$$,
  '完整資料可以發佈');
select is(
  (select status || ':' || (published_at is not null)::text || ':' || coalesce(budget_min_twd::text, 'null')
     from public.briefs where title = '新品開箱'),
  'open:true:null', '發佈後是 open、有 published_at；預算 0 存成 null');
select throws_ok(
  $$select public.save_brief(p_title => 'x', p_description => 'y', p_includes_product => true,
    p_deadline => public.today_tw() + 7, p_publish => true)$$,
  '22023', 'invalid collab_types', '發佈時至少要有一種合作形式');
select throws_ok(
  $$select public.save_brief(p_title => 'x', p_description => 'y', p_collab_types => array['IG 貼文'],
    p_deadline => public.today_tw() + 7, p_publish => true)$$,
  '22023', 'invalid reward', '發佈時要有預算或產品');
select throws_ok(
  $$select public.save_brief(p_title => 'x', p_collab_types => array['IG 貼文'], p_includes_product => true,
    p_deadline => public.today_tw() + 7, p_publish => true)$$,
  '22023', 'invalid description', '發佈時要有說明');
select throws_ok(
  $$select public.save_brief(p_title => 'x', p_description => 'y', p_collab_types => array['IG 貼文'],
    p_includes_product => true, p_deadline => public.today_tw() - 1, p_publish => true)$$,
  '22023', 'invalid deadline', '截止日不能早於今天');
select lives_ok(
  $$select public.save_brief(p_title => '今天截止新案', p_description => 'y', p_collab_types => array['IG 貼文'],
    p_includes_product => true, p_deadline => public.today_tw(), p_publish => true)$$,
  '截止日可以是今天');
select throws_ok(
  $$select public.save_brief(p_title => 'x', p_description => 'y', p_collab_types => array['IG 貼文'],
    p_includes_product => true, p_deadline => public.today_tw() + 91, p_publish => true)$$,
  '22023', 'invalid deadline', '截止日最多 90 天後');
select lives_ok(
  $$select public.save_brief(p_title => '九十天後', p_description => 'y', p_collab_types => array['IG 貼文'],
    p_includes_product => true, p_deadline => public.today_tw() + 90, p_publish => true)$$,
  '截止日可以是 90 天後');
select throws_ok(
  $$select public.save_brief(p_title => E'a‮b')$$, '22023', 'invalid title', '案名不能有雙向控制字元');
select throws_ok(
  $$select public.save_brief(p_title => 'x', p_budget_min_twd => 5000, p_budget_max_twd => 4000)$$,
  '22023', 'invalid budget_max_twd', '預算上限不能低於下限');
select throws_ok(
  $$select public.save_brief(p_title => 'x', p_categories => array['a', 'b', 'c', 'd', 'e', 'f'])$$,
  '22023', 'invalid categories', '內容領域最多 5 個');
select throws_ok(
  $$select public.save_brief(p_title => 'x', p_cover_path => '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000602/cover-a.jpg')$$,
  '22023', 'invalid cover_path', '封面必須在自己的資料夾');
select lives_ok(
  $$select public.save_brief(p_title => '有封面的草稿', p_cover_path => '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000601/cover-a.jpg')$$,
  '封面在自己的資料夾可以');
select lives_ok(
  $$select public.save_brief(p_id => '00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f004', p_title => '草稿發佈', p_description => 'y',
    p_collab_types => array['Reels'], p_includes_product => true, p_deadline => public.today_tw() + 5, p_publish => true)$$,
  '草稿可以發佈');
select is((select status from public.briefs where id = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f004'), 'open', '草稿發佈後是 open');
select lives_ok(
  $$select public.save_brief(p_id => '00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f001', p_title => '春季保養開箱（改）', p_description => '說明',
    p_collab_types => array['IG 貼文'], p_budget_min_twd => 5000, p_deadline => public.today_tw() + 10)$$,
  '發佈中的招募可以修改');
select is(
  (select status || ':' || title from public.briefs where id = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f001'),
  'open:春季保養開箱（改）', '修改發佈中的招募不會變回草稿');
select throws_ok(
  $$select public.save_brief(p_id => '00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f001', p_title => 'x', p_description => 'y',
    p_collab_types => array['IG 貼文'], p_includes_product => true, p_deadline => public.today_tw() - 1)$$,
  '22023', 'invalid deadline', '發佈中的招募截止日不能改到今天以前');
select throws_ok(
  $$select public.save_brief(p_id => '00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f003', p_title => 'x')$$,
  '55000', 'brief not editable', '已截止的招募不能修改');
select throws_ok(
  $$select public.save_brief(p_id => '00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f005', p_title => 'x')$$,
  '55000', 'brief not editable', '已關閉的招募不能修改');
select set_config('request.jwt.claims', '{"sub":"00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000602","role":"authenticated"}', true);
select throws_ok(
  $$select public.save_brief(p_id => '00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f001', p_title => 'x')$$,
  'P0002', 'brief not found', '不能修改別人的招募');
select set_config('request.jwt.claims', '{"sub":"00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000611","role":"authenticated"}', true);
select throws_ok($$select public.save_brief(p_title => 'x')$$, '42501', 'not a brand', '創作者不能刊登招募');

-- ── L. 關閉與刪除 ──
select set_config('request.jwt.claims', '{"sub":"00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000602","role":"authenticated"}', true);
select throws_ok($$select public.close_brief('00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f002')$$, 'P0002', 'brief not found', '不能關閉別人的招募');
select set_config('request.jwt.claims', '{"sub":"00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000601","role":"authenticated"}', true);
select lives_ok($$select public.close_brief('00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f002')$$, '招募主可以關閉發佈中的招募');
select is((select status from public.briefs where id = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f002'), 'closed', '關閉後是 closed');
select throws_ok($$select public.close_brief('00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f002')$$, '55000', 'brief not open', '已關閉的不能再關閉');
select is((select count(*)::int from public.brief_applications where brief_id = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f002'), 1, '關閉後報名紀錄保留');
select throws_ok($$select public.delete_brief('00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f001')$$, '55000', 'brief not draft', '發佈過的招募不能刪除');
select is(
  public.delete_brief((select id from public.briefs where title = '有封面的草稿')),
  '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000601/cover-a.jpg', '刪除草稿並回傳封面路徑');
select is((select count(*)::int from public.briefs where title = '有封面的草稿'), 0, '草稿已刪除');
select set_config('request.jwt.claims', '{"sub":"00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000602","role":"authenticated"}', true);
select throws_ok(
  format('select public.delete_brief(%L)', (select id from public.briefs where title = '只有案名')),
  'P0002', 'brief not found', '不能刪除別人的草稿');
-- 關閉後創作者的機會列表不再出現
select set_config('request.jwt.claims', '{"sub":"00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000613","role":"authenticated"}', true);
select is(
  (select count(*)::int from public.list_opportunities('all') where id = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f002'),
  0, '關閉後機會列表不再出現');

select * from finish();
rollback;
```

- [ ] **Step 2: 更新 `supabase/tests/02_briefs_messaging_rls.test.sql`**

這個檔案的 fixture 與 4 個斷言依舊規則寫成（開放中的招募沒有合作形式、anon 讀得到、品牌看得到別的品牌的招募、直接 insert 報名）。整個檔案換成：

```sql
begin;
create extension if not exists pgtap with schema extensions;
select plan(12);

insert into auth.users (id, email) values
  ('00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000b1', 'b1@test.local'),
  ('00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000b2', 'b2@test.local'),
  ('00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000c1', 'c1@test.local');
insert into public.profiles (id, role, display_name) values
  ('00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000b1', 'brand', '花粉選物'),
  ('00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000b2', 'brand', '森林寵物食堂'),
  ('00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000c1', 'creator', '小美');
insert into public.brand_profiles (user_id, brand_name) values
  ('00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000b1', '花粉選物'),
  ('00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000b2', '森林寵物食堂');
insert into public.creator_profiles (user_id, handle, ig_username, status) values
  ('00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000c1', 'mei', 'mei.ig', 'approved');

insert into public.briefs
  (id, brand_id, title, description, collab_types, includes_product, deadline, status, published_at) values
  ('00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000000f001', '00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000b1',
   '春季新品開箱', 'IG 貼文 + 限動', array['IG 貼文'], true, public.today_tw() + 7, 'open', now()),
  ('00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000000f002', '00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000b1',
   '已截止的招募', '舊案', array['IG 貼文'], true, public.today_tw() - 1, 'closed', now());

insert into public.conversations (id, brand_id, creator_id) values
  ('00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000000a001',
   '00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000b1', '00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000c1');
insert into public.messages (conversation_id, sender_id, body) values
  ('00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000000a001', '00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000b1', '您好，想邀請您合作');

-- 訪客
set local role anon;
select set_config('request.jwt.claims', '{"role":"anon"}', true);
select throws_ok($$select count(*) from public.briefs$$, '42501', null, '訪客讀不到招募（計畫 5 收回 anon 權限）');

-- 品牌 b2（第三方）
set local role authenticated;
select set_config('request.jwt.claims',
  '{"sub":"00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000b2","role":"authenticated"}', true);
select is((select count(*)::int from public.messages), 0, '第三方看不到別人的訊息');
select is((select count(*)::int from public.conversations), 0, '第三方看不到別人的對話');
select is((select count(*)::int from public.briefs), 0, '品牌看不到其他品牌的招募（計畫 5）');
select throws_ok(
  $$insert into public.messages (conversation_id, body)
    values ('00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000000a001', '插話')$$,
  '42501', null, '第三方不能在別人的對話裡發訊息');

-- 創作者 c1
select set_config('request.jwt.claims',
  '{"sub":"00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000c1","role":"authenticated"}', true);
select is((select count(*)::int from public.messages), 1, '對話參與者看得到訊息');
select lives_ok(
  $$insert into public.messages (conversation_id, body)
    values ('00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000000a001', '謝謝，我有興趣！')$$,
  '對話參與者可以發訊息');
select throws_ok(
  $$insert into public.messages (conversation_id, body, flagged_contact)
    values ('00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000000a001', 'x', false)$$,
  '42501', null, '使用者不能自己設定違規標記');
select lives_ok(
  $$select public.apply_to_brief('00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000000f001', '我想報名')$$,
  '創作者可以報名開放中的招募（計畫 5 起經由 apply_to_brief）');
select throws_ok(
  $$select public.apply_to_brief('00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000000f002', '我想報名')$$,
  '55000', 'brief closed', '創作者不能報名已關閉的招募');

-- 品牌 b1（招募主）與 b2
select set_config('request.jwt.claims',
  '{"sub":"00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000b1","role":"authenticated"}', true);
select is((select count(*)::int from public.brief_applications), 1, '招募主看得到報名');
select set_config('request.jwt.claims',
  '{"sub":"00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000b2","role":"authenticated"}', true);
select is((select count(*)::int from public.brief_applications), 0, '其他品牌看不到報名');

select * from finish();
rollback;
```

```bash
npx supabase test db
```
Expected: `11_briefs.test.sql` 失敗（`function public.today_tw(...) does not exist` 或 `column "cover_path" does not exist`）；`02` 失敗。其他檔案通過。

```sql
-- 招募與機會頁（計畫 5）：招募欄位調整、台灣日期、可見性、寫入函式、機會列表、報名者列表、索引
-- 權限一律在這裡明確寫出（雲端新專案不會自動授權，本機會；見 20261124000100_explicit_api_grants.sql）。

-- 0. 保護：cover_url 要改名成 cover_path（值的格式也不同）。目前沒有任何程式寫入招募，正式資料庫應該是 0 筆；
--    萬一已有資料就停下來，不要讓資料默默變成不合規格（要先手動搬移再推這個 migration）。
do $$
begin
  if exists (select 1 from public.briefs) then
    raise exception 'briefs has rows: migrate cover_url to cover_path by hand before applying 20261124000500_briefs.sql';
  end if;
end;
$$;

-- 1. 台灣今天的日期。p_now 只為了讓測試能驗證跨日邊界（UTC 15:59:59 / 16:00:00）；
--    所有 policy 與函式一律不帶參數呼叫（= now()），呼叫者無法影響截止日判斷。
create function public.today_tw(p_now timestamptz default now()) returns date
language sql stable set search_path = '' as $$
  select (p_now at time zone 'Asia/Taipei')::date;
$$;
revoke execute on function public.today_tw(timestamptz) from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
grant execute on function public.today_tw(timestamptz) to authenticated, service_role;

-- 2. briefs 欄位與限制
alter table public.briefs rename column cover_url to cover_path;
alter table public.briefs
  add column published_at timestamptz,
  alter column status set default 'draft',
  -- 草稿只檢查格式、不檢查必填，所以案名、說明、截止日在草稿可以是空的
  alter column title drop not null,
  alter column description drop not null,
  alter column deadline drop not null;

-- 文字清單的每一項都是 1..p_max_len 字（CHECK 不能用子查詢，所以包成 immutable 函式）
create function public.text_items_within(p text[], p_max_len integer) returns boolean
language sql immutable set search_path = '' as $$
  select coalesce((select bool_and(char_length(c) between 1 and p_max_len) from unnest(p) as c), true);
$$;
revoke execute on function public.text_items_within(text[], integer) from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;

alter table public.briefs
  add constraint briefs_title_len check (title is null or char_length(title) between 1 and 60),
  add constraint briefs_description_len check (description is null or char_length(description) between 1 and 2000),
  add constraint briefs_collab_types_list
    check (cardinality(collab_types) <= 6 and public.text_items_within(collab_types, 20)),
  add constraint briefs_categories_list
    check (cardinality(categories) <= 5 and public.text_items_within(categories, 30)),
  add constraint briefs_region_len check (region is null or char_length(region) between 1 and 30),
  -- 預算 0 由 save_brief 轉成 null（0 等於沒填），所以存進來的預算一定是 1..10,000,000
  add constraint briefs_budget_min_range check (budget_min_twd is null or budget_min_twd between 1 and 10000000),
  add constraint briefs_budget_max_range check (budget_max_twd is null or budget_max_twd between 1 and 10000000),
  add constraint briefs_cover_path_check check (
    cover_path is null
    or (
      cover_path ~ ('^' || brand_id::text || '/cover-[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._-]{0,99}$')
      and cover_path !~ '\.\.'
    )
  ),
  -- 草稿以外：必填齊全、至少一種合作形式、有預算或提供產品
  add constraint briefs_published_complete check (
    status = 'draft'
    or (
      title is not null and description is not null and deadline is not null
      and cardinality(collab_types) >= 1
      and (budget_min_twd is not null or budget_max_twd is not null or includes_product)
    )
  ),
  add constraint briefs_published_at_check check ((status = 'draft') = (published_at is null));

alter table public.brief_applications
  add constraint brief_applications_message_len check (message is null or char_length(message) between 1 and 500),
  add constraint brief_applications_price_max check (proposed_price_twd is null or proposed_price_twd <= 10000000);

-- 3. 索引（followups「4. 創作者廣場、5. 招募與機會頁」）
create index briefs_status_deadline_idx on public.briefs (status, deadline);
create index briefs_brand_created_idx on public.briefs (brand_id, created_at desc);
create index brief_applications_creator_created_idx on public.brief_applications (creator_id, created_at desc);
create index brief_applications_brief_status_idx on public.brief_applications (brief_id, status);

-- 4. 可見性：招募主、管理員、創作者看得到開放中且未截止的、報名過的人看得到自己報名的（任何狀態）。
--    品牌看不到其他品牌的招募。definer：讀 brief_applications 時不經過 RLS，避免兩張表的 policy 互相遞迴。
create function public.can_view_brief(p_id uuid, p_brand_id uuid, p_status text, p_deadline date)
returns boolean
language sql stable security definer set search_path = '' as $$
  select auth.uid() is not null and (
    p_brand_id = auth.uid()
    or public.is_admin()
    or (p_status = 'open' and p_deadline >= public.today_tw() and public.my_role() = 'creator')
    or exists (select 1 from public.brief_applications a where a.brief_id = p_id and a.creator_id = auth.uid())
  );
$$;
revoke execute on function public.can_view_brief(uuid, uuid, text, date) from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
grant execute on function public.can_view_brief(uuid, uuid, text, date) to authenticated;

drop policy briefs_select on public.briefs;
drop policy briefs_write_own on public.briefs;
create policy briefs_select on public.briefs
  for select to authenticated
  using (public.can_view_brief(id, brand_id, status, deadline));

-- 報名只能經由 apply_to_brief
drop policy brief_applications_insert_self on public.brief_applications;

-- 寫入一律經由下面的 definer 函式；anon 完全收回（20261124000100 誤給了 anon 寫入權限）
revoke all on public.briefs from anon, authenticated, service_role;
grant select on public.briefs to authenticated, service_role;
revoke all on public.brief_applications from anon, authenticated, service_role;
grant select on public.brief_applications to authenticated, service_role;

-- 5. 「符合你的條件」：四項各自的結果。is_match = 四項都成立。
--    粉絲數 ≥ 門檻；地區不限或相同；內容領域不限或有交集；合作形式有交集（創作者沒填想接的合作時不檢查）。
create type public.brief_match as (
  followers_ok boolean,
  region_ok boolean,
  category_ok boolean,
  collab_ok boolean
);

create function public.match_brief(p_brief public.briefs, p_creator public.creator_profiles)
returns public.brief_match
language sql immutable set search_path = '' as $$
  select row(
    coalesce(p_creator.follower_count >= p_brief.min_followers, false),
    coalesce(p_brief.region is null or p_brief.region = p_creator.region, false),
    coalesce(cardinality(p_brief.categories) = 0 or p_brief.categories && p_creator.categories, false),
    coalesce(cardinality(p_creator.collab_types) = 0 or p_brief.collab_types && p_creator.collab_types, false)
  )::public.brief_match;
$$;
revoke execute on function public.match_brief(public.briefs, public.creator_profiles)
  from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;

-- 6. 品牌：新增或修改招募。p_id 是 null 時新增。
--    草稿只檢查格式；p_publish = true 或原本就是發佈中的招募，檢查完整必填與截止日（今天到 90 天內）。
--    發佈中的招募不能改回草稿；已關閉或已截止的不能修改。預算 0 視為沒填（存成 null）。
create function public.save_brief(
  p_id uuid default null,
  p_title text default null,
  p_description text default null,
  p_collab_types text[] default null,
  p_categories text[] default null,
  p_min_followers integer default null,
  p_region text default null,
  p_budget_min_twd integer default null,
  p_budget_max_twd integer default null,
  p_includes_product boolean default false,
  p_deadline date default null,
  p_cover_path text default null,
  p_publish boolean default false
) returns uuid
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = '' as $$
declare
  v_uid uuid := auth.uid();
  v_today date := public.today_tw();
  v_title text := nullif(public.clean_text(p_title), '');
  v_desc text := nullif(public.clean_text(p_description), '');
  v_region text := nullif(public.clean_text(p_region), '');
  v_followers integer := coalesce(p_min_followers, 0);
  v_min integer := nullif(p_budget_min_twd, 0);
  v_max integer := nullif(p_budget_max_twd, 0);
  v_product boolean := coalesce(p_includes_product, false);
  v_types text[];
  v_cats text[];
  v_cover text;
  v_status text;
  v_old_deadline date;
  v_published timestamptz;
  v_open boolean;
  v_id uuid;
begin
  if v_uid is null then
    raise exception 'not authenticated' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if public.my_role() is distinct from 'brand'
     or not exists (select 1 from public.brand_profiles bp where bp.user_id = v_uid) then
    raise exception 'not a brand' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if p_id is not null then
    select b.status, b.deadline, b.published_at into v_status, v_old_deadline, v_published
      from public.briefs b where b.id = p_id and b.brand_id = v_uid for update;
    if not found then
      raise exception 'brief not found' using errcode = 'P0002';
    end if;
    if v_status = 'closed' or (v_status = 'open' and v_old_deadline < v_today) then
      raise exception 'brief not editable' using errcode = '55000';
    end if;
  end if;

  -- 格式（草稿也檢查）
  if v_title is not null and (public.has_bidi(v_title) or char_length(v_title) > 60) then
    raise exception 'invalid title' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if v_desc is not null and (public.has_bidi(v_desc) or char_length(v_desc) > 2000) then
    raise exception 'invalid description' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  v_types := public.check_text_list(p_collab_types, 'collab_types', 6, 20);
  v_cats := public.check_text_list(p_categories, 'categories', 5, 30);
  if v_region is not null and (public.has_bidi(v_region) or char_length(v_region) > 30) then
    raise exception 'invalid region' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if v_followers < 0 then
    raise exception 'invalid min_followers' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if v_min is not null and v_min not between 1 and 10000000 then
    raise exception 'invalid budget_min_twd' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if v_max is not null and (v_max not between 1 and 10000000 or v_max < coalesce(v_min, 0)) then
    raise exception 'invalid budget_max_twd' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  v_cover := public.check_media_path(v_uid, p_cover_path, 'cover', 'cover_path');

  -- 必填（發佈、或修改發佈中的招募）
  v_open := coalesce(p_publish, false) or v_status = 'open';
  if v_open then
    if v_title is null then
      raise exception 'invalid title' using errcode = '22023';
    end if;
    if v_desc is null then
      raise exception 'invalid description' using errcode = '22023';
    end if;
    if cardinality(v_types) = 0 then
      raise exception 'invalid collab_types' using errcode = '22023';
    end if;
    if v_min is null and v_max is null and not v_product then
      raise exception 'invalid reward' using errcode = '22023';
    end if;
    if p_deadline is null or p_deadline < v_today or p_deadline > v_today + 90 then
      raise exception 'invalid deadline' using errcode = '22023';
    end if;
  end if;

  if p_id is null then
    insert into public.briefs (brand_id, title, description, collab_types, categories, min_followers, region,
                               budget_min_twd, budget_max_twd, includes_product, deadline, cover_path,
                               status, published_at)
    values (v_uid, v_title, v_desc, v_types, v_cats, v_followers, v_region,
            v_min, v_max, v_product, p_deadline, v_cover,
            case when v_open then 'open' else 'draft' end, case when v_open then now() end)
    returning id into v_id;
  else
    update public.briefs b
       set title = v_title, description = v_desc, collab_types = v_types, categories = v_cats,
           min_followers = v_followers, region = v_region, budget_min_twd = v_min, budget_max_twd = v_max,
           includes_product = v_product, deadline = p_deadline, cover_path = v_cover,
           status = case when v_open then 'open' else 'draft' end,
           published_at = case when v_open then coalesce(v_published, now()) end
     where b.id = p_id
    returning b.id into v_id;
  end if;
  return v_id;
end;
$$;
revoke execute on function public.save_brief(uuid, text, text, text[], text[], integer, text, integer, integer, boolean, date, text, boolean)
  from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
grant execute on function public.save_brief(uuid, text, text, text[], text[], integer, text, integer, integer, boolean, date, text, boolean)
  to authenticated;

-- 7. 品牌：關閉（open → closed）。已截止但狀態仍是 open 的也可以關閉。
create function public.close_brief(p_id uuid) returns void
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = '' as $$
declare
  v_uid uuid := auth.uid();
begin
  if v_uid is null then
    raise exception 'not authenticated' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  update public.briefs b set status = 'closed'
   where b.id = p_id and b.brand_id = v_uid and b.status = 'open';
  if not found then
    if exists (select 1 from public.briefs b where b.id = p_id and b.brand_id = v_uid) then
      raise exception 'brief not open' using errcode = '55000';
    end if;
    raise exception 'brief not found' using errcode = 'P0002';
  end if;
end;
$$;
revoke execute on function public.close_brief(uuid) from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
grant execute on function public.close_brief(uuid) to authenticated;

-- 8. 品牌：刪除草稿。回傳封面路徑（沒有封面是 null），讓 App 盡力刪除 Storage 物件。
create function public.delete_brief(p_id uuid) returns text
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = '' as $$
declare
  v_uid uuid := auth.uid();
  v_cover text;
begin
  if v_uid is null then
    raise exception 'not authenticated' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  delete from public.briefs b
   where b.id = p_id and b.brand_id = v_uid and b.status = 'draft'
  returning b.cover_path into v_cover;
  if not found then
    if exists (select 1 from public.briefs b where b.id = p_id and b.brand_id = v_uid) then
      raise exception 'brief not draft' using errcode = '55000';
    end if;
    raise exception 'brief not found' using errcode = 'P0002';
  end if;
  return v_cover;
end;
$$;
revoke execute on function public.delete_brief(uuid) from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
grant execute on function public.delete_brief(uuid) to authenticated;

-- 9. 創作者：報名。必須已審核；招募必須是 open 且未截止（台灣時間，截止日當天可以）；
--    同一則只能報名一次（撤回後也不能再報名）；留言最多 500 字；報價 0..10,000,000。
create function public.apply_to_brief(p_brief_id uuid, p_message text default null, p_proposed_price integer default null)
returns uuid
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = '' as $$
declare
  v_uid uuid := auth.uid();
  v_creator_status text;
  v_status text;
  v_deadline date;
  v_msg text := nullif(public.clean_text(p_message), '');
  v_id uuid;
begin
  if v_uid is null then
    raise exception 'not authenticated' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  select cp.status into v_creator_status from public.creator_profiles cp where cp.user_id = v_uid;
  if not found or public.my_role() is distinct from 'creator' then
    raise exception 'not a creator' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if v_creator_status <> 'approved' then
    raise exception 'creator not approved' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  select b.status, b.deadline into v_status, v_deadline
    from public.briefs b where b.id = p_brief_id for share;
  if not found or v_status = 'draft' then
    raise exception 'brief not found' using errcode = 'P0002';
  end if;
  if v_status <> 'open' or v_deadline < public.today_tw() then
    raise exception 'brief closed' using errcode = '55000';
  end if;
  if v_msg is not null and (public.has_bidi(v_msg) or char_length(v_msg) > 500) then
    raise exception 'invalid message' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if p_proposed_price is not null and p_proposed_price not between 0 and 10000000 then
    raise exception 'invalid proposed_price_twd' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  begin
    insert into public.brief_applications (brief_id, creator_id, message, proposed_price_twd)
    values (p_brief_id, v_uid, v_msg, p_proposed_price)
    returning id into v_id;
  exception when unique_violation then
    raise exception 'already applied' using errcode = '23505';
  end;
  return v_id;
end;
$$;
revoke execute on function public.apply_to_brief(uuid, text, integer) from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
grant execute on function public.apply_to_brief(uuid, text, integer) to authenticated;

-- 10. 創作者：撤回自己待回覆的報名（applied → withdrawn）
create function public.withdraw_application(p_id uuid) returns void
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = '' as $$
declare
  v_uid uuid := auth.uid();
begin
  if v_uid is null then
    raise exception 'not authenticated' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  update public.brief_applications a set status = 'withdrawn'
   where a.id = p_id and a.creator_id = v_uid and a.status = 'applied';
  if not found then
    if exists (select 1 from public.brief_applications a where a.id = p_id and a.creator_id = v_uid) then
      raise exception 'application not pending' using errcode = '55000';
    end if;
    raise exception 'application not found' using errcode = 'P0002';
  end if;
end;
$$;
revoke execute on function public.withdraw_application(uuid) from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
grant execute on function public.withdraw_application(uuid) to authenticated;

-- 11. 招募主：錄取或婉拒待處理的報名（applied → accepted / rejected）。招募關閉或截止後也可以。
create function public.decide_application(p_id uuid, p_accept boolean) returns void
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = '' as $$
declare
  v_uid uuid := auth.uid();
begin
  if v_uid is null then
    raise exception 'not authenticated' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if p_accept is null then
    raise exception 'invalid accept' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  update public.brief_applications a
     set status = case when p_accept then 'accepted' else 'rejected' end
    from public.briefs b
   where a.id = p_id and b.id = a.brief_id and b.brand_id = v_uid and a.status = 'applied';
  if not found then
    if exists (select 1 from public.brief_applications a join public.briefs b on b.id = a.brief_id
                where a.id = p_id and b.brand_id = v_uid) then
      raise exception 'application not pending' using errcode = '55000';
    end if;
    raise exception 'application not found' using errcode = 'P0002';
  end if;
end;
$$;
revoke execute on function public.decide_application(uuid, boolean) from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
grant execute on function public.decide_application(uuid, boolean) to authenticated;

-- 12. 報名人數：只算 applied 與 accepted；只回傳呼叫者看得到的招募。
--     pending_count（待處理 = applied）只有招募主看得到，其他人是 null。
create function public.brief_application_counts(p_ids uuid[])
returns table (brief_id uuid, applicant_count bigint, pending_count bigint)
language sql stable security definer set search_path = '' as $$
  select b.id,
         count(a.id) filter (where a.status in ('applied', 'accepted')),
         case when b.brand_id = auth.uid() then count(a.id) filter (where a.status = 'applied') end
    from public.briefs b
    left join public.brief_applications a on a.brief_id = b.id
   where b.id = any (coalesce(p_ids, '{}'::uuid[]))
     and public.can_view_brief(b.id, b.brand_id, b.status, b.deadline)
   group by b.id, b.brand_id;
$$;
revoke execute on function public.brief_application_counts(uuid[]) from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
grant execute on function public.brief_application_counts(uuid[]) to authenticated;

-- 13. 機會列表：只給創作者（含未審核）。只列 open 且未截止的；依截止日由近到遠。
--     p_filter：match（預設，清單以外的值也當 match）、all、category（p_category 必須在招募的內容領域裡，
--     「不限」的招募不算）、cash（有預算）、product（提供產品）。p_limit 夾在 1..40。
create function public.list_opportunities(
  p_filter text default 'match',
  p_category text default null,
  p_limit integer default 20,
  p_offset integer default 0
)
returns table (
  id uuid,
  title text,
  collab_types text[],
  categories text[],
  min_followers integer,
  region text,
  budget_min_twd integer,
  budget_max_twd integer,
  includes_product boolean,
  deadline date,
  cover_path text,
  brand_id uuid,
  brand_name text,
  brand_logo_path text,
  applicant_count bigint,
  followers_ok boolean,
  region_ok boolean,
  category_ok boolean,
  collab_ok boolean,
  is_match boolean,
  has_applied boolean,
  my_status text,
  total_count bigint
)
language plpgsql stable security definer set search_path = '' as $$
#variable_conflict use_column
declare
  v_uid uuid := auth.uid();
  v_cp public.creator_profiles;
  v_filter text := case when p_filter in ('match', 'all', 'category', 'cash', 'product') then p_filter else 'match' end;
  v_cat text := nullif(left(public.clean_text(p_category), 30), '');
  v_limit integer := least(greatest(coalesce(p_limit, 20), 1), 40);
  v_offset integer := greatest(coalesce(p_offset, 0), 0);
  v_today date := public.today_tw();
begin
  select * into v_cp from public.creator_profiles cp where cp.user_id = v_uid;
  if v_uid is null or not found or public.my_role() is distinct from 'creator' then
    raise exception 'not a creator' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;

  return query
  with base as (
    select b.id, b.title, b.collab_types, b.categories, b.min_followers, b.region, b.budget_min_twd,
           b.budget_max_twd, b.includes_product, b.deadline, b.cover_path, b.brand_id, b.created_at,
           bp.brand_name, bp.logo_path as brand_logo_path,
           m.followers_ok, m.region_ok, m.category_ok, m.collab_ok,
           (m.followers_ok and m.region_ok and m.category_ok and m.collab_ok) as is_match,
           my.status as my_status
      from public.briefs b
      join public.brand_profiles bp on bp.user_id = b.brand_id
      cross join lateral public.match_brief(b, v_cp) as m
      left join public.brief_applications my on my.brief_id = b.id and my.creator_id = v_uid
     where b.status = 'open' and b.deadline >= v_today
  ),
  filtered as (
    select x.* from base x
     where case v_filter
             when 'match' then x.is_match
             when 'category' then v_cat is not null and x.categories @> array[v_cat]
             when 'cash' then coalesce(x.budget_min_twd, 0) > 0 or coalesce(x.budget_max_twd, 0) > 0
             when 'product' then x.includes_product
             else true
           end
  ),
  paged as (
    select f.*, count(*) over () as total_count
      from filtered f
     order by f.deadline, f.created_at desc, f.id
     limit v_limit offset v_offset
  )
  select p.id, p.title, p.collab_types, p.categories, p.min_followers, p.region, p.budget_min_twd,
         p.budget_max_twd, p.includes_product, p.deadline, p.cover_path, p.brand_id, p.brand_name,
         p.brand_logo_path,
         (select count(*) from public.brief_applications a
           where a.brief_id = p.id and a.status in ('applied', 'accepted')),
         p.followers_ok, p.region_ok, p.category_ok, p.collab_ok, p.is_match,
         p.my_status is not null, p.my_status, p.total_count
    from paged p
   order by p.deadline, p.created_at desc, p.id;
end;
$$;
revoke execute on function public.list_opportunities(text, text, integer, integer)
  from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
grant execute on function public.list_opportunities(text, text, integer, integer) to authenticated;

-- 14. 機會詳情：只給創作者。看得到的條件與 briefs_select 相同（開放中且未截止、或自己報名過）；
--     草稿與看不到的招募不回傳任何列（頁面顯示 404）。另外帶出自己的報名。
create function public.get_opportunity(p_id uuid)
returns table (
  id uuid,
  title text,
  description text,
  status text,
  collab_types text[],
  categories text[],
  min_followers integer,
  region text,
  budget_min_twd integer,
  budget_max_twd integer,
  includes_product boolean,
  deadline date,
  cover_path text,
  brand_id uuid,
  brand_name text,
  brand_logo_path text,
  applicant_count bigint,
  followers_ok boolean,
  region_ok boolean,
  category_ok boolean,
  collab_ok boolean,
  is_match boolean,
  my_application_id uuid,
  my_status text,
  my_message text,
  my_proposed_price_twd integer
)
language plpgsql stable security definer set search_path = '' as $$
#variable_conflict use_column
declare
  v_uid uuid := auth.uid();
  v_cp public.creator_profiles;
begin
  select * into v_cp from public.creator_profiles cp where cp.user_id = v_uid;
  if v_uid is null or not found or public.my_role() is distinct from 'creator' then
    raise exception 'not a creator' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;

  return query
  select b.id, b.title, b.description, b.status, b.collab_types, b.categories, b.min_followers, b.region,
         b.budget_min_twd, b.budget_max_twd, b.includes_product, b.deadline, b.cover_path, b.brand_id,
         bp.brand_name, bp.logo_path,
         (select count(*) from public.brief_applications a
           where a.brief_id = b.id and a.status in ('applied', 'accepted')),
         m.followers_ok, m.region_ok, m.category_ok, m.collab_ok,
         (m.followers_ok and m.region_ok and m.category_ok and m.collab_ok),
         my.id, my.status, my.message, my.proposed_price_twd
    from public.briefs b
    join public.brand_profiles bp on bp.user_id = b.brand_id
    cross join lateral public.match_brief(b, v_cp) as m
    left join public.brief_applications my on my.brief_id = b.id and my.creator_id = v_uid
   where b.id = p_id
     and b.status <> 'draft'
     and ((b.status = 'open' and b.deadline >= public.today_tw()) or my.id is not null);
end;
$$;
revoke execute on function public.get_opportunity(uuid) from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
grant execute on function public.get_opportunity(uuid) to authenticated;

-- 15. 報名者列表：只給招募主。創作者的公開資料（名稱、頭像、粉絲、互動率）品牌不一定讀得到
--     （未審核或停權的不公開），所以用 definer 函式照實帶出；報名本來就只有已審核的創作者。
create function public.list_brief_applicants(p_brief_id uuid)
returns table (
  id uuid,
  creator_id uuid,
  handle text,
  display_name text,
  avatar_path text,
  follower_count integer,
  engagement_rate numeric,
  region text,
  message text,
  proposed_price_twd integer,
  status text,
  created_at timestamptz
)
language plpgsql stable security definer set search_path = '' as $$
#variable_conflict use_column
begin
  if auth.uid() is null
     or not exists (select 1 from public.briefs b where b.id = p_brief_id and b.brand_id = auth.uid()) then
    raise exception 'not brief owner' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  return query
  select a.id, a.creator_id, cp.handle, p.display_name, cp.avatar_path, cp.follower_count,
         cp.engagement_rate::numeric, cp.region, a.message, a.proposed_price_twd, a.status, a.created_at
    from public.brief_applications a
    join public.creator_profiles cp on cp.user_id = a.creator_id
    join public.profiles p on p.id = a.creator_id
   where a.brief_id = p_brief_id
   order by a.created_at desc, a.id;
end;
$$;
revoke execute on function public.list_brief_applicants(uuid) from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
grant execute on function public.list_brief_applicants(uuid) to authenticated;
```

### Task 2: 封面 bucket `brief-covers` 與上傳路徑

**Files:**
- Create: `supabase/migrations/20261124000600_brief_covers.sql`、`supabase/tests/12_brief_covers.test.sql`、`src/lib/brief-cover.ts`、`src/lib/brief-cover.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**

set local role authenticated;

select set_config('request.jwt.claims', '{"sub":"00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000000006c1","role":"authenticated"}', true);
select lives_ok(

  0, '自己的封面已被刪除');

select * from finish();
rollback;

- [ ] **Step 2: 寫失敗的單元測試 `src/lib/brief-cover.test.ts`**

```ts
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { BRIEF_COVER_MAX_BYTES, briefCoverUrl, planCoverUpload } from "./brief-cover";

afterEach(() => vi.unstubAllEnvs());

describe("planCoverUpload", () => {
  it("產生 <uid>/cover-<id>.<副檔名>", () => {
    expect(planCoverUpload("u1", { type: "image/png", size: 10 }, "abc")).toEqual({ path: "u1/cover-abc.png" });
    expect(planCoverUpload("u1", { type: "image/jpeg", size: 10 }, "abc")).toEqual({ path: "u1/cover-abc.jpg" });
    expect(planCoverUpload("u1", { type: "image/webp", size: 10 }, "abc")).toEqual({ path: "u1/cover-abc.webp" });
  });
  it("只接受 JPG、PNG、WebP（原型鏈上的名稱也不行）", () => {
    expect(planCoverUpload("u1", { type: "image/gif", size: 10 }, "abc")).toEqual({ error: "只接受 JPG、PNG、WebP" });
    expect(planCoverUpload("u1", { type: "constructor", size: 10 }, "abc")).toEqual({ error: "只接受 JPG、PNG、WebP" });
  });
  it("檔案上限 5MB", () => {
    expect(planCoverUpload("u1", { type: "image/png", size: BRIEF_COVER_MAX_BYTES }, "abc")).toEqual({ path: "u1/cover-abc.png" });
    expect(planCoverUpload("u1", { type: "image/png", size: BRIEF_COVER_MAX_BYTES + 1 }, "abc")).toEqual({ error: "檔案不能超過 5MB" });
  });
});

describe("briefCoverUrl", () => {
  it("組出 brief-covers 的公開網址", () => {
    vi.stubEnv("NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL", "http://127.0.0.1:54321");
    expect(briefCoverUrl("u1/cover-a.png")).toBe("http://127.0.0.1:54321/storage/v1/object/public/brief-covers/u1/cover-a.png");
    expect(briefCoverUrl(null)).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 寫 migration `supabase/migrations/20261124000600_brief_covers.sql`**

```sql
-- 招募封面圖（計畫 5）
-- bucket 是公開的：草稿的封面網址若被猜到也看得到；路徑含 UUID，與 creator-media 相同的取捨（見 README）。
insert into storage.buckets (id, name, public, file_size_limit, allowed_mime_types)
values ('brief-covers', 'brief-covers', true, 5242880, array['image/jpeg', 'image/png', 'image/webp'])
on conflict (id) do update set
  public = excluded.public,
  file_size_limit = excluded.file_size_limit,
  allowed_mime_types = excluded.allowed_mime_types;

-- 路徑規則與 briefs.cover_path 一致：<uid>/cover-<檔名>；不可巢狀、不可含 ..
-- 只有品牌可以寫入（my_role() = 'brand'）；創作者與還沒建立帳號的使用者都不行
create policy brief_covers_select_own on storage.objects
  for select to authenticated
  using (bucket_id = 'brief-covers' and (storage.foldername(name))[1] = auth.uid()::text);
create policy brief_covers_insert_own on storage.objects
  for insert to authenticated
  with check (
    bucket_id = 'brief-covers'
    and (storage.foldername(name))[1] = auth.uid()::text
    and public.my_role() = 'brand'
    and name ~ ('^' || auth.uid()::text || '/cover-[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._-]{0,99}$')
    and name !~ '\.\.'
  );
create policy brief_covers_update_own on storage.objects
  for update to authenticated
  using (bucket_id = 'brief-covers' and (storage.foldername(name))[1] = auth.uid()::text)
  with check (
    bucket_id = 'brief-covers'
    and (storage.foldername(name))[1] = auth.uid()::text
    and public.my_role() = 'brand'
    and name ~ ('^' || auth.uid()::text || '/cover-[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._-]{0,99}$')
    and name !~ '\.\.'
  );
create policy brief_covers_delete_own on storage.objects
  for delete to authenticated
  using (
    bucket_id = 'brief-covers'
    and (storage.foldername(name))[1] = auth.uid()::text
    and public.my_role() = 'brand'
  );
```

- [ ] **Step 5: 寫 `src/lib/brief-cover.ts`**

```ts
import { publicObjectUrl } from "./storage-url";

export const BRIEF_COVER_BUCKET = "brief-covers";
export const BRIEF_COVER_MAX_BYTES = 5 * 1024 * 1024;
const EXT_BY_TYPE: Record<string, string> = { "image/jpeg": "jpg", "image/png": "png", "image/webp": "webp" };

export function briefCoverUrl(path: string | null): string | null {
  return publicObjectUrl(BRIEF_COVER_BUCKET, path);
}

/**
 * 上傳前檢查檔案並產生路徑 <userId>/cover-<id>.<副檔名>。
 * 規則與 storage policy（20261124000600_brief_covers.sql）、briefs.cover_path 的 CHECK 一致（id 用 crypto.randomUUID()）。
 */
export function planCoverUpload(
  userId: string, file: { type: string; size: number }, id: string,
): { path: string } | { error: string } {
  if (!Object.hasOwn(EXT_BY_TYPE, file.type)) return { error: "只接受 JPG、PNG、WebP" };
  if (file.size > BRIEF_COVER_MAX_BYTES) return { error: "檔案不能超過 5MB" };
  return { path: `${userId}/cover-${id}.${EXT_BY_TYPE[file.type]}` };
}
```

describe("cleanTextWithin", () => {
  it("與 cleanText 相同的清理，但截斷長度可以指定", () => {
    expect(cleanTextWithin("  小美​ ", 10)).toBe("小美");
    const long = "字".repeat(1500);
    expect(cleanText(long)).toHaveLength(MAX_RAW_LENGTH);
    expect(cleanTextWithin(long, 8000)).toBe(long);
    expect(cleanTextWithin("x".repeat(9000), 8000)).toHaveLength(8000);
  });
  it("cleanText 可以直接當 map 的 callback（不會把索引當成截斷長度）", () => {

  });
});

`src/lib/briefs/dates.test.ts`：

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  addDays, daysBetween, deadlineLabel, formatDate, formatDateTimeTw, formatDateTw, isIsoDate, todayTw,
} from "./dates";

describe("todayTw", () => {
  it("台灣時間跨日的邊界與 SQL today_tw() 相同（UTC 16:00 是台灣隔天 00:00）", () => {
    expect(todayTw(new Date("2026-11-24T15:59:59Z"))).toBe("2026-11-24");
    expect(todayTw(new Date("2026-11-24T16:00:00Z"))).toBe("2026-11-25");
  });
  it("跨年也正確", () => {
    expect(todayTw(new Date("2026-12-31T16:00:00Z"))).toBe("2027-01-01");
  });
});

describe("isIsoDate", () => {
  it("只接受存在的 YYYY-MM-DD", () => {
    expect(isIsoDate("2026-11-24")).toBe(true);
    expect(isIsoDate("2028-02-29")).toBe(true);
    expect(isIsoDate("2026-02-29")).toBe(false);
    expect(isIsoDate("2026-13-01")).toBe(false);
    expect(isIsoDate("2026-1-1")).toBe(false);
    expect(isIsoDate("")).toBe(false);
    expect(isIsoDate("2026-11-24T00:00")).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe("addDays / daysBetween", () => {
  it("跨月、跨年", () => {
    expect(addDays("2026-11-24", 90)).toBe("2027-02-22");
    expect(addDays("2026-12-31", 1)).toBe("2027-01-01");
    expect(addDays("2026-11-24", -1)).toBe("2026-11-23");
    expect(daysBetween("2026-11-24", "2027-02-22")).toBe(90);
    expect(daysBetween("2026-11-24", "2026-11-23")).toBe(-1);
  });
});

describe("deadlineLabel", () => {
  it("今天截止、剩 N 天、已截止", () => {
    expect(deadlineLabel("2026-11-24", "2026-11-24")).toBe("今天截止");
    expect(deadlineLabel("2026-11-27", "2026-11-24")).toBe("剩 3 天");
    expect(deadlineLabel("2026-11-23", "2026-11-24")).toBe("已截止");
  });
});

describe("格式化", () => {
  it("日期與台灣時間", () => {
    expect(formatDate("2026-11-24")).toBe("2026/11/24");
    expect(formatDateTw("2026-11-24T16:30:00Z")).toBe("2026/11/25");
    expect(formatDateTimeTw("2026-11-24T06:05:00Z")).toBe("2026/11/24 14:05");
    expect(formatDateTimeTw("2026-11-24T16:00:00Z")).toBe("2026/11/25 00:00");
  });
});
```

`src/lib/validation/brief.test.ts`：

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  EMPTY_BRIEF_INPUT, firstErrorStep, hasBadChar, parseApplicationForm, parseBriefForm, toSaveBriefArgs,
  validateApplication, validateBrief, validateBriefStep, type BriefInput,
} from "./brief";

const TODAY = "2026-11-24";
const UID = "00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000601";
const ok: BriefInput = {
  title: "春季保養開箱", description: "說明", collabTypes: ["IG 貼文"], coverPath: `${UID}/cover-a.jpg`,
  categories: ["美妝"], minFollowers: "1000", region: "台北市", budgetMin: "5000", budgetMax: "20000",
  includesProduct: false, deadline: "2026-12-01",
};

function fd(entries: [string, string][]): FormData {
  const f = new FormData();
  for (const [k, v] of entries) f.append(k, v);
  return f;
}

describe("hasBadChar", () => {
  it("雙向控制字元與 NUL 都不允許", () => {
    expect(hasBadChar("a‮b")).toBe(true);
    expect(hasBadChar("a\u0000b")).toBe(true);
    expect(hasBadChar("正常")).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe("parseBriefForm", () => {
  it("清理文字、數字去掉千分位、勾選框、空封面是 null", () => {
    const i = parseBriefForm(fd([
      ["title", "  春季​保養 "], ["description", "第一行\r\n第二行"], ["collabTypes", "IG 貼文"],
      ["collabTypes", " Reels "], ["coverPath", ""], ["categories", "美妝"], ["minFollowers", "1,000"],
      ["region", "台北市"], ["budgetMin", "5,000"], ["budgetMax", " 20，000 "], ["includesProduct", "on"],
      ["deadline", "2026-12-01"],
    ]));
    expect(i).toEqual({
      title: "春季保養", description: "第一行\n第二行", collabTypes: ["IG 貼文", "Reels"], coverPath: null,
      categories: ["美妝"], minFollowers: "1000", region: "台北市", budgetMin: "5000", budgetMax: "20000",
      includesProduct: true, deadline: "2026-12-01",
    });
  });
  it("缺欄位與非字串值當成空的", () => {
    const f = new FormData();
    f.append("title", new Blob(["x"]));
    expect(parseBriefForm(f)).toEqual(EMPTY_BRIEF_INPUT);
  });
  it("2000 字的說明不會被截斷", () => {
    const long = "字".repeat(2000);
    expect(parseBriefForm(fd([["description", long]])).description).toBe(long);
  });
});

describe("validateBrief", () => {
  it("完整資料可以發佈", () => expect(validateBrief(ok, "publish", TODAY)).toBeNull());
  it("草稿可以缺必填", () => expect(validateBrief(EMPTY_BRIEF_INPUT, "draft", TODAY)).toBeNull());
  it("發佈時檢查必填", () => {
    expect(validateBrief(EMPTY_BRIEF_INPUT, "publish", TODAY)).toEqual({
      title: "請填寫案名（60 字以內）", description: "請填寫合作說明", collabTypes: "請至少選一種合作形式",
      reward: "預算和產品至少要有一項", deadline: "請選擇截止日",
    });
  });
  it("草稿也檢查格式", () => {
    expect(validateBrief({ ...EMPTY_BRIEF_INPUT, title: "字".repeat(61) }, "draft", TODAY)).toEqual({ title: "案名最多 60 字" });
    expect(validateBrief({ ...EMPTY_BRIEF_INPUT, description: "字".repeat(2001) }, "draft", TODAY)).toEqual({ description: "說明最多 2000 字" });
    expect(validateBrief({ ...EMPTY_BRIEF_INPUT, title: "a\u0000" }, "draft", TODAY)).toEqual({ title: "含有不允許的特殊字元" });
    expect(validateBrief({ ...EMPTY_BRIEF_INPUT, deadline: "2026-02-30" }, "draft", TODAY)).toEqual({ deadline: "日期格式不正確" });
  });
  it("案名 60 字邊界（以 code point 計算）", () => {
    expect(validateBrief({ ...ok, title: "😀".repeat(60) }, "publish", TODAY)).toBeNull();
  });
  it("合作形式與內容領域的數量、長度、重複", () => {
    expect(validateBrief({ ...ok, collabTypes: ["a", "a"] }, "publish", TODAY)).toEqual({ collabTypes: "請重新選擇合作形式" });
    expect(validateBrief({ ...ok, collabTypes: ["字".repeat(21)] }, "publish", TODAY)).toEqual({ collabTypes: "請重新選擇合作形式" });
    expect(validateBrief({ ...ok, categories: ["a", "b", "c", "d", "e", "f"] }, "publish", TODAY)).toEqual({ categories: "最多選 5 個內容領域" });
    expect(validateBrief({ ...ok, categories: [] }, "publish", TODAY)).toBeNull();
  });
  it("清單以外的值只要格式正確就接受（白名單只在 UI 端）", () => {
    expect(validateBrief({ ...ok, collabTypes: ["開箱影片"], categories: ["園藝"], region: "火星" }, "publish", TODAY)).toBeNull();
  });
  it("封面路徑格式", () => {
    expect(validateBrief({ ...ok, coverPath: "x/cover-a.jpg" }, "publish", TODAY)).toEqual({ coverPath: "封面圖片有問題，請重新上傳" });
    expect(validateBrief({ ...ok, coverPath: `${UID}/cover-..jpg` }, "publish", TODAY)).toEqual({ coverPath: "封面圖片有問題，請重新上傳" });
    expect(validateBrief({ ...ok, coverPath: null }, "publish", TODAY)).toBeNull();
  });
  it("粉絲門檻", () => {
    expect(validateBrief({ ...ok, minFollowers: "" }, "publish", TODAY)).toBeNull();
    expect(validateBrief({ ...ok, minFollowers: "-1" }, "publish", TODAY)).toEqual({ minFollowers: "請填 0 以上的整數" });
    expect(validateBrief({ ...ok, minFollowers: "1.5" }, "publish", TODAY)).toEqual({ minFollowers: "請填 0 以上的整數" });
  });
  it("預算：範圍、上限不低於下限、只填一個可以", () => {
    expect(validateBrief({ ...ok, budgetMin: "10000001" }, "publish", TODAY)).toEqual({ budgetMin: "請填 0 到 10,000,000 的整數" });
    expect(validateBrief({ ...ok, budgetMin: "5000", budgetMax: "4000" }, "publish", TODAY)).toEqual({ budgetMax: "上限不能低於下限" });
    expect(validateBrief({ ...ok, budgetMin: "", budgetMax: "4000" }, "publish", TODAY)).toBeNull();
    expect(validateBrief({ ...ok, budgetMin: "5000", budgetMax: "" }, "publish", TODAY)).toBeNull();
    expect(validateBrief({ ...ok, budgetMin: "5000", budgetMax: "0" }, "publish", TODAY)).toBeNull();
  });
  it("預算和產品至少一項；預算 0 等於沒填", () => {
    expect(validateBrief({ ...ok, budgetMin: "0", budgetMax: "" }, "publish", TODAY)).toEqual({ reward: "預算和產品至少要有一項" });
    expect(validateBrief({ ...ok, budgetMin: "", budgetMax: "", includesProduct: true }, "publish", TODAY)).toBeNull();
  });
  it("截止日：今天到 90 天內（含兩端）", () => {
    expect(validateBrief({ ...ok, deadline: TODAY }, "publish", TODAY)).toBeNull();
    expect(validateBrief({ ...ok, deadline: "2027-02-22" }, "publish", TODAY)).toBeNull();
    expect(validateBrief({ ...ok, deadline: "2026-11-23" }, "publish", TODAY)).toEqual({ deadline: "截止日要在今天到 90 天內" });
    expect(validateBrief({ ...ok, deadline: "2027-02-23" }, "publish", TODAY)).toEqual({ deadline: "截止日要在今天到 90 天內" });
    expect(validateBrief({ ...ok, deadline: "2026-11-23" }, "draft", TODAY)).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe("validateBriefStep / firstErrorStep", () => {
  it("下一步只檢查這一步的欄位", () => {
    expect(validateBriefStep(1, { ...EMPTY_BRIEF_INPUT, title: "x", description: "y", collabTypes: ["IG 貼文"] }, TODAY)).toBeNull();
    expect(validateBriefStep(2, EMPTY_BRIEF_INPUT, TODAY)).toBeNull();
    expect(validateBriefStep(3, EMPTY_BRIEF_INPUT, TODAY)).toEqual({ reward: "預算和產品至少要有一項", deadline: "請選擇截止日" });
    expect(Object.keys(validateBriefStep(4, EMPTY_BRIEF_INPUT, TODAY) ?? {})).toContain("title");
  });
  it("跳到有錯誤的第一步", () => {
    expect(firstErrorStep({ deadline: "x", title: "y" })).toBe(1);
    expect(firstErrorStep({ region: "x" })).toBe(2);
    expect(firstErrorStep({ reward: "x" })).toBe(3);
    expect(firstErrorStep({ formError: "x" })).toBe(4);
  });
});

describe("toSaveBriefArgs", () => {
  it("轉成 save_brief 的參數（沒填的省略）", () => {
    expect(toSaveBriefArgs(null, { ...EMPTY_BRIEF_INPUT, title: "x" }, false)).toEqual({
      p_id: undefined, p_title: "x", p_description: "", p_collab_types: [], p_categories: [], p_min_followers: 0,
      p_region: "", p_budget_min_twd: undefined, p_budget_max_twd: undefined, p_includes_product: false,
      p_deadline: undefined, p_cover_path: undefined, p_publish: false,
    });
    expect(toSaveBriefArgs("id-1", ok, true)).toMatchObject({
      p_id: "id-1", p_min_followers: 1000, p_budget_min_twd: 5000, p_budget_max_twd: 20000,
      p_deadline: "2026-12-01", p_cover_path: `${UID}/cover-a.jpg`, p_publish: true,
    });
  });
});

describe("報名", () => {
  it("parseApplicationForm 清理留言與報價", () => {
    expect(parseApplicationForm(fd([["briefId", " b1 "], ["message", " 你好\r\n "], ["proposedPrice", "8,000"]])))
      .toEqual({ briefId: "b1", message: "你好", proposedPrice: "8000" });
  });
  it("validateApplication", () => {
    expect(validateApplication({ briefId: "b", message: "", proposedPrice: "" })).toBeNull();
    expect(validateApplication({ briefId: "b", message: "字".repeat(500), proposedPrice: "0" })).toBeNull();
    expect(validateApplication({ briefId: "b", message: "字".repeat(501), proposedPrice: "" })).toEqual({ message: "最多 500 字" });
    expect(validateApplication({ briefId: "b", message: "a‮", proposedPrice: "" })).toEqual({ message: "含有不允許的特殊字元" });
    expect(validateApplication({ briefId: "b", message: "", proposedPrice: "10000001" })).toEqual({ proposedPrice: "請填 0 到 10,000,000 的整數" });
  });
});
```
