```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { EMPTY_BRIEF_INPUT } from "@/lib/validation/brief";
import {
  appendUniqueById, audienceLabel, brandTabOf, briefToInput, displayStatusOf, filterApplicants, matchItems,
  previewFromInput, proposedPriceLabel, rewardLabel, toApplicant, toApplicationStatus, toBrief, toBriefStatus,
  toMyApplications, toOpportunityDetail, toOpportunityPage, withCounts,
  type ApplicantRow, type BriefRow, type OpportunityDetailRow, type OpportunityRow,
} from "./view";

const briefRow: BriefRow = {
  id: "b1", brand_id: "u1", title: null, description: null, collab_types: null, categories: null, min_followers: null,
  region: null, budget_min_twd: null, budget_max_twd: null, includes_product: null, deadline: null, cover_path: null,
  status: "draft", published_at: null, created_at: "2026-11-24T00:00:00Z",
};
const oppRow: OpportunityRow = {
  id: "o1", title: "春季", collab_types: ["IG 貼文"], categories: ["美妝"], min_followers: 1000, region: "台北市",
  budget_min_twd: 5000, budget_max_twd: null, includes_product: false, deadline: "2026-12-01", cover_path: null,
  brand_name: "品牌甲", brand_logo_path: null, applicant_count: "3", followers_ok: true, region_ok: null,
  category_ok: true, collab_ok: true, is_match: null, my_status: null, total_count: "41",
};

describe("狀態", () => {
  it("不認得的狀態取最保守的值", () => {
    expect(toBriefStatus("open")).toBe("open");
    expect(toBriefStatus("weird")).toBe("closed");
    expect(toApplicationStatus("accepted")).toBe("accepted");
    expect(toApplicationStatus(null)).toBe("withdrawn");
  });
  it("open 但截止日已過顯示已截止；截止日當天仍是發佈中", () => {
    expect(displayStatusOf("open", "2026-11-23", "2026-11-24")).toBe("expired");
    expect(displayStatusOf("open", "2026-11-24", "2026-11-24")).toBe("open");
    expect(displayStatusOf("draft", "2026-01-01", "2026-11-24")).toBe("draft");
    expect(displayStatusOf("closed", "2027-01-01", "2026-11-24")).toBe("closed");
  });
  it("品牌列表的分頁", () => {
    expect(brandTabOf("open")).toBe("open");
    expect(brandTabOf("draft")).toBe("draft");
    expect(brandTabOf("expired")).toBe("ended");
    expect(brandTabOf("closed")).toBe("ended");
  });
});

describe("rewardLabel", () => {
  const r = (budgetMin: number | null, budgetMax: number | null, includesProduct = false) => rewardLabel({ budgetMin, budgetMax, includesProduct });
  it("spec 的五種寫法", () => {
    expect(r(5000, 20000)).toBe("NT$5,000–20,000");
    expect(r(5000, null)).toBe("NT$5,000 起");
    expect(r(null, 20000)).toBe("最高 NT$20,000");
    expect(r(3000, null, true)).toBe("產品互惠 + NT$3,000 起");
    expect(r(null, null, true)).toBe("產品互惠");
  });
  it("上下限相同、0 當成沒填、都沒有", () => {
    expect(r(5000, 5000)).toBe("NT$5,000");
    expect(r(0, 20000)).toBe("最高 NT$20,000");
    expect(r(null, null)).toBe("—");
  });
});

  it("audienceLabel", () => {
    expect(audienceLabel(0)).toBe("粉絲數不限");
    expect(audienceLabel(12000)).toBe("粉絲 12,000 以上");

  it("matchItems 四項依序", () => {

      minFollowers: 0, region: null, categories: [], collabTypes: ["IG 貼文", "Reels"],
      match: { followers: true, region: true, category: false, collab: true },

});

});

describe("機會", () => {
  it("toOpportunityPage：null 當 false、字串數字轉成數字、hasMore", () => {
    const page = toOpportunityPage([oppRow], 20);
    expect(page.total).toBe(41);
    expect(page.hasMore).toBe(true);
    expect(page.items[0]).toMatchObject({
      applicantCount: 3, isMatch: false, match: { followers: true, region: false, category: true, collab: true }, myStatus: null,
    });
    expect(toOpportunityPage([oppRow], 40).hasMore).toBe(false);
    expect(toOpportunityPage([], 0)).toEqual({ items: [], total: 0, hasMore: false });
  });
  it("appendUniqueById", () => {
    expect(appendUniqueById([{ id: "a" }], [{ id: "a" }, { id: "b" }, { id: "b" }])).toEqual([{ id: "a" }, { id: "b" }]);
  });
  it("toOpportunityDetail 帶出自己的報名", () => {
    const row: OpportunityDetailRow = {
      ...oppRow, description: null, status: "open", my_application_id: "a1", my_status: "applied",
      my_message: null, my_proposed_price_twd: 8000,
    };
    expect(toOpportunityDetail(row)).toMatchObject({
      description: "", status: "open", myStatus: "applied",
      application: { id: "a1", status: "applied", message: null, proposedPrice: 8000 },
    });
    expect(toOpportunityDetail({ ...row, my_application_id: null, my_status: null }).application).toBeNull();
  });
  it("toMyApplications 略過讀不到招募的", () => {
    expect(toMyApplications([
      { id: "a1", status: "accepted", created_at: "t", briefs: {
        id: "b1", title: "x", deadline: "2026-12-01", status: "closed", budget_min_twd: null, budget_max_twd: 3000,
        includes_product: null, brand_profiles: { brand_name: "品牌甲" },
      } },
      { id: "a2", status: "applied", created_at: "t", briefs: null },
    ])).toEqual([{
      id: "a1", status: "accepted", createdAt: "t",
      brief: { id: "b1", title: "x", deadline: "2026-12-01", status: "closed", brandName: "品牌甲", budgetMin: null, budgetMax: 3000, includesProduct: false },
    }]);
  });
});

describe("報名者", () => {
  const row: ApplicantRow = {
    id: "a1", creator_id: "c1", handle: "mei", display_name: "小美", avatar_path: null, follower_count: null,
    engagement_rate: "3.50", region: null, message: null, proposed_price_twd: null, status: "rejected", created_at: "t",
  };
  it("toApplicant 與篩選", () => {
    const a = toApplicant(row);
    expect(a).toMatchObject({ followerCount: 0, engagementRate: 3.5, status: "rejected" });
    expect(filterApplicants([a], "all")).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(filterApplicants([a], "applied")).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(filterApplicants([a], "rejected")).toHaveLength(1);
  });
  it("沒有提出報價顯示「依招募預算」", () => {
    expect(proposedPriceLabel(null)).toBe("依招募預算");
    expect(proposedPriceLabel(0)).toBe("NT$0");
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  OPPORTUNITY_FILTERS, isUuid, opportunityHref, opportunityQueryString, parseBrandBriefTab, parseBriefStep,
  parseOpportunityQuery, sameOpportunityQuery, toListArgs,
} from "./query";

describe("isUuid", () => {
  it("只接受 UUID 字串", () => {
    expect(isUuid("00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f001")).toBe(true);
    expect(isUuid("abc")).toBe(false);
    expect(isUuid(123)).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe("parseOpportunityQuery", () => {
  it("預設是適合我；垃圾值也是", () => {
    expect(parseOpportunityQuery({})).toEqual({ tab: "match", category: null });
    expect(parseOpportunityQuery({ tab: "drop table" })).toEqual({ tab: "match", category: null });
    expect(parseOpportunityQuery(new URLSearchParams("tab=match"))).toEqual({ tab: "match", category: null });
  });
  it("tab 與 cat；cat 優先、會清理並截成 30 字", () => {
    expect(parseOpportunityQuery({ tab: "cash" })).toEqual({ tab: "cash", category: null });
    expect(parseOpportunityQuery({ tab: ["applied", "all"] })).toEqual({ tab: "applied", category: null });
    expect(parseOpportunityQuery({ tab: "all", cat: " 美妝 " })).toEqual({ tab: "category", category: "美妝" });
    expect(parseOpportunityQuery({ cat: "字".repeat(40) }).category).toBe("字".repeat(30));
    expect(parseOpportunityQuery({ cat: "不在清單" })).toEqual({ tab: "category", category: "不在清單" });
    expect(parseOpportunityQuery({ cat: "  " })).toEqual({ tab: "match", category: null });
  });
});

describe("網址與參數", () => {
  it("opportunityHref／QueryString 來回一致", () => {
    for (const { query } of OPPORTUNITY_FILTERS) {
      expect(parseOpportunityQuery(new URLSearchParams(opportunityQueryString(query)))).toEqual(query);
    }
    expect(opportunityHref({ tab: "match", category: null })).toBe("/creator");
    expect(opportunityHref({ tab: "applied", category: null })).toBe("/creator?tab=applied");
    expect(opportunityHref({ tab: "category", category: "美妝" })).toBe("/creator?cat=%E7%BE%8E%E5%A6%9D");
  });
  it("toListArgs", () => {
    expect(toListArgs({ tab: "match", category: null }, 0)).toEqual({ p_filter: "match", p_limit: 20, p_offset: 0 });
    expect(toListArgs({ tab: "category", category: "美妝" }, 20)).toEqual({ p_filter: "category", p_category: "美妝", p_limit: 20, p_offset: 20 });
  });
  it("篩選標籤：適合我、全部、各內容領域、現金、互惠", () => {
    expect(OPPORTUNITY_FILTERS.map((f) => f.label).slice(0, 3)).toEqual(["適合我", "全部", "美妝"]);
    expect(OPPORTUNITY_FILTERS.at(-1)?.label).toBe("互惠");
    expect(sameOpportunityQuery({ tab: "category", category: "美妝" }, { tab: "category", category: "美妝" })).toBe(true);
    expect(sameOpportunityQuery({ tab: "category", category: "美妝" }, { tab: "category", category: "保養" })).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe("品牌", () => {
  it("parseBrandBriefTab", () => {
    expect(parseBrandBriefTab(undefined)).toBe("open");
    expect(parseBrandBriefTab("draft")).toBe("draft");
    expect(parseBrandBriefTab(["ended"])).toBe("ended");
    expect(parseBrandBriefTab("x")).toBe("open");
  });
  it("parseBriefStep", () => {
    expect(parseBriefStep("3")).toBe(3);
    expect(parseBriefStep(["4"])).toBe(4);
    expect(parseBriefStep("5")).toBe(1);
    expect(parseBriefStep("2.0")).toBe(1);
    expect(parseBriefStep(undefined)).toBe(1);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 確認失敗**

```bash
npx vitest run src/lib/validation src/lib/briefs
```
Expected: `cleanTextWithin is not a function`；其他檔案找不到模組。

- [ ] **Step 4: 實作 `cleanTextWithin`**

`src/lib/validation/onboarding.ts` 的 `cleanText` 開頭兩行：

```ts
export function cleanText(v: string): string {
  // 超長輸入不做昂貴處理；回傳的前 MAX_RAW_LENGTH 個單位（至少 500 個字）必然超過所有欄位的長度上限。
  if (v.length > MAX_RAW_LENGTH) return v.slice(0, MAX_RAW_LENGTH);
```

換成（函式其餘部分不動，成為 `cleanTextWithin` 的本體）：

```ts
export function cleanText(v: string): string {
  // 超長輸入不做昂貴處理；回傳的前 MAX_RAW_LENGTH 個單位（至少 500 個字）必然超過 499 字以內欄位的長度上限。
  // 注意：常被當成 .map(cleanText) 使用，所以不能加第二個參數（map 會把索引傳進來）。
  return cleanTextWithin(v, MAX_RAW_LENGTH);
}

/**
 * 與 cleanText 相同，但可以指定截斷長度（給上限 500 字以上的欄位，例如招募說明 2000 字）。
 * 截斷後至少有 maxRaw / 2 個字，所以 maxRaw 必須大於「欄位字數上限 × 2」。
 */
export function cleanTextWithin(v: string, maxRaw: number): string {
  if (v.length > maxRaw) return v.slice(0, maxRaw);
```

- [ ] **Step 5: 實作其他模組**

`src/lib/briefs/dates.ts`：

```ts
// 「今天」一律是台灣時間（Asia/Taipei）的日期，與 SQL 的 public.today_tw() 相同規則。
// 日期字串一律是 YYYY-MM-DD（Postgres date 的 JSON 格式）。

const TW_DATE = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-CA", {
  timeZone: "Asia/Taipei", year: "numeric", month: "2-digit", day: "2-digit",
});
const TW_DATE_TIME = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-CA", {
  timeZone: "Asia/Taipei", year: "numeric", month: "2-digit", day: "2-digit",
  hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit", hourCycle: "h23",
});
const ISO_DATE_RE = /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/;
const DAY_MS = 86_400_000;

/** 截止日最晚是今天起算第 90 天（含）。 */
export const MAX_DEADLINE_DAYS = 90;

/** 台灣今天的日期。會讀現在時間：伺服器元件裡只能在 requireRole／await searchParams 之後呼叫。 */
export function todayTw(now: Date = new Date()): string {
  return TW_DATE.format(now);
}

/** 是否為存在的日期（拒絕 2026-02-30 這種）。 */
export function isIsoDate(v: string): boolean {
  const m = ISO_DATE_RE.exec(v);
  if (!m) return false;
  const [y, mo, d] = [Number(m[1]), Number(m[2]), Number(m[3])];
  const t = new Date(Date.UTC(y, mo - 1, d));
  return t.getUTCFullYear() === y && t.getUTCMonth() === mo - 1 && t.getUTCDate() === d;
}

function toUtc(date: string): number {
  const [y, m, d] = date.split("-").map(Number);
  return Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d);
}

export function addDays(date: string, days: number): string {
  return new Date(toUtc(date) + days * DAY_MS).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
}

/** to − from 的天數（同一天是 0）。 */
export function daysBetween(from: string, to: string): number {
  return Math.round((toUtc(to) - toUtc(from)) / DAY_MS);
}

/** 「今天截止」「剩 3 天」；已過截止日是「已截止」。 */
export function deadlineLabel(deadline: string, today: string): string {
  const n = daysBetween(today, deadline);
  if (n < 0) return "已截止";
  if (n === 0) return "今天截止";
  return `剩 ${n} 天`;
}

/** 2026-11-24 → 2026/11/24 */
export function formatDate(date: string): string {
  return date.replaceAll("-", "/");
}

/** timestamptz（ISO 字串）→ 台灣時間的 2026/11/24 */
export function formatDateTw(iso: string): string {
  return formatDate(todayTw(new Date(iso)));
}

/** timestamptz（ISO 字串）→ 台灣時間的 2026/11/24 14:05 */
export function formatDateTimeTw(iso: string): string {
  const parts = Object.fromEntries(TW_DATE_TIME.formatToParts(new Date(iso)).map((p) => [p.type, p.value]));
  return `${parts.year}/${parts.month}/${parts.day} ${parts.hour}:${parts.minute}`;
}
```

`src/lib/validation/brief.ts`：

```ts
// 規則與 supabase/migrations/20261124000500_briefs.sql 的 save_brief、apply_to_brief 一致；資料庫是最後防線。
import { addDays, isIsoDate, MAX_DEADLINE_DAYS } from "@/lib/briefs/dates";
import { MAX_PRICE_TWD } from "./creator-profile";
import { cleanText, cleanTextWithin, hasBidi } from "./onboarding";

export const MAX_FOLLOWERS = 100_000_000;
const BAD_CHAR_MESSAGE = "含有不允許的特殊字元";
// 只檢查格式；「必須在自己的資料夾」由資料庫的 check_media_path 檢查
const COVER_PATH_RE = /^[0-9a-f-]{36}\/cover-[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._-]{0,99}$/;

/**
 * 雙向控制字元或 NUL。NUL（U+0000）不能存進 Postgres text（PostgREST 會回 22P05），
 * 資料庫端根本收不到，所以只能在這裡擋（followups「NUL」一項）。
 */
export function hasBadChar(v: string): boolean {
  return hasBidi(v) || v.includes("\u0000");
}

/** 字數（與 Postgres char_length 一致，以 code point 計算）。 */
function len(v: string): number {
  return [...v].length;
}

/** 數字欄位：去掉千分位逗號與空白。 */
function digits(v: string): string {
  return cleanText(v).replace(/[,，\s]/g, "");
}

function str(fd: FormData, key: string): string {
  const v = fd.get(key);
  return typeof v === "string" ? v : "";
}

function strings(fd: FormData, key: string): string[] {
  return fd.getAll(key).filter((v): v is string => typeof v === "string");
}

/** 表單上的招募內容（全部是使用者輸入的文字，數字欄位空字串代表沒填）。 */
export type BriefInput = {
  title: string;
  description: string;
  collabTypes: string[];
  coverPath: string | null;
  categories: string[];
  minFollowers: string;
  region: string;
  budgetMin: string;
  budgetMax: string;
  includesProduct: boolean;
  deadline: string;
};

/** reward 是「預算和產品至少要有一項」，顯示在第 3 步。 */
export type BriefField = Exclude<keyof BriefInput, "includesProduct"> | "reward";
export type BriefStep = 1 | 2 | 3 | 4;

export const BRIEF_STEP_FIELDS: Record<1 | 2 | 3, BriefField[]> = {
  1: ["title", "description", "collabTypes", "coverPath"],
  2: ["categories", "minFollowers", "region"],
  3: ["budgetMin", "budgetMax", "reward", "deadline"],
};

export const EMPTY_BRIEF_INPUT: BriefInput = {
  title: "", description: "", collabTypes: [], coverPath: null, categories: [], minFollowers: "",
  region: "", budgetMin: "", budgetMax: "", includesProduct: false, deadline: "",
};

/** 說明的截斷長度：8000 個 UTF-16 單位至少 4000 字，必然超過 2000 字上限。 */
const DESCRIPTION_RAW_LIMIT = 8000;
/** 報名留言的截斷長度：2000 個單位至少 1000 字，必然超過 500 字上限。 */
const MESSAGE_RAW_LIMIT = 2000;

export function parseBriefForm(fd: FormData): BriefInput {
  const cover = cleanText(str(fd, "coverPath"));
  return {
    title: cleanText(str(fd, "title")),
    description: cleanTextWithin(str(fd, "description").replace(/\r\n?/g, "\n"), DESCRIPTION_RAW_LIMIT),
    collabTypes: strings(fd, "collabTypes").map(cleanText),
    coverPath: cover || null,
    categories: strings(fd, "categories").map(cleanText),
    minFollowers: digits(str(fd, "minFollowers")),
    region: cleanText(str(fd, "region")),
    budgetMin: digits(str(fd, "budgetMin")),
    budgetMax: digits(str(fd, "budgetMax")),
    includesProduct: fd.get("includesProduct") === "on",
    deadline: cleanText(str(fd, "deadline")),
  };
}

function listError(list: string[], maxCount: number, maxLen: number): boolean {
  return list.length > maxCount
    || list.some((t) => hasBadChar(t) || len(t) < 1 || len(t) > maxLen)
    || new Set(list).size !== list.length;
}

function priceOk(v: string): boolean {
  return /^\d{1,8}$/.test(v) && Number(v) <= MAX_PRICE_TWD;
}

/** 預算：空字串與 0 都是「沒填」（與資料庫 nullif(…, 0) 一致）。 */
function budgetValue(v: string): number {
  return priceOk(v) ? Number(v) : 0;
}

/**
 * draft：只檢查格式（草稿可以缺必填）；publish：再檢查必填、預算或產品、截止日（今天到 90 天內）。
 * 錯誤的 key 是 BriefField。
 */
export function validateBrief(i: BriefInput, mode: "draft" | "publish", today: string): Record<string, string> | null {
  const e: Record<string, string> = {};
  const publish = mode === "publish";

  if (hasBadChar(i.title)) e.title = BAD_CHAR_MESSAGE;
  else if (len(i.title) > 60) e.title = "案名最多 60 字";
  else if (publish && i.title === "") e.title = "請填寫案名（60 字以內）";

  if (hasBadChar(i.description)) e.description = BAD_CHAR_MESSAGE;
  else if (len(i.description) > 2000) e.description = "說明最多 2000 字";
  else if (publish && i.description === "") e.description = "請填寫合作說明";

  if (listError(i.collabTypes, 6, 20)) e.collabTypes = "請重新選擇合作形式";
  else if (publish && i.collabTypes.length === 0) e.collabTypes = "請至少選一種合作形式";

  if (i.coverPath !== null && (!COVER_PATH_RE.test(i.coverPath) || i.coverPath.includes(".."))) {
    e.coverPath = "封面圖片有問題，請重新上傳";
  }

  if (listError(i.categories, 5, 30)) e.categories = "最多選 5 個內容領域";

  if (i.minFollowers !== "" && !(/^\d{1,9}$/.test(i.minFollowers) && Number(i.minFollowers) <= MAX_FOLLOWERS)) {
    e.minFollowers = "請填 0 以上的整數";
  }

  if (hasBadChar(i.region) || len(i.region) > 30) e.region = "請重新選擇地區";

  if (i.budgetMin !== "" && !priceOk(i.budgetMin)) e.budgetMin = "請填 0 到 10,000,000 的整數";
  if (i.budgetMax !== "" && !priceOk(i.budgetMax)) e.budgetMax = "請填 0 到 10,000,000 的整數";
  const min = budgetValue(i.budgetMin);
  const max = budgetValue(i.budgetMax);
  if (!e.budgetMin && !e.budgetMax && max > 0 && max < min) e.budgetMax = "上限不能低於下限";
  if (publish && min === 0 && max === 0 && !i.includesProduct) e.reward = "預算和產品至少要有一項";

  if (i.deadline !== "" && !isIsoDate(i.deadline)) {
    e.deadline = "日期格式不正確";
  } else if (publish) {
    if (i.deadline === "") e.deadline = "請選擇截止日";
    else if (i.deadline < today || i.deadline > addDays(today, MAX_DEADLINE_DAYS)) {
      e.deadline = `截止日要在今天到 ${MAX_DEADLINE_DAYS} 天內`;
    }
  }
  return Object.keys(e).length ? e : null;
}

/** 按「下一步」時只檢查這一步的欄位（必填也檢查）；第 4 步檢查全部。 */
export function validateBriefStep(step: BriefStep, i: BriefInput, today: string): Record<string, string> | null {
  const all = validateBrief(i, "publish", today);
  if (!all || step === 4) return all;
  const fields: string[] = BRIEF_STEP_FIELDS[step];
  const picked = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(all).filter(([k]) => fields.includes(k)));
  return Object.keys(picked).length ? picked : null;
}

/** 有錯誤的欄位中，最前面的那一步（送出失敗時跳過去）。 */
export function firstErrorStep(errors: Record<string, string>): BriefStep {
  for (const step of [1, 2, 3] as const) {
    if (BRIEF_STEP_FIELDS[step].some((f) => f in errors)) return step;
  }
  return 4;
}

/** save_brief 的參數。空字串 → null；預算 0 由資料庫轉成 null。 */
export function toSaveBriefArgs(id: string | null, i: BriefInput, publish: boolean) {
  return {
    p_id: id ?? undefined,
    p_title: i.title,
    p_description: i.description,
    p_collab_types: i.collabTypes,
    p_categories: i.categories,
    p_min_followers: i.minFollowers === "" ? 0 : Number(i.minFollowers),
    p_region: i.region,
    p_budget_min_twd: i.budgetMin === "" ? undefined : Number(i.budgetMin),
    p_budget_max_twd: i.budgetMax === "" ? undefined : Number(i.budgetMax),
    p_includes_product: i.includesProduct,
    p_deadline: i.deadline === "" ? undefined : i.deadline,
    p_cover_path: i.coverPath ?? undefined,
    p_publish: publish,
  };
}

export type ApplicationInput = { briefId: string; message: string; proposedPrice: string };

export function parseApplicationForm(fd: FormData): ApplicationInput {
  return {
    briefId: cleanText(str(fd, "briefId")),
    message: cleanTextWithin(str(fd, "message").replace(/\r\n?/g, "\n"), MESSAGE_RAW_LIMIT),
    proposedPrice: digits(str(fd, "proposedPrice")),
  };
}

export function validateApplication(i: ApplicationInput): Record<string, string> | null {
  const e: Record<string, string> = {};
  if (hasBadChar(i.message)) e.message = BAD_CHAR_MESSAGE;
  else if (len(i.message) > 500) e.message = "最多 500 字";
  if (i.proposedPrice !== "" && !priceOk(i.proposedPrice)) e.proposedPrice = "請填 0 到 10,000,000 的整數";
  return Object.keys(e).length ? e : null;
}
```

`src/lib/briefs/view.ts`：

```ts
// 招募、機會、報名的顯示用型別與轉換。
// supabase gen types 把 RPC 的 RETURNS TABLE 欄位都標成非 null，實際上可能是 null：Row 型別一律自己定義、欄位可為 null。
import { formatNumber, formatTwd } from "@/lib/format";
import { toNumberOrNull } from "@/lib/creators/view";
import type { BriefInput } from "@/lib/validation/brief";

export type BriefStatus = "draft" | "open" | "closed";
/** 畫面上的狀態：open 但已過截止日顯示「已截止」（不寫進資料庫）。 */
export type BriefDisplayStatus = "draft" | "open" | "expired" | "closed";
export type ApplicationStatus = "applied" | "accepted" | "rejected" | "withdrawn";

const BRIEF_STATUSES: readonly string[] = ["draft", "open", "closed"];
const APPLICATION_STATUSES: readonly string[] = ["applied", "accepted", "rejected", "withdrawn"];

/** 不認得的值當成 closed（最保守：不能編輯、不能報名）。 */
export function toBriefStatus(v: unknown): BriefStatus {
  return typeof v === "string" && BRIEF_STATUSES.includes(v) ? (v as BriefStatus) : "closed";
}

/** 不認得的值當成 withdrawn（最保守：沒有任何操作）。 */
export function toApplicationStatus(v: unknown): ApplicationStatus {
  return typeof v === "string" && APPLICATION_STATUSES.includes(v) ? (v as ApplicationStatus) : "withdrawn";
}

export function displayStatusOf(status: BriefStatus, deadline: string | null, today: string): BriefDisplayStatus {
  if (status === "open" && deadline !== null && deadline < today) return "expired";
  return status;
}

export const BRIEF_STATUS_LABEL: Record<BriefDisplayStatus, string> = {
  draft: "草稿", open: "發佈中", expired: "已截止", closed: "已關閉",
};
export const CREATOR_APPLICATION_LABEL: Record<ApplicationStatus, string> = {
  applied: "待回覆", accepted: "已錄取", rejected: "未錄取", withdrawn: "已撤回",
};
export const BRAND_APPLICATION_LABEL: Record<ApplicationStatus, string> = {
  applied: "待處理", accepted: "已錄取", rejected: "已婉拒", withdrawn: "已撤回",
};

export type Reward = { budgetMin: number | null; budgetMax: number | null; includesProduct: boolean };

/** 「NT$5,000–20,000」「NT$5,000 起」「最高 NT$20,000」「產品互惠 + NT$3,000 起」「產品互惠」；都沒有是「—」。 */
export function rewardLabel(r: Reward): string {
  const min = r.budgetMin !== null && r.budgetMin > 0 ? r.budgetMin : null;
  const max = r.budgetMax !== null && r.budgetMax > 0 ? r.budgetMax : null;
  let cash: string | null = null;
  if (min !== null && max !== null) cash = min === max ? formatTwd(min) : `${formatTwd(min)}–${formatNumber(max)}`;
  else if (min !== null) cash = `${formatTwd(min)} 起`;
  else if (max !== null) cash = `最高 ${formatTwd(max)}`;
  if (r.includesProduct) return cash ? `產品互惠 + ${cash}` : "產品互惠";
  return cash ?? "—";
}

export function audienceLabel(minFollowers: number): string {
  return minFollowers > 0 ? `粉絲 ${formatNumber(minFollowers)} 以上` : "粉絲數不限";
}

export type MatchFlags = { followers: boolean; region: boolean; category: boolean; collab: boolean };

/** 詳情頁的條件清單：每一項是否符合。 */
export function matchItems(o: {
  minFollowers: number; region: string | null; categories: string[]; collabTypes: string[]; match: MatchFlags;
}): { label: string; ok: boolean }[] {
  return [
    { label: audienceLabel(o.minFollowers), ok: o.match.followers },
    { label: o.region ? `地區：${o.region}` : "地區不限", ok: o.match.region },
    { label: o.categories.length ? `內容領域：${o.categories.join("、")}` : "內容領域不限", ok: o.match.category },
    { label: `合作形式：${o.collabTypes.join("、") || "—"}`, ok: o.match.collab },
  ];
}

// ── 品牌端：自己的招募 ──

export type Brief = Reward & {
  id: string;
  brandId: string;
  title: string;
  description: string;
  collabTypes: string[];
  categories: string[];
  minFollowers: number;
  region: string | null;
  deadline: string | null;
  coverPath: string | null;
  status: BriefStatus;
  publishedAt: string | null;
  createdAt: string;
};

export type BriefRow = {
  id: string;
  brand_id: string;
  title: string | null;
  description: string | null;
  collab_types: string[] | null;
  categories: string[] | null;
  min_followers: number | null;
  region: string | null;
  budget_min_twd: number | null;
  budget_max_twd: number | null;
  includes_product: boolean | null;
  deadline: string | null;
  cover_path: string | null;
  status: string;
  published_at: string | null;
  created_at: string;
};

export const BRIEF_COLUMNS =
  "id, brand_id, title, description, collab_types, categories, min_followers, region, budget_min_twd, "
  + "budget_max_twd, includes_product, deadline, cover_path, status, published_at, created_at";

export function toBrief(r: BriefRow): Brief {
  return {
    id: r.id,
    brandId: r.brand_id,
    title: r.title ?? "",
    description: r.description ?? "",
    collabTypes: r.collab_types ?? [],
    categories: r.categories ?? [],
    minFollowers: r.min_followers ?? 0,
    region: r.region ?? null,
    budgetMin: r.budget_min_twd ?? null,
    budgetMax: r.budget_max_twd ?? null,
    includesProduct: r.includes_product === true,
    deadline: r.deadline ?? null,
    coverPath: r.cover_path ?? null,
    status: toBriefStatus(r.status),
    publishedAt: r.published_at ?? null,
    createdAt: r.created_at,
  };
}

export const UNTITLED_DRAFT = "（未命名草稿）";

export type CountRow = { brief_id: string | null; applicant_count: number | string | null; pending_count: number | string | null };
export type BrandBriefListItem = Brief & { applicantCount: number; pendingCount: number };

export function withCounts(briefs: Brief[], counts: CountRow[]): BrandBriefListItem[] {
  const byId = new Map(counts.filter((c) => c.brief_id).map((c) => [c.brief_id as string, c]));
  return briefs.map((b) => {
    const c = byId.get(b.id);
    return { ...b, applicantCount: toNumberOrNull(c?.applicant_count ?? null) ?? 0, pendingCount: toNumberOrNull(c?.pending_count ?? null) ?? 0 };
  });
}

export type BrandBriefTab = "open" | "draft" | "ended";

export function brandTabOf(s: BriefDisplayStatus): BrandBriefTab {
  if (s === "draft") return "draft";
  if (s === "open") return "open";
  return "ended";
}

/** 編輯表單的初始值。 */
export function briefToInput(b: Brief): BriefInput {
  return {
    title: b.title,
    description: b.description,
    collabTypes: b.collabTypes,
    coverPath: b.coverPath,
    categories: b.categories,
    minFollowers: b.minFollowers > 0 ? String(b.minFollowers) : "",
    region: b.region ?? "",
    budgetMin: b.budgetMin !== null ? String(b.budgetMin) : "",
    budgetMax: b.budgetMax !== null ? String(b.budgetMax) : "",
    includesProduct: b.includesProduct,
    deadline: b.deadline ?? "",
  };
}

// ── 詳情頁（創作者看到的樣式；品牌的預覽也用這個） ──

export type BriefDetailData = Reward & {
  title: string;
  description: string;
  collabTypes: string[];
  categories: string[];
  minFollowers: number;
  region: string | null;
  deadline: string | null;
  coverPath: string | null;
  brandName: string;
  brandLogoPath: string | null;
  /** 預覽時是 null（還沒有人報名，不顯示） */
  applicantCount: number | null;
};

function toIntOrNull(v: string): number | null {
  if (!/^\d{1,9}$/.test(v)) return null;
  const n = Number(v);
  return n > 0 ? n : null;
}

/** 表單預覽：還沒存的內容照填寫的樣子顯示，數字填錯的當成沒填。 */
export function previewFromInput(i: BriefInput, brand: { name: string; logoPath: string | null }): BriefDetailData {
  return {
    title: i.title || "（未填案名）",
    description: i.description,
    collabTypes: i.collabTypes,
    categories: i.categories,
    minFollowers: toIntOrNull(i.minFollowers) ?? 0,
    region: i.region || null,
    budgetMin: toIntOrNull(i.budgetMin),
    budgetMax: toIntOrNull(i.budgetMax),
    includesProduct: i.includesProduct,
    deadline: i.deadline || null,
    coverPath: i.coverPath,
    brandName: brand.name,
    brandLogoPath: brand.logoPath,
    applicantCount: null,
  };
}

// ── 創作者端：機會 ──

export type Opportunity = Reward & {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  collabTypes: string[];
  categories: string[];
  minFollowers: number;
  region: string | null;
  deadline: string;
  coverPath: string | null;
  brandName: string;
  brandLogoPath: string | null;
  applicantCount: number;
  match: MatchFlags;
  isMatch: boolean;
  myStatus: ApplicationStatus | null;
};

export type OpportunityRow = {
  id: string;
  title: string | null;
  collab_types: string[] | null;
  categories: string[] | null;
  min_followers: number | null;
  region: string | null;
  budget_min_twd: number | null;
  budget_max_twd: number | null;
  includes_product: boolean | null;
  deadline: string | null;
  cover_path: string | null;
  brand_name: string | null;
  brand_logo_path: string | null;
  applicant_count: number | string | null;
  followers_ok: boolean | null;
  region_ok: boolean | null;
  category_ok: boolean | null;
  collab_ok: boolean | null;
  is_match: boolean | null;
  my_status: string | null;
  total_count?: number | string | null;
};

export function toOpportunity(r: OpportunityRow): Opportunity {
  return {
    id: r.id,
    title: r.title ?? "",
    collabTypes: r.collab_types ?? [],
    categories: r.categories ?? [],
    minFollowers: r.min_followers ?? 0,
    region: r.region ?? null,
    budgetMin: r.budget_min_twd ?? null,
    budgetMax: r.budget_max_twd ?? null,
    includesProduct: r.includes_product === true,
    deadline: r.deadline ?? "",
    coverPath: r.cover_path ?? null,
    brandName: r.brand_name ?? "",
    brandLogoPath: r.brand_logo_path ?? null,
    applicantCount: toNumberOrNull(r.applicant_count) ?? 0,
    match: {
      followers: r.followers_ok === true,
      region: r.region_ok === true,
      category: r.category_ok === true,
      collab: r.collab_ok === true,
    },
    isMatch: r.is_match === true,
    myStatus: r.my_status === null || r.my_status === undefined ? null : toApplicationStatus(r.my_status),
  };
}

export const OPPORTUNITY_PAGE_SIZE = 20;
export type OpportunityPage = { items: Opportunity[]; total: number; hasMore: boolean };

export function toOpportunityPage(rows: OpportunityRow[], offset: number): OpportunityPage {
  const total = toNumberOrNull(rows[0]?.total_count ?? null) ?? 0;
  return { items: rows.map(toOpportunity), total, hasMore: offset + rows.length < total };
}

/** 載入更多：已顯示的在前，新的接在後面，以 id 去重。 */
export function appendUniqueById<T extends { id: string }>(current: T[], more: T[]): T[] {
  const seen = new Set(current.map((x) => x.id));
  return [...current, ...more.filter((x) => !seen.has(x.id) && seen.add(x.id))];
}

export type OpportunityDetail = Opportunity & {
  description: string;
  status: BriefStatus;
  application: { id: string; status: ApplicationStatus; message: string | null; proposedPrice: number | null } | null;
};

export type OpportunityDetailRow = OpportunityRow & {
  description: string | null;
  status: string;
  my_application_id: string | null;
  my_message: string | null;
  my_proposed_price_twd: number | null;
};

export function toOpportunityDetail(r: OpportunityDetailRow): OpportunityDetail {
  return {
    ...toOpportunity(r),
    description: r.description ?? "",
    status: toBriefStatus(r.status),
    application: r.my_application_id
      ? {
        id: r.my_application_id,
        status: toApplicationStatus(r.my_status),
        message: r.my_message ?? null,
        proposedPrice: r.my_proposed_price_twd ?? null,
      }
      : null,
  };
}

export function detailDataOf(o: OpportunityDetail): BriefDetailData {
  return {
    title: o.title, description: o.description, collabTypes: o.collabTypes, categories: o.categories,
    minFollowers: o.minFollowers, region: o.region, budgetMin: o.budgetMin, budgetMax: o.budgetMax,
    includesProduct: o.includesProduct, deadline: o.deadline, coverPath: o.coverPath, brandName: o.brandName,
    brandLogoPath: o.brandLogoPath, applicantCount: o.applicantCount,
  };
}

export type MyApplication = {

  createdAt: string;

};

export type MyApplicationRow = {

  status: string;

  briefs: {
    id: string;
    title: string | null;

    budget_max_twd: number | null;

    brand_profiles: { brand_name: string | null } | null;
  } | null;
};

export const MY_APPLICATION_COLUMNS =
  "id, status, created_at, briefs(id, title, deadline, status, budget_min_twd, budget_max_twd, includes_product, brand_profiles(brand_name))";

/** 招募讀不到（理論上不會）的報名略過。 */
export function toMyApplications(rows: MyApplicationRow[]): MyApplication[] {
  return rows.flatMap((r) => r.briefs
    ? [{
      id: r.id,
      status: toApplicationStatus(r.status),
      createdAt: r.created_at,
      brief: {
        id: r.briefs.id,
        title: r.briefs.title ?? "",
        deadline: r.briefs.deadline ?? null,
        status: toBriefStatus(r.briefs.status),
        brandName: r.briefs.brand_profiles?.brand_name ?? "",
        budgetMin: r.briefs.budget_min_twd ?? null,
        budgetMax: r.briefs.budget_max_twd ?? null,
        includesProduct: r.briefs.includes_product === true,
      },
    }]
    : []);
}

// ── 品牌端：報名者 ──

export type Applicant = {
  id: string;
  creatorId: string;
  handle: string;
  displayName: string;
  avatarPath: string | null;
  followerCount: number;
  engagementRate: number | null;
  region: string | null;

};

export type ApplicantRow = {
  id: string;
  creator_id: string;
  handle: string | null;
  display_name: string | null;
  avatar_path: string | null;
  follower_count: number | null;
  engagement_rate: number | string | null;
  region: string | null;
  message: string | null;
  proposed_price_twd: number | null;
  status: string;
  created_at: string;
};

export function toApplicant(r: ApplicantRow): Applicant {
  return {
    id: r.id,
    creatorId: r.creator_id,
    handle: r.handle ?? "",
    displayName: r.display_name ?? "",
    avatarPath: r.avatar_path ?? null,
    followerCount: r.follower_count ?? 0,
    engagementRate: toNumberOrNull(r.engagement_rate),
    region: r.region ?? null,
    message: r.message ?? null,
    proposedPrice: r.proposed_price_twd ?? null,
    status: toApplicationStatus(r.status),
    createdAt: r.created_at,
  };
}

export type ApplicantFilter = "all" | "applied" | "accepted" | "rejected";
export const APPLICANT_FILTERS: { value: ApplicantFilter; label: string }[] = [
  { value: "all", label: "全部" },
  { value: "applied", label: "待處理" },
  { value: "accepted", label: "已錄取" },
  { value: "rejected", label: "已婉拒" },
];

export function filterApplicants(list: Applicant[], f: ApplicantFilter): Applicant[] {
  return f === "all" ? list : list.filter((a) => a.status === f);
}

export function proposedPriceLabel(p: number | null): string {
  return p === null ? "依招募預算" : formatTwd(p);
}
```

`src/lib/briefs/query.ts`：

```ts
// 網址參數 ⇄ 查詢。任何垃圾值都不能讓頁面或資料庫報錯（followups「搜尋與篩選必須能容忍清單以外的值」）。
import { CATEGORIES } from "@/lib/options";
import { cleanText } from "@/lib/validation/onboarding";
import type { BriefStep } from "@/lib/validation/brief";
import { OPPORTUNITY_PAGE_SIZE, type BrandBriefTab } from "./view";

type Params = Record<string, string | string[] | undefined> | URLSearchParams;

function first(p: Params, key: string): string | null {
  if (p instanceof URLSearchParams) return p.get(key);
  const v = p[key];
  return Array.isArray(v) ? (v[0] ?? null) : (v ?? null);
}

const UUID_RE = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i;

/** 網址的 [id] 與 Server Action 的 id 先檢查格式，避免把垃圾送進 uuid 參數（22P02）。 */
export function isUuid(v: unknown): v is string {
  return typeof v === "string" && UUID_RE.test(v);
}

// ── 創作者：機會頁 ──

export type OpportunityTab = "match" | "all" | "category" | "cash" | "product" | "applied";
export type OpportunityQuery = { tab: OpportunityTab; category: string | null };
export const DEFAULT_OPPORTUNITY_QUERY: OpportunityQuery = { tab: "match", category: null };

const SIMPLE_TABS: readonly string[] = ["all", "cash", "product", "applied"];

/** ?tab=all|cash|product|applied、?cat=<內容領域>（cat 優先）；其他一律是「適合我」。 */
export function parseOpportunityQuery(p: Params): OpportunityQuery {
  const cat = Array.from(cleanText(first(p, "cat") ?? "")).slice(0, 30).join("");
  if (cat) return { tab: "category", category: cat };
  const tab = first(p, "tab");
  if (tab && SIMPLE_TABS.includes(tab)) return { tab: tab as OpportunityTab, category: null };
  return DEFAULT_OPPORTUNITY_QUERY;
}

/** 查詢字串（不含 ?）：載入更多時原樣送回伺服器重新解析。 */
export function opportunityQueryString(q: OpportunityQuery): string {
  if (q.tab === "category" && q.category) return new URLSearchParams({ cat: q.category }).toString();
  if (q.tab === "match" || q.tab === "category") return "";
  return new URLSearchParams({ tab: q.tab }).toString();
}

export function opportunityHref(q: OpportunityQuery): string {
  const qs = opportunityQueryString(q);
  return qs ? `/creator?${qs}` : "/creator";
}

/** 機會頁上方的篩選標籤（一次只選一個）。 */
export const OPPORTUNITY_FILTERS: { label: string; query: OpportunityQuery }[] = [
  { label: "適合我", query: DEFAULT_OPPORTUNITY_QUERY },
  { label: "全部", query: { tab: "all", category: null } },
  ...CATEGORIES.map((c) => ({ label: c, query: { tab: "category" as const, category: c } })),
  { label: "現金", query: { tab: "cash", category: null } },
  { label: "互惠", query: { tab: "product", category: null } },
];

export function sameOpportunityQuery(a: OpportunityQuery, b: OpportunityQuery): boolean {
  return a.tab === b.tab && a.category === b.category;
}

/** list_opportunities 的參數（「我的報名」不呼叫這個函式）。 */
export function toListArgs(q: OpportunityQuery, offset: number) {
  const filter = q.tab === "applied" ? "all" : q.tab;
  return {
    p_filter: filter,
    ...(filter === "category" && q.category ? { p_category: q.category } : {}),
    p_limit: OPPORTUNITY_PAGE_SIZE,
    p_offset: offset,
  };
}

// ── 品牌：我的招募、表單步驟 ──

export function parseBrandBriefTab(v: string | string[] | undefined): BrandBriefTab {
  const s = Array.isArray(v) ? v[0] : v;
  return s === "draft" || s === "ended" ? s : "open";
}

export const BRAND_BRIEF_TABS: { value: BrandBriefTab; label: string }[] = [
  { value: "open", label: "發佈中" },
  { value: "draft", label: "草稿" },
  { value: "ended", label: "已結束" },
];

/** ?step=1..4；其他一律是 1。 */
export function parseBriefStep(v: string | string[] | undefined | null): BriefStep {
  const s = Array.isArray(v) ? v[0] : v;
  return s === "2" || s === "3" || s === "4" ? (Number(s) as BriefStep) : 1;
}
```

```ts
// 招募相關 definer 函式的錯誤（supabase/migrations/20261124000500_briefs.sql）→ 畫面文字。
type DbError = { code?: string; message?: string };

const MESSAGES: Record<string, string> = {
  "not authenticated": "登入已逾時，請重新登入",
  "not a brand": "這個帳號不是品牌，無法執行此操作",
  "not a creator": "這個帳號不是創作者，無法執行此操作",
  "creator not approved": "審核通過後即可報名",
  "not brief owner": "找不到這則招募",
  "brief not found": "找不到這則招募",
  "brief not editable": "這則招募已結束，不能再修改",
  "brief not open": "這則招募已經不是發佈中",
  "brief not draft": "只有草稿可以刪除",
  "brief closed": "這則招募已結束，無法報名",
  "already applied": "你已經報名過這則招募",
  "application not found": "找不到這筆報名",
  "application not pending": "這筆報名已經處理過了",
};

const FIELD_BY_DB: Record<string, string> = {
  title: "title",
  description: "description",
  collab_types: "collabTypes",
  categories: "categories",
  region: "region",
  min_followers: "minFollowers",
  budget_min_twd: "budgetMin",
  budget_max_twd: "budgetMax",
  reward: "reward",
  deadline: "deadline",
  cover_path: "coverPath",
  message: "message",
  proposed_price_twd: "proposedPrice",
};

const FIELD_MESSAGE: Record<string, string> = {
  reward: "預算和產品至少要有一項",
  deadline: "截止日要在今天到 90 天內",
};

export function briefErrorMessage(error: DbError): string {
  const m = error.message ?? "";
  return Object.hasOwn(MESSAGES, m) ? MESSAGES[m] : "發生錯誤，請稍後再試";
}

/** 22023 invalid <欄位> → 表單欄位錯誤；其他錯誤回傳 null。 */
export function briefFieldError(error: DbError): Record<string, string> | null {
  if (error.code !== "22023") return null;
  const key = (error.message ?? "").replace(/^invalid /, "");
  if (!Object.hasOwn(FIELD_BY_DB, key)) return null;
  const field = FIELD_BY_DB[key];
  return { [field]: Object.hasOwn(FIELD_MESSAGE, field) ? FIELD_MESSAGE[field] : "格式不正確" };
}
```

- Produces:

  - `listOpportunities(query, offset = 0): Promise<OpportunityPage>`、`loadOpportunity(id): Promise<OpportunityDetail | null>`、`loadMyApplications(creatorId): Promise<MyApplication[]>`（`src/lib/briefs/creator.ts`）。
  - `src/app/brand/briefs/actions.ts`：`BriefFormState = { fieldErrors?; formError?; saved? }`、`ActionResult`、`saveBriefAction(prev, fd)`（表單另有隱藏欄位 `id`、`step`，按鈕 `name="intent"` 值 `draft|publish`）、`closeBriefAction(id)`、`deleteBriefAction(id)`（成功 redirect 到 `/brand/briefs?tab=draft`）、`decideApplicationAction(applicationId, briefId, accept)`。
  - `src/app/creator/actions.ts`：`LoadMoreOpportunitiesResult`、`ApplyFormState = { ok?; fieldErrors?; formError? }`、`ActionResult`、`loadMoreOpportunitiesAction(queryString, offset)`、`applyToBriefAction(prev, fd)`、`withdrawApplicationAction(applicationId, briefId)`。

```ts
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";

const { rpc, from } = vi.hoisted(() => ({ rpc: vi.fn(), from: vi.fn() }));
vi.mock("server-only", () => ({}));
vi.mock("@/lib/supabase/server", () => ({ createClient: async () => ({ rpc, from }) }));

import { loadApplicants, loadMyBriefs, loadOwnBrief } from "./brand";

/** 模擬 supabase-js 的鏈式查詢：每個方法回傳自己，await 時得到 result。 */
function chain(result: unknown) {
  const q: Record<string, unknown> = {};
  for (const m of ["select", "eq", "order", "limit"]) q[m] = vi.fn(() => q);
  q.maybeSingle = vi.fn(async () => result);
  q.then = (resolve: (v: unknown) => unknown) => resolve(result);
  return q;
}

const row = {
  id: "b1", brand_id: "u1", title: "x", description: null, collab_types: null, categories: null, min_followers: null,
  region: null, budget_min_twd: null, budget_max_twd: null, includes_product: null, deadline: null, cover_path: null,
  status: "draft", published_at: null, created_at: "2026-11-24T00:00:00Z",
};

beforeEach(() => {
  rpc.mockReset();
  from.mockReset();
});

describe("loadMyBriefs", () => {
  it("只讀自己的，再查人數", async () => {
    const q = chain({ data: [row], error: null });
    from.mockReturnValue(q);
    rpc.mockResolvedValue({ data: [{ brief_id: "b1", applicant_count: 2, pending_count: 1 }], error: null });
    const list = await loadMyBriefs("u1");
    expect(from).toHaveBeenCalledWith("briefs");
    expect(q.eq).toHaveBeenCalledWith("brand_id", "u1");
    expect(rpc).toHaveBeenCalledWith("brief_application_counts", { p_ids: ["b1"] });
    expect(list[0]).toMatchObject({ id: "b1", applicantCount: 2, pendingCount: 1 });
  });
  it("沒有招募時不查人數", async () => {
    from.mockReturnValue(chain({ data: [], error: null }));
    expect(await loadMyBriefs("u1")).toEqual([]);
    expect(rpc).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
  it("錯誤時丟出", async () => {
    from.mockReturnValue(chain({ data: null, error: { message: "boom" } }));
    await expect(loadMyBriefs("u1")).rejects.toEqual({ message: "boom" });
  });
});

describe("loadOwnBrief", () => {
  it("找不到回傳 null；找到轉成 Brief", async () => {
    from.mockReturnValue(chain({ data: null, error: null }));
    expect(await loadOwnBrief("u1", "b1")).toBeNull();
    const q = chain({ data: row, error: null });
    from.mockReturnValue(q);
    expect(await loadOwnBrief("u1", "b1")).toMatchObject({ id: "b1", title: "x" });
    expect(q.eq).toHaveBeenCalledWith("brand_id", "u1");
  });
});

describe("loadApplicants", () => {
  it("呼叫 list_brief_applicants", async () => {
    rpc.mockResolvedValue({ data: [{ id: "a1", creator_id: "c1", handle: "mei", display_name: "小美", avatar_path: null,
      follower_count: 10, engagement_rate: null, region: null, message: null, proposed_price_twd: null, status: "applied",
      created_at: "t" }], error: null });
    expect(await loadApplicants("b1")).toMatchObject([{ id: "a1", status: "applied" }]);
    expect(rpc).toHaveBeenCalledWith("list_brief_applicants", { p_brief_id: "b1" });
  });
});
```

```ts
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";

const { requireRole, rpc, remove, revalidatePath, redirect } = vi.hoisted(() => ({
  requireRole: vi.fn(), rpc: vi.fn(), remove: vi.fn(), revalidatePath: vi.fn(),
  redirect: vi.fn((url: string) => { throw new Error(`REDIRECT ${url}`); }),
}));
vi.mock("server-only", () => ({}));
vi.mock("next/cache", () => ({ revalidatePath }));
vi.mock("next/navigation", () => ({ redirect }));
vi.mock("@/lib/auth/current-user", () => ({ requireRole }));
vi.mock("@/lib/supabase/server", () => ({
  createClient: async () => ({ rpc, storage: { from: () => ({ remove }) } }),
}));

import { closeBriefAction, decideApplicationAction, deleteBriefAction, saveBriefAction } from "./actions";
import { todayTw } from "@/lib/briefs/dates";

const ID = "00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006f001";
const APP = "00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000006a001";

function form(entries: Record<string, string | string[]>): FormData {
  const f = new FormData();
  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(entries)) for (const x of [v].flat()) f.append(k, x);
  return f;
}

const full = {
  title: "春季保養開箱", description: "說明", collabTypes: "IG 貼文", includesProduct: "on", deadline: todayTw(),
};

beforeEach(() => {
  requireRole.mockReset().mockResolvedValue({ id: "brand-1" });
  rpc.mockReset();
  remove.mockReset().mockResolvedValue({ data: [], error: null });
  revalidatePath.mockReset();
  redirect.mockClear();
});

describe("saveBriefAction", () => {
  it("一定先檢查是品牌", async () => {
    await saveBriefAction({}, form({ intent: "bogus" }));
    expect(requireRole).toHaveBeenCalledWith("brand");
  });
  it("id 或 intent 格式不對時不碰資料庫", async () => {
    expect(await saveBriefAction({}, form({ intent: "draft", id: "x" }))).toEqual({ formError: "資料格式不正確，請重新整理頁面" });
    expect(await saveBriefAction({}, form({ intent: "post" }))).toEqual({ formError: "資料格式不正確，請重新整理頁面" });
    expect(rpc).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
  it("發佈時伺服器再完整驗證一次", async () => {
    const res = await saveBriefAction({}, form({ intent: "publish", title: "x" }));
    expect(res.fieldErrors).toMatchObject({ description: "請填寫合作說明", reward: "預算和產品至少要有一項" });
    expect(rpc).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
  it("存草稿：新增後導到編輯頁同一步", async () => {
    rpc.mockResolvedValue({ data: ID, error: null });
    await expect(saveBriefAction({}, form({ intent: "draft", step: "2", title: "只有案名" })))
      .rejects.toThrow(`REDIRECT /brand/briefs/${ID}/edit?step=2&saved=1`);
    expect(rpc).toHaveBeenCalledWith("save_brief", expect.objectContaining({ p_id: undefined, p_title: "只有案名", p_publish: false }));
    expect(revalidatePath).toHaveBeenCalledWith("/brand/briefs");
  });
  it("存既有的草稿：留在原頁、回傳 saved", async () => {
    rpc.mockResolvedValue({ data: ID, error: null });
    expect(await saveBriefAction({}, form({ intent: "draft", id: ID, step: "3", title: "改案名" }))).toEqual({ saved: true });
    expect(redirect).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(revalidatePath).toHaveBeenCalledWith(`/brand/briefs/${ID}`);
  });
  it("發佈：導到招募詳情", async () => {
    rpc.mockResolvedValue({ data: ID, error: null });
    await expect(saveBriefAction({}, form({ intent: "publish", id: ID, ...full })))
      .rejects.toThrow(`REDIRECT /brand/briefs/${ID}`);
    expect(rpc).toHaveBeenCalledWith("save_brief", expect.objectContaining({ p_id: ID, p_publish: true, p_includes_product: true }));
  });
  it("資料庫的欄位錯誤與其他錯誤", async () => {
    rpc.mockResolvedValue({ data: null, error: { code: "22023", message: "invalid deadline" } });
    expect(await saveBriefAction({}, form({ intent: "publish", ...full })))
      .toEqual({ fieldErrors: { deadline: "截止日要在今天到 90 天內" } });
    rpc.mockResolvedValue({ data: null, error: { code: "55000", message: "brief not editable" } });
    expect(await saveBriefAction({}, form({ intent: "draft", id: ID, title: "x" })))
      .toEqual({ formError: "這則招募已結束，不能再修改" });
  });
});

describe("closeBriefAction", () => {
  it("格式不對時不碰資料庫", async () => {
    expect(await closeBriefAction("x")).toEqual({ ok: false, error: "資料格式不正確，請重新整理頁面" });
    expect(rpc).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
  it("關閉並重新整理列表與詳情", async () => {
    rpc.mockResolvedValue({ data: null, error: null });
    expect(await closeBriefAction(ID)).toEqual({ ok: true });
    expect(requireRole).toHaveBeenCalledWith("brand");
    expect(rpc).toHaveBeenCalledWith("close_brief", { p_id: ID });
    expect(revalidatePath).toHaveBeenCalledWith(`/brand/briefs/${ID}`);
  });
  it("錯誤訊息", async () => {
    rpc.mockResolvedValue({ data: null, error: { code: "55000", message: "brief not open" } });
    expect(await closeBriefAction(ID)).toEqual({ ok: false, error: "這則招募已經不是發佈中" });
  });
});

describe("deleteBriefAction", () => {
  it("刪除草稿、刪封面檔、回到草稿列表", async () => {
    rpc.mockResolvedValue({ data: "brand-1/cover-a.jpg", error: null });
    await expect(deleteBriefAction(ID)).rejects.toThrow("REDIRECT /brand/briefs?tab=draft");
    expect(rpc).toHaveBeenCalledWith("delete_brief", { p_id: ID });
    expect(remove).toHaveBeenCalledWith(["brand-1/cover-a.jpg"]);
  });
  it("沒有封面時不刪檔；刪檔失敗也不影響", async () => {
    rpc.mockResolvedValue({ data: null, error: null });
    await expect(deleteBriefAction(ID)).rejects.toThrow("REDIRECT");
    expect(remove).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    rpc.mockResolvedValue({ data: "brand-1/cover-a.jpg", error: null });
    remove.mockRejectedValue(new Error("network"));
    await expect(deleteBriefAction(ID)).rejects.toThrow("REDIRECT");
  });
  it("不是草稿", async () => {
    rpc.mockResolvedValue({ data: null, error: { code: "55000", message: "brief not draft" } });
    expect(await deleteBriefAction(ID)).toEqual({ ok: false, error: "只有草稿可以刪除" });
  });
});

describe("decideApplicationAction", () => {
  it("參數格式不對時不碰資料庫", async () => {
    expect(await decideApplicationAction(APP, ID, "yes")).toMatchObject({ ok: false });
    expect(await decideApplicationAction("x", ID, true)).toMatchObject({ ok: false });
    expect(rpc).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
  it("錄取並重新整理詳情", async () => {
    rpc.mockResolvedValue({ data: null, error: null });
    expect(await decideApplicationAction(APP, ID, true)).toEqual({ ok: true });
    expect(rpc).toHaveBeenCalledWith("decide_application", { p_id: APP, p_accept: true });
    expect(revalidatePath).toHaveBeenCalledWith(`/brand/briefs/${ID}`);
  });
  it("已處理過", async () => {
    rpc.mockResolvedValue({ data: null, error: { code: "55000", message: "application not pending" } });
    expect(await decideApplicationAction(APP, ID, false)).toEqual({ ok: false, error: "這筆報名已經處理過了" });
  });
});
```

```ts
    expect(rpc).toHaveBeenCalledWith("apply_to_brief", { p_brief_id: BRIEF, p_message: "你好" });
```

`src/lib/briefs/brand.ts`：

```ts
import "server-only";
import { createClient } from "@/lib/supabase/server";
import {
  BRIEF_COLUMNS, toApplicant, toBrief, withCounts,
  type Applicant, type ApplicantRow, type BrandBriefListItem, type Brief, type BriefRow, type CountRow,
} from "./view";

// 品牌端讀取。全部用品牌自己的 client（RLS／definer 函式自己檢查身分）。會讀 cookie：只能在 requireRole 之後呼叫。不快取。

/** 自己的招募（最新的在前，最多 200 則）＋ 報名人數與待處理人數。 */
export async function loadMyBriefs(brandId: string): Promise<BrandBriefListItem[]> {
  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from("briefs").select(BRIEF_COLUMNS).eq("brand_id", brandId)
    .order("created_at", { ascending: false }).limit(200);
  if (error) throw error;
  const briefs = ((data ?? []) as unknown as BriefRow[]).map(toBrief);
  if (briefs.length === 0) return [];
  const { data: counts, error: e2 } = await supabase.rpc("brief_application_counts", { p_ids: briefs.map((b) => b.id) });
  if (e2) throw e2;
  return withCounts(briefs, (counts ?? []) as unknown as CountRow[]);
}

/** 自己的一則招募；不是自己的或不存在回傳 null。 */
export async function loadOwnBrief(brandId: string, id: string): Promise<Brief | null> {
  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from("briefs").select(BRIEF_COLUMNS).eq("id", id).eq("brand_id", brandId).maybeSingle();
  if (error) throw error;
  return data ? toBrief(data as unknown as BriefRow) : null;
}

/** 報名者（最新的在前）。只有招募主能呼叫（否則 42501 not brief owner）。 */
export async function loadApplicants(briefId: string): Promise<Applicant[]> {
  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("list_brief_applicants", { p_brief_id: briefId });
  if (error) throw error;
  return ((data ?? []) as unknown as ApplicantRow[]).map(toApplicant);
}
```

`src/lib/briefs/creator.ts`：

```ts
import "server-only";
import { createClient } from "@/lib/supabase/server";
import { toListArgs, type OpportunityQuery } from "./query";
import {
  MY_APPLICATION_COLUMNS, toMyApplications, toOpportunityDetail, toOpportunityPage,
  type MyApplication, type MyApplicationRow, type OpportunityDetail, type OpportunityDetailRow,
  type OpportunityPage, type OpportunityRow,
} from "./view";

// 創作者端讀取。用創作者自己的 client（definer 函式只給創作者）。會讀 cookie：只能在 requireRole 之後呼叫。不快取。

export async function listOpportunities(query: OpportunityQuery, offset = 0): Promise<OpportunityPage> {
  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("list_opportunities", toListArgs(query, offset));
  if (error) throw error;
  return toOpportunityPage((data ?? []) as unknown as OpportunityRow[], offset);
}

/** 看不到（草稿、別人的、已截止且沒報名過）回傳 null。 */
export async function loadOpportunity(id: string): Promise<OpportunityDetail | null> {
  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("get_opportunity", { p_id: id });
  if (error) throw error;
  const row = ((data ?? []) as unknown as OpportunityDetailRow[])[0];
  return row ? toOpportunityDetail(row) : null;
}

/** 我的報名（最新的在前，最多 100 筆）。 */
export async function loadMyApplications(creatorId: string): Promise<MyApplication[]> {
  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from("brief_applications").select(MY_APPLICATION_COLUMNS).eq("creator_id", creatorId)
    .order("created_at", { ascending: false }).limit(100);
  if (error) throw error;
  return toMyApplications((data ?? []) as unknown as MyApplicationRow[]);
}
```

`src/app/brand/briefs/actions.ts`：

```ts
"use server";

import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { requireRole } from "@/lib/auth/current-user";
import { createClient } from "@/lib/supabase/server";
import { BRIEF_COVER_BUCKET } from "@/lib/brief-cover";
import { todayTw } from "@/lib/briefs/dates";
import { briefErrorMessage, briefFieldError } from "@/lib/briefs/errors";
import { isUuid, parseBriefStep } from "@/lib/briefs/query";
import { parseBriefForm, toSaveBriefArgs, validateBrief } from "@/lib/validation/brief";

const BAD_INPUT = "資料格式不正確，請重新整理頁面";

export type BriefFormState = { fieldErrors?: Record<string, string>; formError?: string; saved?: boolean };
export type ActionResult = { ok: true } | { ok: false; error: string };

function str(fd: FormData, key: string): string {
  const v = fd.get(key);
  return typeof v === "string" ? v : "";
}

/**
 * 存成草稿或發佈（intent = draft | publish）。id 空白代表新增。
 * 發佈 → redirect 到招募詳情。新增的草稿 → redirect 到編輯頁同一步（之後重新整理會從資料庫讀回來）；
 * 已存在的草稿 → 留在原頁，回傳 { saved: true } 顯示「已存成草稿」。
 */
export async function saveBriefAction(_prev: BriefFormState, fd: FormData): Promise<BriefFormState> {
  await requireRole("brand");
  const id = str(fd, "id");
  const intent = fd.get("intent");
  if ((id !== "" && !isUuid(id)) || (intent !== "draft" && intent !== "publish")) return { formError: BAD_INPUT };
  const step = parseBriefStep(str(fd, "step"));
  const input = parseBriefForm(fd);
  const fieldErrors = validateBrief(input, intent, todayTw());
  if (fieldErrors) return { fieldErrors };

  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("save_brief", toSaveBriefArgs(id || null, input, intent === "publish"));
  if (error) {
    const fe = briefFieldError(error);
    return fe ? { fieldErrors: fe } : { formError: briefErrorMessage(error) };
  }
  const savedId = data as unknown as string;
  revalidatePath("/brand/briefs");
  if (intent === "publish") redirect(`/brand/briefs/${savedId}`);
  if (!id) redirect(`/brand/briefs/${savedId}/edit?step=${step}&saved=1`);
  revalidatePath(`/brand/briefs/${savedId}`);
  return { saved: true };
}

/** 關閉發佈中的招募。 */
export async function closeBriefAction(id: unknown): Promise<ActionResult> {
  await requireRole("brand");
  if (!isUuid(id)) return { ok: false, error: BAD_INPUT };
  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { error } = await supabase.rpc("close_brief", { p_id: id });
  if (error) return { ok: false, error: briefErrorMessage(error) };
  revalidatePath("/brand/briefs");
  revalidatePath(`/brand/briefs/${id}`);
  return { ok: true };
}

/** 刪除草稿，並盡力刪除封面檔（失敗就留給孤兒檔排程）；成功後回到列表。 */
export async function deleteBriefAction(id: unknown): Promise<ActionResult> {
  await requireRole("brand");
  if (!isUuid(id)) return { ok: false, error: BAD_INPUT };
  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("delete_brief", { p_id: id });
  if (error) return { ok: false, error: briefErrorMessage(error) };
  const cover = data as unknown as string | null;
  if (cover) {
    try {
      await supabase.storage.from(BRIEF_COVER_BUCKET).remove([cover]);
    } catch {
      // 忽略
    }
  }
  revalidatePath("/brand/briefs");
  redirect("/brand/briefs?tab=draft");
}

/** 錄取（accept = true）或婉拒。招募關閉或截止後也可以。 */
export async function decideApplicationAction(applicationId: unknown, briefId: unknown, accept: unknown): Promise<ActionResult> {
  await requireRole("brand");
  if (!isUuid(applicationId) || !isUuid(briefId) || typeof accept !== "boolean") return { ok: false, error: BAD_INPUT };
  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { error } = await supabase.rpc("decide_application", { p_id: applicationId, p_accept: accept });
  if (error) return { ok: false, error: briefErrorMessage(error) };
  revalidatePath(`/brand/briefs/${briefId}`);
  revalidatePath("/brand/briefs");
  return { ok: true };
}
```

`src/app/creator/actions.ts`：

```ts
"use server";

import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";
import { requireRole } from "@/lib/auth/current-user";
import { createClient } from "@/lib/supabase/server";
import { listOpportunities } from "@/lib/briefs/creator";
import { briefErrorMessage, briefFieldError } from "@/lib/briefs/errors";
import { isUuid, parseOpportunityQuery } from "@/lib/briefs/query";
import type { Opportunity } from "@/lib/briefs/view";
import { parseApplicationForm, validateApplication } from "@/lib/validation/brief";

const BAD_INPUT = "資料格式不正確，請重新整理頁面";
const MAX_OFFSET = 10_000;
const MAX_QUERY_LENGTH = 500;

export type LoadMoreOpportunitiesResult = { ok: true; items: Opportunity[]; hasMore: boolean } | { ok: false; error: string };
export type ApplyFormState = { ok?: boolean; fieldErrors?: Record<string, string>; formError?: string };
export type ActionResult = { ok: true } | { ok: false; error: string };

/** 「載入更多」：query 是目前網址的查詢字串，在伺服器重新解析，不信任 client 傳來的物件。 */
export async function loadMoreOpportunitiesAction(query: unknown, offset: unknown): Promise<LoadMoreOpportunitiesResult> {
  await requireRole("creator");
  if (
    typeof query !== "string" || query.length > MAX_QUERY_LENGTH
    || typeof offset !== "number" || !Number.isInteger(offset) || offset < 0 || offset > MAX_OFFSET
  ) {
    return { ok: false, error: BAD_INPUT };
  }
  const q = parseOpportunityQuery(new URLSearchParams(query));
  if (q.tab === "applied") return { ok: false, error: BAD_INPUT };
  try {
    const page = await listOpportunities(q, offset);
    return { ok: true, items: page.items, hasMore: page.hasMore };
  } catch {
    return { ok: false, error: "載入失敗，請稍後再試" };
  }
}

/** 報名。未審核在這裡先擋（資料庫也會擋）。 */
export async function applyToBriefAction(_prev: ApplyFormState, fd: FormData): Promise<ApplyFormState> {
  const me = await requireRole("creator");
  if (me.creator?.status !== "approved") return { formError: "審核通過後即可報名" };
  const input = parseApplicationForm(fd);
  if (!isUuid(input.briefId)) return { formError: BAD_INPUT };
  const fieldErrors = validateApplication(input);
  if (fieldErrors) return { fieldErrors };

  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { error } = await supabase.rpc("apply_to_brief", {
    p_brief_id: input.briefId,
    p_message: input.message,
    ...(input.proposedPrice === "" ? {} : { p_proposed_price: Number(input.proposedPrice) }),
  });
  if (error) {
    const fe = briefFieldError(error);
    return fe ? { fieldErrors: fe } : { formError: briefErrorMessage(error) };
  }
  revalidatePath(`/creator/briefs/${input.briefId}`);
  revalidatePath("/creator");
  return { ok: true };
}

/** 撤回待回覆的報名（撤回後不能再報名同一則）。 */
export async function withdrawApplicationAction(applicationId: unknown, briefId: unknown): Promise<ActionResult> {
  await requireRole("creator");
  if (!isUuid(applicationId) || !isUuid(briefId)) return { ok: false, error: BAD_INPUT };
  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { error } = await supabase.rpc("withdraw_application", { p_id: applicationId });
  if (error) return { ok: false, error: briefErrorMessage(error) };
  revalidatePath(`/creator/briefs/${briefId}`);
  revalidatePath("/creator");
  return { ok: true };
}
```

```tsx
import {
  BRAND_APPLICATION_LABEL, BRIEF_STATUS_LABEL, CREATOR_APPLICATION_LABEL,
  type ApplicationStatus, type BriefDisplayStatus,
} from "@/lib/briefs/view";

const base = "inline-flex shrink-0 items-center whitespace-nowrap rounded-full px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold";

const BRIEF_TONE: Record<BriefDisplayStatus, string> = {
  draft: "border border-line bg-surface text-ink",
  open: "bg-success-soft text-success",
  expired: "bg-primary-soft text-muted",
  closed: "bg-primary-soft text-muted",
};

const APPLICATION_TONE: Record<ApplicationStatus, string> = {
  applied: "border border-line bg-surface text-ink",
  accepted: "bg-success-soft text-success",
  rejected: "bg-primary-soft text-muted",
  withdrawn: "bg-primary-soft text-muted",
};

export function BriefStatusBadge({ status }: { status: BriefDisplayStatus }) {
  return <span className={`${base} ${BRIEF_TONE[status]}`}>{BRIEF_STATUS_LABEL[status]}</span>;
}

export function ApplicationStatusBadge({ status, viewer }: { status: ApplicationStatus; viewer: "brand" | "creator" }) {
  const label = (viewer === "brand" ? BRAND_APPLICATION_LABEL : CREATOR_APPLICATION_LABEL)[status];
  return <span className={`${base} ${APPLICATION_TONE[status]}`}>{label}</span>;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 列表頁 `src/app/brand/briefs/page.tsx`**

```tsx
import { Suspense } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { Plus } from "lucide-react";
import { BriefStatusBadge } from "@/components/briefs/StatusBadges";
import { requireRole } from "@/lib/auth/current-user";
import { loadMyBriefs } from "@/lib/briefs/brand";
import { deadlineLabel, formatDate, formatDateTw, todayTw } from "@/lib/briefs/dates";
import { BRAND_BRIEF_TABS, parseBrandBriefTab } from "@/lib/briefs/query";
import { brandTabOf, displayStatusOf, UNTITLED_DRAFT } from "@/lib/briefs/view";
import { formatNumber } from "@/lib/format";

type Props = { searchParams: Promise<Record<string, string | string[] | undefined>> };

// 頁面殼是靜態的；searchParams 與 session 都在 <Suspense> 內讀（cacheComponents）
export default function BrandBriefsPage({ searchParams }: Props) {
  return (
    <div>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <h1 className="text-[32px] font-bold text-ink">我的招募</h1>
        <Link href="/brand/briefs/new" className="inline-flex h-11 items-center gap-1 rounded-btn bg-ink px-4 font-semibold text-on-ink">
          <Plus size={18} strokeWidth={1.5} aria-hidden="true" />刊登招募
        </Link>
      </div>
      <div className="mt-6">
        <Suspense fallback={<ListSkeleton />}>
          <BriefList searchParams={searchParams} />
        </Suspense>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

async function BriefList({ searchParams }: Props) {
  // layout 的 Gate 在用戶端換頁時不會重跑，頁面自己也要檢查
  const me = await requireRole("brand");
  const tab = parseBrandBriefTab((await searchParams).tab);
  const today = todayTw();
  const all = (await loadMyBriefs(me.id)).map((b) => ({ ...b, display: displayStatusOf(b.status, b.deadline, today) }));

  if (all.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-card border border-line bg-surface p-8 text-center">
        <p className="font-semibold text-ink">還沒有招募，刊登第一則吧</p>
        <Link href="/brand/briefs/new" className="mt-4 inline-flex h-10 items-center rounded-btn bg-ink px-4 text-sm font-semibold text-on-ink">
          刊登招募
        </Link>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const rows = all.filter((b) => brandTabOf(b.display) === tab);
  return (
    <div>
      <nav aria-label="招募狀態" className="flex gap-2">
        {BRAND_BRIEF_TABS.map((t) => {
          const count = all.filter((b) => brandTabOf(b.display) === t.value).length;
          const active = t.value === tab;
          return (
            <Link
              key={t.value} href={t.value === "open" ? "/brand/briefs" : `/brand/briefs?tab=${t.value}`}
              aria-current={active ? "page" : undefined}
              className={`h-9 rounded-full border px-4 text-sm font-semibold leading-9 ${active ? "border-ink bg-ink text-on-ink" : "border-line bg-surface text-ink"}`}
            >
              {t.label} {count}
            </Link>
          );
        })}
      </nav>
      {rows.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="mt-6 rounded-card border border-line bg-surface p-8 text-center text-sm text-muted">這裡還沒有招募</p>
      ) : (
        <ul className="mt-4 divide-y divide-line overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-surface">
          {rows.map((b) => (
            <li key={b.id}>
              <Link href={`/brand/briefs/${b.id}`} className="block p-4 hover:bg-primary-soft focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-inset focus-visible:ring-ink md:p-5">
                <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                  <p className="min-w-0 break-words font-semibold text-ink">{b.title || UNTITLED_DRAFT}</p>
                  <BriefStatusBadge status={b.display} />
                </div>
                <p className="mt-1 flex flex-wrap gap-x-4 gap-y-1 text-sm text-muted">
                  <span>{b.deadline ? `截止 ${formatDate(b.deadline)}（${deadlineLabel(b.deadline, today)}）` : "未設定截止日"}</span>
                  {b.status !== "draft" && (
                    <span>報名 {formatNumber(b.applicantCount)} 人（待處理 {formatNumber(b.pendingCount)}）</span>
                  )}
                  <span>建立於 {formatDateTw(b.createdAt)}</span>
                </p>
              </Link>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function ListSkeleton() {
  return (
    <div aria-busy="true" className="space-y-3">
      <p className="sr-only">載入中…</p>
      {[0, 1, 2].map((i) => <div key={i} className="h-20 rounded-card border border-line bg-surface" />)}
    </div>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: 品牌標誌與封面**

`src/components/briefs/BrandMark.tsx`：

```tsx
import { brandLogoUrl } from "@/lib/brand-logo";
import { initialOf } from "@/lib/format";

const SIZE = { sm: "h-8 w-8 text-sm", md: "h-10 w-10 text-base" } as const;

/** 品牌 Logo；沒有時顯示品牌名稱第一個字。 */
export function BrandMark({ name, logoPath, size }: { name: string; logoPath: string | null; size: keyof typeof SIZE }) {
  const url = brandLogoUrl(logoPath);
  if (url) {
    // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
    return <img src={url} alt="" className={`${SIZE[size]} shrink-0 rounded-btn border border-line bg-surface object-contain`} />;
  }
  return (
    <div aria-hidden="true" className={`${SIZE[size]} flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-btn bg-ink font-semibold text-on-ink`}>
      {initialOf(name)}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`src/components/briefs/BriefCover.tsx`：

```tsx
import { brandLogoUrl } from "@/lib/brand-logo";
import { briefCoverUrl } from "@/lib/brief-cover";
import { initialOf } from "@/lib/format";

/** 封面圖（2:1）；沒有封面時顯示品牌 Logo，再沒有就顯示品牌名稱第一個字。 */
export function BriefCover({ coverPath, brandName, brandLogoPath, className = "" }: {
  coverPath: string | null; brandName: string; brandLogoPath: string | null; className?: string;
}) {
  const cover = briefCoverUrl(coverPath);
  const logo = brandLogoUrl(brandLogoPath);
  return (
    <div className={`relative aspect-[2/1] overflow-hidden bg-primary-soft ${className}`}>
      {cover ? (
        // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
        <img src={cover} alt="" loading="lazy" className="absolute inset-0 h-full w-full object-cover" />
      ) : logo ? (
        // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
        <img src={logo} alt="" loading="lazy" className="absolute inset-0 m-auto h-1/2 w-1/2 object-contain" />
      ) : (
        <div aria-hidden="true" className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center text-4xl font-semibold text-muted">
          {initialOf(brandName)}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { Check, X } from "lucide-react";
import { deadlineLabel, formatDate } from "@/lib/briefs/dates";
import { audienceLabel, matchItems, rewardLabel, type BriefDetailData, type MatchFlags } from "@/lib/briefs/view";
import { formatNumber } from "@/lib/format";
import { BrandMark } from "./BrandMark";
import { BriefCover } from "./BriefCover";

type Props = {
  brief: BriefDetailData;
  today: string;
  /** 創作者看時帶入：條件逐項顯示符合或不符合。品牌預覽時省略。 */
  match?: MatchFlags;
  /** 右欄（桌面）／底部（手機）：報名區塊。 */
  aside?: React.ReactNode;
};

/** 招募詳情（創作者看到的樣子）。品牌表單第 4 步的預覽也用這個元件，所以不能用 hook。 */
export function BriefDetailView({ brief: b, today, match, aside }: Props) {
  return (
    <div className={aside ? "grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_320px] md:items-start md:gap-6" : ""}>
      <article className="min-w-0 overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-surface">
        <BriefCover coverPath={b.coverPath} brandName={b.brandName} brandLogoPath={b.brandLogoPath} />
        <div className="space-y-6 p-5 md:p-8">
          <div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <BrandMark name={b.brandName} logoPath={b.brandLogoPath} size="sm" />
              <p className="min-w-0 truncate text-sm font-semibold text-ink">{b.brandName}</p>
            </div>
            <h1 className="mt-3 break-words text-2xl font-bold text-ink md:text-[28px]">{b.title}</h1>
            <p className="mt-2 text-xl font-bold text-primary">{rewardLabel(b)}</p>
            {b.collabTypes.length > 0 && (
              <ul aria-label="合作形式" className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                {b.collabTypes.map((t) => (
                  <li key={t} className="rounded-full border border-line px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-ink">{t}</li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            )}
          </div>
          <dl className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 text-sm">
            <div>
              <dt className="text-xs text-muted">截止日</dt>
              <dd className="mt-0.5 font-semibold text-ink">
                {b.deadline ? `${formatDate(b.deadline)}（${deadlineLabel(b.deadline, today)}）` : "未設定"}
              </dd>
            </div>
            {b.applicantCount !== null && (
              <div>
                <dt className="text-xs text-muted">已報名</dt>
                <dd className="mt-0.5 font-semibold text-ink">{formatNumber(b.applicantCount)} 人</dd>
              </div>
            )}
          </dl>
          <section>
            <h2 className="text-sm font-bold text-ink">合作說明</h2>
            <p className="mt-2 whitespace-pre-line break-words text-sm leading-relaxed text-ink">{b.description || "—"}</p>
          </section>
          <section>
            <h2 className="text-sm font-bold text-ink">創作者條件</h2>
            {match ? (
              <ul className="mt-2 space-y-1.5 text-sm">
                {matchItems({ ...b, match }).map((it) => (
                  <li key={it.label} className="flex items-start gap-2">
                    {it.ok
                      ? <Check size={16} strokeWidth={1.5} aria-hidden="true" className="mt-0.5 shrink-0 text-success" />
                      : <X size={16} strokeWidth={1.5} aria-hidden="true" className="mt-0.5 shrink-0 text-danger" />}
                    <span className="min-w-0 break-words text-ink">{it.label}</span>
                    <span className="sr-only">{it.ok ? "（符合）" : "（不符合）"}</span>
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            ) : (
              <ul className="mt-2 space-y-1.5 text-sm text-ink">
                <li>{audienceLabel(b.minFollowers)}</li>
                <li>{b.region ? `地區：${b.region}` : "地區不限"}</li>
                <li>{b.categories.length ? `內容領域：${b.categories.join("、")}` : "內容領域不限"}</li>
              </ul>
            )}
          </section>
        </div>
      </article>
      {aside}
    </div>
  );
}
```
