- [ ] **Step 3: 表單元件**

`src/components/forms/ChipMultiSelect.tsx`：

```tsx
"use client";

import { useId } from "react";

type Props = {
  legend: string;
  /** 表單欄位名稱：每個選到的值輸出一個隱藏欄位 */
  name: string;
  options: readonly string[];
  value: string[];
  onChange: (next: string[]) => void;
  max: number;
  error?: string;
  hint?: string;
};

/** 受控的多選標籤。清單以外的既有值（資料庫裡的舊值）也會顯示，可以取消。 */
export function ChipMultiSelect({ legend, name, options, value, onChange, max, error, hint }: Props) {
  const noteId = useId();
  const all = [...options, ...value.filter((v) => !options.includes(v))];
  function toggle(c: string) {
    if (value.includes(c)) onChange(value.filter((x) => x !== c));
    else if (value.length < max) onChange([...value, c]);
  }
  const note = error ?? hint;
  return (
    <fieldset aria-describedby={note ? noteId : undefined}>
      <legend className="text-sm font-semibold text-ink">{legend}</legend>
      <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
        {all.map((c) => {
          const on = value.includes(c);
          return (
            <button
              key={c} type="button" onClick={() => toggle(c)} aria-pressed={on}
              className={`min-h-9 rounded-full border px-3 py-1 text-sm font-medium ${on ? "border-ink bg-ink text-on-ink" : "border-line bg-surface text-ink"}`}
            >
              {c}
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>
      {value.map((c) => <input key={c} type="hidden" name={name} value={c} />)}
      {note && <span id={noteId} className={`mt-1 block text-xs ${error ? "text-danger" : "text-muted"}`}>{note}</span>}
    </fieldset>
  );
}
```

`src/components/forms/CoverField.tsx`：

```tsx
"use client";

import { useId, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { BriefCover } from "@/components/briefs/BriefCover";
import { createClient } from "@/lib/supabase/client";
import { BRIEF_COVER_BUCKET, planCoverUpload } from "@/lib/brief-cover";

type Props = {
  userId: string;
  value: string | null;
  onChange: (path: string | null) => void;
  brandName: string;
  brandLogoPath: string | null;
  error?: string;
  onBusyChange?: (busy: boolean) => void;
  disabled?: boolean;
};

/**
 * 招募封面（選填）。上傳到 brief-covers/<uid>/cover-<uuid>.<副檔名>；沒有封面時預覽顯示品牌 Logo。
 * 只刪除這次畫面上傳、後來被換掉的檔案；已存進資料庫的舊封面不刪（孤兒檔由排程清理，見 followups）。
 */
export function CoverField({ userId, value, onChange, brandName, brandLogoPath, error, onBusyChange, disabled }: Props) {
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const ownedRef = useRef<string | null>(null);
  const noteId = useId();

  function setBusyState(b: boolean) {
    setBusy(b);
    onBusyChange?.(b);
  }

  function discard(p: string | null) {
    if (disabled || !p || p !== ownedRef.current) return;
    ownedRef.current = null;
    void Promise.resolve(createClient().storage.from(BRIEF_COVER_BUCKET).remove([p])).catch(() => {});
  }

  async function onFile(file: File | undefined) {
    if (!file) return;
    setMsg(null);
    const plan = planCoverUpload(userId, file, crypto.randomUUID());
    if ("error" in plan) return setMsg(plan.error);
    setBusyState(true);
    try {
      const { error: e } = await createClient().storage.from(BRIEF_COVER_BUCKET)
        .upload(plan.path, file, { contentType: file.type });
      if (e) return setMsg("上傳失敗，請再試一次");
      discard(ownedRef.current);
      ownedRef.current = plan.path;
      onChange(plan.path);
    } catch {
      setMsg("上傳失敗，請再試一次");
    } finally {
      setBusyState(false);
    }
  }

  function remove() {
    if (disabled) return;
    discard(value);
    onChange(null);
    setMsg(null);
  }

  const note = msg ?? error;
  return (
    <div>
      <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-ink">封面圖（選填）</span>
      <div className="mt-1 max-w-sm overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line">
        <BriefCover coverPath={value} brandName={brandName} brandLogoPath={brandLogoPath} />
      </div>
      <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
        <input
          type="file" className="min-w-0 flex-1" accept="image/png,image/jpeg,image/webp" disabled={busy || disabled}
          aria-label="上傳封面圖" aria-describedby={note ? noteId : undefined} aria-invalid={note ? true : undefined}
          onChange={(e) => {
            const file = e.target.files?.[0];
            e.target.value = "";
            void onFile(file);
          }}
        />
        {value && <button type="button" disabled={busy || disabled} className="text-sm text-muted" onClick={remove}>移除封面</button>}
      </div>
      <input type="hidden" name="coverPath" value={value ?? ""} />
      {note && <span id={noteId} role={msg ? "status" : undefined} className="mt-1 block text-xs text-danger">{note}</span>}
      <span className="mt-1 block text-xs text-muted">JPG、PNG、WebP，5MB 以內；沒有封面時顯示品牌 Logo</span>
    </div>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 四步驟表單 `src/components/briefs/BriefForm.tsx`**

重點：四步的欄位都在同一個 `<form>`（非目前步驟 `hidden`）；`intent` 由送出按鈕的 `name`／`value` 帶入（React 19 的 form action 會把 submitter 放進 FormData）；伺服器回傳的 state 改變時，在 render 中比對前一次的 state 來跳步驟與顯示「已存成草稿」（不用 effect）；步驟用 `replaceState` 寫進網址。

```tsx
"use client";

import { useActionState, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { saveBriefAction, type BriefFormState } from "@/app/brand/briefs/actions";
import { ChipMultiSelect } from "@/components/forms/ChipMultiSelect";
import { CoverField } from "@/components/forms/CoverField";
import { Field, inputClass } from "@/components/forms/Field";
import { addDays, MAX_DEADLINE_DAYS } from "@/lib/briefs/dates";
import { previewFromInput } from "@/lib/briefs/view";
import { CATEGORIES, COLLAB_TYPES, REGIONS } from "@/lib/options";
import { firstErrorStep, validateBrief, validateBriefStep, type BriefInput, type BriefStep } from "@/lib/validation/brief";
import { BriefDetailView } from "./BriefDetailView";

const STEPS: { step: BriefStep; label: string }[] = [
  { step: 1, label: "合作內容" },
  { step: 2, label: "創作者條件" },
  { step: 3, label: "酬勞與期限" },
  { step: 4, label: "預覽並發佈" },
];

type Props = {
  /** 新增時是 null */
  briefId: string | null;
  /** 發佈中的招募不能存成草稿，只能「儲存變更」 */
  isOpen: boolean;
  initial: BriefInput;
  initialStep: BriefStep;
  /** 剛存成草稿（網址有 saved=1） */
  saved: boolean;
  userId: string;
  brandName: string;
  brandLogoPath: string | null;
  /** 台灣今天（伺服器算好傳進來，避免 hydration 不一致） */
  today: string;
};

/**
 * 刊登／編輯招募的四步驟表單。四步的欄位都在同一個 <form> 裡（非目前步驟用 hidden 隱藏），
 * 所以送出時 FormData 含全部欄位。步驟記在網址 ?step=（replaceState，不重新渲染伺服器端）。
 * 未儲存的內容只在畫面上；新招募存成草稿後由 Server Action redirect 到編輯頁，重新整理會從資料庫讀回來。
 */
export function BriefForm({ briefId, isOpen, initial, initialStep, saved, userId, brandName, brandLogoPath, today }: Props) {
  const [values, setValues] = useState<BriefInput>(initial);
  const [step, setStep] = useState<BriefStep>(initialStep);
  const [clientErrors, setClientErrors] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [showSaved, setShowSaved] = useState(saved);
  const [coverBusy, setCoverBusy] = useState(false);
  const [state, formAction, pending] = useActionState<BriefFormState, FormData>(saveBriefAction, {});

  // 伺服器回傳欄位錯誤時，跳到有錯誤的第一步（在 render 中依前一次的 state 調整，不用 effect）
  const [seen, setSeen] = useState(state);
  if (state !== seen) {
    setSeen(state);
    setClientErrors({});
    setShowSaved(state.saved === true);
    if (state.fieldErrors) setStep(firstErrorStep(state.fieldErrors));
  }

  // 步驟寫進網址：重新整理後停在同一步。用 replaceState，不產生歷史紀錄，也不觸發伺服器重新渲染。
  useEffect(() => {
    const url = new URL(window.location.href);
    url.searchParams.set("step", String(step));
    url.searchParams.delete("saved");
    window.history.replaceState(null, "", url);
  }, [step]);

  const errors: Record<string, string> = { ...(state.fieldErrors ?? {}), ...clientErrors };
  const set = <K extends keyof BriefInput>(key: K, v: BriefInput[K]) => setValues((s) => ({ ...s, [key]: v }));

  function goTo(next: BriefStep) {
    setClientErrors({});
    setShowSaved(false);
    setStep(next);
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0 });
  }

  function next() {
    const e = validateBriefStep(step, values, today);
    if (e) return setClientErrors(e);
    goTo((step + 1) as BriefStep);
  }

  /** 送出前在前端先檢查；有錯就不送，跳到有錯的那一步。 */
  function guard(mode: "draft" | "publish") {
    return (ev: React.MouseEvent<HTMLButtonElement>) => {
      const e = validateBrief(values, mode, today);
      if (!e) return;
      ev.preventDefault();
      setClientErrors(e);
      setStep(firstErrorStep(e));
    };
  }

  const busy = pending || coverBusy;
  const descLen = [...values.description].length;
  const regionOptions = values.region && !(REGIONS as readonly string[]).includes(values.region)
    ? [...REGIONS, values.region] : REGIONS;

  return (
    <form action={formAction} noValidate className="space-y-6">
      <input type="hidden" name="id" value={briefId ?? ""} />
      <input type="hidden" name="step" value={step} />

      <ol className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-2" aria-label="步驟">
        {STEPS.map((s) => (
          <li
            key={s.step} aria-current={s.step === step ? "step" : undefined}
            className={`rounded-btn border px-2 py-2 text-center text-xs font-semibold ${s.step === step ? "border-ink bg-ink text-on-ink" : s.step < step ? "border-line bg-surface text-ink" : "border-line text-muted"}`}
          >
            <span className="block">{s.step}</span>
            <span className="hidden sm:block">{s.label}</span>
            <span className="sr-only sm:hidden">{s.label}</span>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ol>

      {showSaved && <p role="status" className="text-sm text-success">已存成草稿</p>}

      <section hidden={step !== 1} className="space-y-4 rounded-card border border-line bg-surface p-5 md:p-8">
        <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-ink">合作內容</h2>
        <Field label="案名" error={errors.title} hint="1–60 字">
          {(c) => <input {...c} name="title" value={values.title} onChange={(e) => set("title", e.target.value)} className={inputClass} />}
        </Field>
        <Field label="合作說明" error={errors.description} hint={`${descLen}/2000 字`}>
          {(c) => (
            <textarea
              {...c} name="description" rows={8} value={values.description}
              onChange={(e) => set("description", e.target.value)}
              className="w-full rounded-input border border-line bg-surface px-3 py-2 outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-ink"
            />
          )}
        </Field>
        <ChipMultiSelect
          legend="合作形式（至少 1 個）" name="collabTypes" options={COLLAB_TYPES} max={6}
          value={values.collabTypes} onChange={(v) => set("collabTypes", v)} error={errors.collabTypes}
        />
        <CoverField
          userId={userId} value={values.coverPath} onChange={(v) => set("coverPath", v)} brandName={brandName}
          brandLogoPath={brandLogoPath} error={errors.coverPath} onBusyChange={setCoverBusy} disabled={pending}
        />
      </section>

      <section hidden={step !== 2} className="space-y-4 rounded-card border border-line bg-surface p-5 md:p-8">
        <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-ink">創作者條件</h2>
        <ChipMultiSelect
          legend="內容領域（最多 5 個）" name="categories" options={CATEGORIES} max={5} hint="不選代表不限"
          value={values.categories} onChange={(v) => set("categories", v)} error={errors.categories}
        />
        <Field label="最低粉絲數" error={errors.minFollowers} hint="0 或空白代表不限">
          {(c) => (
            <input
              {...c} name="minFollowers" inputMode="numeric" placeholder="例如 1000" value={values.minFollowers}
              onChange={(e) => set("minFollowers", e.target.value)} className={inputClass}
            />
          )}
        </Field>
        <Field label="地區" error={errors.region}>
          {(c) => (
            <select {...c} name="region" value={values.region} onChange={(e) => set("region", e.target.value)} className={inputClass}>
              <option value="">不限</option>
              {regionOptions.map((r) => <option key={r} value={r}>{r}</option>)}
            </select>
          )}
        </Field>
      </section>

      <section hidden={step !== 3} className="space-y-4 rounded-card border border-line bg-surface p-5 md:p-8">
        <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-ink">酬勞與期限</h2>
        <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
          <Field label="預算下限（NT$，選填）" error={errors.budgetMin}>
            {(c) => (
              <input
                {...c} name="budgetMin" inputMode="numeric" placeholder="例如 5000" value={values.budgetMin}
                onChange={(e) => set("budgetMin", e.target.value)} className={inputClass}
              />
            )}
          </Field>
          <Field label="預算上限（NT$，選填）" error={errors.budgetMax}>
            {(c) => (
              <input
                {...c} name="budgetMax" inputMode="numeric" placeholder="例如 20000" value={values.budgetMax}
                onChange={(e) => set("budgetMax", e.target.value)} className={inputClass}
              />
            )}
          </Field>
        </div>
        <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold text-ink">
          <input
            type="checkbox" name="includesProduct" checked={values.includesProduct}
            onChange={(e) => set("includesProduct", e.target.checked)} className="h-4 w-4 accent-ink"
          />
          提供產品（互惠）
        </label>
        {errors.reward && <p className="text-xs text-danger">{errors.reward}</p>}
        <Field label="截止日" error={errors.deadline} hint={`今天到 ${MAX_DEADLINE_DAYS} 天內；截止日當天（台灣時間）仍可報名`}>
          {(c) => (
            <input
              {...c} type="date" name="deadline" min={today} max={addDays(today, MAX_DEADLINE_DAYS)}
              value={values.deadline} onChange={(e) => set("deadline", e.target.value)} className={inputClass}
            />
          )}
        </Field>
      </section>

      <section hidden={step !== 4} className="space-y-4">
        <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-ink">預覽（創作者看到的樣子）</h2>
        <BriefDetailView brief={previewFromInput(values, { name: brandName, logoPath: brandLogoPath })} today={today} />
      </section>

      {state.formError && <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-danger">{state.formError}</p>}

      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        {step > 1 && (
          <button type="button" onClick={() => goTo((step - 1) as BriefStep)} disabled={busy}
            className="h-11 rounded-btn border border-line bg-surface px-5 font-semibold text-ink">
            上一步
          </button>
        )}
        {!isOpen && (
          <button type="submit" name="intent" value="draft" formNoValidate onClick={guard("draft")} disabled={busy}
            className="h-11 rounded-btn border border-line bg-surface px-5 font-semibold text-ink disabled:opacity-60">
            存成草稿
          </button>
        )}
        <div className="ml-auto">
          {step < 4 ? (
            <button type="button" onClick={next} disabled={busy}
              className="h-11 rounded-btn bg-ink px-6 font-semibold text-on-ink disabled:opacity-60">
              下一步
            </button>
          ) : (
            <button type="submit" name="intent" value="publish" onClick={guard("publish")} disabled={busy}
              className="h-11 rounded-btn bg-ink px-6 font-semibold text-on-ink disabled:opacity-60">
              {pending ? "處理中…" : isOpen ? "儲存變更" : "發佈"}
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { Suspense } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { BriefForm } from "@/components/briefs/BriefForm";
import { requireRole } from "@/lib/auth/current-user";
import { todayTw } from "@/lib/briefs/dates";

import { EMPTY_BRIEF_INPUT } from "@/lib/validation/brief";

type Props = { searchParams: Promise<Record<string, string | string[] | undefined>> };

export default function NewBriefPage({ searchParams }: Props) {

    <div className="max-w-3xl">
      <Link href="/brand/briefs" className="text-sm text-muted">← 我的招募</Link>
      <h1 className="mt-2 text-[32px] font-bold text-ink">刊登招募</h1>
      <div className="mt-6">

          <NewBrief searchParams={searchParams} />
        </Suspense>
      </div>

}

async function NewBrief({ searchParams }: Props) {
  const me = await requireRole("brand");
  const step = parseBriefStep((await searchParams).step);
  return (
    <BriefForm
      briefId={null} isOpen={false} initial={EMPTY_BRIEF_INPUT} initialStep={step} saved={false}
      userId={me.id} brandName={me.brand?.brandName ?? ""} brandLogoPath={me.brand?.logoPath ?? null} today={todayTw()}
    />
  );
}
```

`src/app/brand/briefs/[id]/edit/page.tsx`：

```tsx
import { Suspense } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { notFound, redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { BriefForm } from "@/components/briefs/BriefForm";
import { requireRole } from "@/lib/auth/current-user";
import { loadOwnBrief } from "@/lib/briefs/brand";
import { todayTw } from "@/lib/briefs/dates";
import { isUuid, parseBriefStep } from "@/lib/briefs/query";
import { briefToInput, displayStatusOf } from "@/lib/briefs/view";

type Props = {
  params: Promise<{ id: string }>;
  searchParams: Promise<Record<string, string | string[] | undefined>>;
};

// params 與 searchParams 都是請求時才知道的值：在 <Suspense> 內讀（dynamic-routes.md「Without generateStaticParams」）
export default function EditBriefPage({ params, searchParams }: Props) {
  return (
    <div className="max-w-3xl">
      <Link href="/brand/briefs" className="text-sm text-muted">← 我的招募</Link>
      <h1 className="mt-2 text-[32px] font-bold text-ink">編輯招募</h1>
      <div className="mt-6">
        <Suspense fallback={<p className="text-muted">載入中…</p>}>
          <EditBrief params={params} searchParams={searchParams} />
        </Suspense>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

async function EditBrief({ params, searchParams }: Props) {
  const me = await requireRole("brand");
  const { id } = await params;
  if (!isUuid(id)) notFound();
  const brief = await loadOwnBrief(me.id, id);
  if (!brief) notFound();
  const today = todayTw();
  const display = displayStatusOf(brief.status, brief.deadline, today);
  // 已關閉或已截止不能編輯：回到詳情頁
  if (display === "closed" || display === "expired") redirect(`/brand/briefs/${id}`);
  const sp = await searchParams;
  return (
    <BriefForm
      briefId={brief.id} isOpen={brief.status === "open"} initial={briefToInput(brief)}
      initialStep={parseBriefStep(sp.step)} saved={sp.saved === "1"} userId={me.id}
      brandName={me.brand?.brandName ?? ""} brandLogoPath={me.brand?.logoPath ?? null} today={today}
    />
  );
}
```

- Produces: `ConfirmAction({ label, message, confirmLabel, danger?, onConfirm: () => Promise<ActionResult | undefined> })`（Task 9 也用）；`BriefOwnerActions({ briefId, display })`；`ApplicantList({ briefId, applicants })`；頁面 `/brand/briefs/<id>`（只有招募主，否則 404）。

- [ ] **Step 1: 確認區塊 `src/components/briefs/ConfirmAction.tsx`**

```tsx
"use client";

import { useId, useState, useTransition } from "react";

type Result = { ok: true } | { ok: false; error: string } | undefined;

type Props = {
  label: string;
  /** 確認區塊裡的說明文字 */
  message: string;
  confirmLabel: string;
  danger?: boolean;
  onConfirm: () => Promise<Result>;
};

/** 頁面內的確認區塊（不用瀏覽器的 confirm()）：先按按鈕，展開說明與「確定／取消」。 */
export function ConfirmAction({ label, message, confirmLabel, danger, onConfirm }: Props) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [pending, startTransition] = useTransition();
  const regionId = useId();

  function confirm() {
    setError(null);
    startTransition(async () => {
      try {
        const res = await onConfirm();
        if (res && !res.ok) setError(res.error);
        else setOpen(false);
      } catch {
        setError("發生錯誤，請稍後再試");
      }
    });
  }

  const tone = danger ? "border-danger text-danger" : "border-line text-ink";
  return (
    <div className="w-full sm:w-auto">
      {!open ? (
        <button
          type="button" onClick={() => setOpen(true)} aria-expanded={false} aria-controls={regionId}
          className={`h-10 w-full rounded-btn border bg-surface px-4 text-sm font-semibold sm:w-auto ${tone}`}
        >
          {label}
        </button>
      ) : (
        <div id={regionId} role="group" aria-label={label} className="rounded-card border border-line bg-surface p-4">
          <p className="text-sm text-ink">{message}</p>
          <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            <button
              type="button" onClick={confirm} disabled={pending}
              className={`h-10 rounded-btn px-4 text-sm font-semibold text-on-ink disabled:opacity-60 ${danger ? "bg-danger" : "bg-ink"}`}
            >
              {pending ? "處理中…" : confirmLabel}
            </button>
            <button
              type="button" onClick={() => { setOpen(false); setError(null); }} disabled={pending}
              className="h-10 rounded-btn border border-line px-4 text-sm font-semibold text-ink"
            >
              取消
            </button>
          </div>
          {error && <p role="alert" className="mt-2 text-sm text-danger">{error}</p>}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: 招募主的按鈕 `src/components/briefs/BriefOwnerActions.tsx`**

```tsx
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { closeBriefAction, deleteBriefAction } from "@/app/brand/briefs/actions";
import type { BriefDisplayStatus } from "@/lib/briefs/view";
import { ConfirmAction } from "./ConfirmAction";

const linkClass = "inline-flex h-10 w-full items-center justify-center rounded-btn border border-line bg-surface px-4 text-sm font-semibold text-ink sm:w-auto";

/** 招募詳情上方的按鈕：草稿（編輯、發佈、刪除）、發佈中（編輯、關閉）；已結束沒有按鈕。 */
export function BriefOwnerActions({ briefId, display }: { briefId: string; display: BriefDisplayStatus }) {
  if (display === "draft") {
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col items-stretch gap-2 sm:flex-row sm:flex-wrap sm:items-start">
        <Link href={`/brand/briefs/${briefId}/edit?step=1`} className={linkClass}>編輯</Link>
        <Link href={`/brand/briefs/${briefId}/edit?step=4`} className={`${linkClass} border-ink bg-ink text-on-ink`}>發佈</Link>
        <ConfirmAction
          label="刪除草稿" danger confirmLabel="確定刪除"
          message="刪除後無法復原，確定要刪除這則草稿嗎？"
          onConfirm={() => deleteBriefAction(briefId)}
        />
      </div>
    );
  }
  if (display === "open") {
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col items-stretch gap-2 sm:flex-row sm:flex-wrap sm:items-start">
        <Link href={`/brand/briefs/${briefId}/edit?step=1`} className={linkClass}>編輯</Link>
        <ConfirmAction
          label="關閉招募" danger confirmLabel="確定關閉"
          message="關閉後創作者就看不到這則招募，也不能再報名；已報名的紀錄會保留，仍可以錄取或婉拒。關閉後不能重新開啟。"
          onConfirm={() => closeBriefAction(briefId)}
        />
      </div>
    );
  }
  return null;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 報名者列表 `src/components/briefs/ApplicantList.tsx`**

```tsx
"use client";

import { useState, useTransition } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { MapPin } from "lucide-react";
import { decideApplicationAction } from "@/app/brand/briefs/actions";
import { Avatar } from "@/components/square/Avatar";
import { formatDateTimeTw } from "@/lib/briefs/dates";
import { APPLICANT_FILTERS, filterApplicants, proposedPriceLabel, type Applicant, type ApplicantFilter } from "@/lib/briefs/view";
import { formatNumber, formatPercent } from "@/lib/format";
import { ApplicationStatusBadge } from "./StatusBadges";

/** 報名者列表與篩選（全部、待處理、已錄取、已婉拒）。篩選只在畫面上，不寫進網址。 */
export function ApplicantList({ briefId, applicants }: { briefId: string; applicants: Applicant[] }) {
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<ApplicantFilter>("all");
  const shown = filterApplicants(applicants, filter);
  return (
    <div>
      <div role="group" aria-label="篩選報名者" className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
        {APPLICANT_FILTERS.map((f) => (
          <button
            key={f.value} type="button" onClick={() => setFilter(f.value)} aria-pressed={filter === f.value}
            className={`h-9 rounded-full border px-4 text-sm font-semibold ${filter === f.value ? "border-ink bg-ink text-on-ink" : "border-line bg-surface text-ink"}`}
          >
            {f.label} {filterApplicants(applicants, f.value).length}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
      {shown.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="mt-4 rounded-card border border-line bg-surface p-6 text-center text-sm text-muted">沒有符合的報名者</p>
      ) : (
        <ul className="mt-4 space-y-3">
          {shown.map((a) => <li key={a.id}><ApplicantItem a={a} briefId={briefId} /></li>)}
        </ul>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function ApplicantItem({ a, briefId }: { a: Applicant; briefId: string }) {
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [pending, startTransition] = useTransition();

  function decide(accept: boolean) {
    setError(null);
    startTransition(async () => {
      try {
        const res = await decideApplicationAction(a.id, briefId, accept);
        if (!res.ok) setError(res.error);
      } catch {
        setError("發生錯誤，請稍後再試");
      }
    });
  }

  return (
    <article className="rounded-card border border-line bg-surface p-4 md:p-5">
      <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
        <Avatar path={a.avatarPath} name={a.displayName} size="md" />
        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
            <Link href={`/c/${a.handle}`} className="min-w-0 break-words font-bold text-ink underline-offset-2 hover:underline">
              {a.displayName}
            </Link>
            <ApplicationStatusBadge status={a.status} viewer="brand" />
          </div>
          <p className="mt-1 flex flex-wrap gap-x-3 gap-y-0.5 text-xs text-muted">
            <span>粉絲 {formatNumber(a.followerCount)}</span>
            <span>互動率 {formatPercent(a.engagementRate)}</span>
            {a.region && <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-0.5"><MapPin size={12} strokeWidth={1.5} aria-hidden="true" />{a.region}</span>}
            <span>報名於 {formatDateTimeTw(a.createdAt)}</span>
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <dl className="mt-3 space-y-2 text-sm">
        <div>
          <dt className="text-xs text-muted">提出的報價</dt>
          <dd className="font-semibold text-ink">{proposedPriceLabel(a.proposedPrice)}</dd>
        </div>
        {a.message && (
          <div>
            <dt className="text-xs text-muted">給品牌的話</dt>
            <dd className="whitespace-pre-line break-words text-ink">{a.message}</dd>
          </div>
        )}
      </dl>
      {a.status === "applied" && (
        <div className="mt-4 flex gap-2">
          <button type="button" onClick={() => decide(true)} disabled={pending}
            className="h-10 flex-1 rounded-btn bg-ink px-4 text-sm font-semibold text-on-ink disabled:opacity-60 sm:flex-none">
            錄取
          </button>
          <button type="button" onClick={() => decide(false)} disabled={pending}
            className="h-10 flex-1 rounded-btn border border-line bg-surface px-4 text-sm font-semibold text-ink disabled:opacity-60 sm:flex-none">
            婉拒
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
      {error && <p role="alert" className="mt-2 text-sm text-danger">{error}</p>}
    </article>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { Suspense } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { notFound } from "next/navigation";
import { ApplicantList } from "@/components/briefs/ApplicantList";
import { BriefDetailView } from "@/components/briefs/BriefDetailView";
import { BriefOwnerActions } from "@/components/briefs/BriefOwnerActions";
import { BriefStatusBadge } from "@/components/briefs/StatusBadges";
import { requireRole } from "@/lib/auth/current-user";
import { loadApplicants, loadOwnBrief } from "@/lib/briefs/brand";
import { deadlineLabel, formatDate, todayTw } from "@/lib/briefs/dates";
import { isUuid } from "@/lib/briefs/query";
import { displayStatusOf, rewardLabel, UNTITLED_DRAFT } from "@/lib/briefs/view";

type Props = { params: Promise<{ id: string }> };

export default function BrandBriefPage({ params }: Props) {
  return (
    <div>
      <Link href="/brand/briefs" className="text-sm text-muted">← 我的招募</Link>
      <div className="mt-2">
        <Suspense fallback={<p className="text-muted">載入中…</p>}>
          <BriefDetail params={params} />
        </Suspense>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

async function BriefDetail({ params }: Props) {
  const me = await requireRole("brand");
  const { id } = await params;
  if (!isUuid(id)) notFound();
  // 只有招募主看得到：別人的或不存在都是 404
  const brief = await loadOwnBrief(me.id, id);
  if (!brief) notFound();
  const today = todayTw();
  const display = displayStatusOf(brief.status, brief.deadline, today);
  const applicants = brief.status === "draft" ? [] : await loadApplicants(id);
  const counted = applicants.filter((a) => a.status === "applied" || a.status === "accepted").length;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <header className="space-y-3">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          <h1 className="min-w-0 break-words text-[28px] font-bold text-ink md:text-[32px]">{brief.title || UNTITLED_DRAFT}</h1>
          <BriefStatusBadge status={display} />
        </div>
        <p className="flex flex-wrap gap-x-4 gap-y-1 text-sm text-muted">
          <span className="font-semibold text-primary">{rewardLabel(brief)}</span>
          <span>{brief.deadline ? `截止 ${formatDate(brief.deadline)}（${deadlineLabel(brief.deadline, today)}）` : "未設定截止日"}</span>
          {brief.status !== "draft" && <span>報名 {counted} 人</span>}
        </p>
        <BriefOwnerActions briefId={brief.id} display={display} />
      </header>

      <details className="rounded-card border border-line bg-surface">
        <summary className="cursor-pointer p-4 text-sm font-semibold text-ink">查看招募內容（創作者看到的樣子）</summary>
        <div className="p-2 pt-0 md:p-4 md:pt-0">
          <BriefDetailView
            brief={{ ...brief, brandName: me.brand?.brandName ?? "", brandLogoPath: me.brand?.logoPath ?? null, applicantCount: counted }}
            today={today}
          />
        </div>
      </details>

      <section aria-labelledby="applicants-title">
        <h2 id="applicants-title" className="text-lg font-bold text-ink">報名者</h2>
        <div className="mt-3">
          {brief.status === "draft" ? (
            <p className="rounded-card border border-line bg-surface p-6 text-center text-sm text-muted">發佈後，創作者的報名會出現在這裡</p>
          ) : applicants.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="rounded-card border border-line bg-surface p-6 text-center text-sm text-muted">還沒有人報名</p>
          ) : (
            <ApplicantList briefId={brief.id} applicants={applicants} />
          )}
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import Link from "next/link";
import { Check } from "lucide-react";
import { deadlineLabel } from "@/lib/briefs/dates";
import { audienceLabel, rewardLabel, type Opportunity } from "@/lib/briefs/view";
import { formatNumber } from "@/lib/format";
import { BrandMark } from "./BrandMark";
import { BriefCover } from "./BriefCover";

/** 機會卡片（ui-design §5.4）。整張可點，連到 /creator/briefs/<id>。 */
export function OpportunityCard({ o, today }: { o: Opportunity; today: string }) {
  const applied = o.myStatus !== null;
  return (
    <article className="relative flex h-full flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-surface focus-within:ring-2 focus-within:ring-ink hover:border-ink">
      <BriefCover coverPath={o.coverPath} brandName={o.brandName} brandLogoPath={o.brandLogoPath} />
      {applied && (
        <span className="absolute right-3 top-3 rounded-full bg-ink px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold text-on-ink">
          {o.myStatus === "withdrawn" ? "已撤回" : "已報名"}
        </span>
      )}
      <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col gap-3 p-5">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <BrandMark name={o.brandName} logoPath={o.brandLogoPath} size="sm" />
          <p className="min-w-0 truncate text-sm text-muted">{o.brandName}</p>
        </div>
        <div>
          <h3 className="break-words font-bold text-ink">
            <Link href={`/creator/briefs/${o.id}`} className="after:absolute after:inset-0 after:content-[''] focus-visible:outline-none">
              {o.title}
            </Link>
          </h3>
          <p className="mt-0.5 truncate text-xs text-muted">{o.collabTypes.join("、")}</p>
        </div>
        <p className="text-lg font-bold text-primary">{rewardLabel(o)}</p>
        {o.isMatch && (
          <p className="inline-flex w-fit items-center gap-1 rounded-full bg-success-soft px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold text-success">
            <Check size={14} strokeWidth={1.5} aria-hidden="true" />符合你的條件
          </p>
        )}
        <dl className="mt-auto grid grid-cols-2 gap-x-3 gap-y-1 text-xs">
          <div className="min-w-0"><dt className="sr-only">門檻</dt><dd className="truncate text-muted">{audienceLabel(o.minFollowers)}</dd></div>
          <div className="min-w-0"><dt className="sr-only">地區</dt><dd className="truncate text-muted">{o.region ?? "地區不限"}</dd></div>
          <div><dt className="sr-only">已報名</dt><dd className="text-muted">已報名 {formatNumber(o.applicantCount)} 人</dd></div>
          <div><dt className="sr-only">截止</dt><dd className="font-semibold text-ink">{deadlineLabel(o.deadline, today)}</dd></div>
        </dl>
      </div>
    </article>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import Link from "next/link";
import { OPPORTUNITY_FILTERS, opportunityHref, sameOpportunityQuery, type OpportunityQuery } from "@/lib/briefs/query";

/** 「機會／我的報名」分頁，以及機會的篩選標籤（一次只選一個）。全部是連結，狀態只在網址。 */
export function OpportunityFilters({ query }: { query: OpportunityQuery }) {
  const applied = query.tab === "applied";
  const tab = "h-10 rounded-btn px-4 text-sm font-semibold leading-10";
  return (
    <div className="space-y-3">
      <nav aria-label="機會分頁" className="flex gap-2">
        <Link href="/creator" aria-current={!applied ? "page" : undefined}
          className={`${tab} ${!applied ? "bg-ink text-on-ink" : "border border-line bg-surface text-ink"}`}>
          機會
        </Link>
        <Link href="/creator?tab=applied" aria-current={applied ? "page" : undefined}
          className={`${tab} ${applied ? "bg-ink text-on-ink" : "border border-line bg-surface text-ink"}`}>
          我的報名
        </Link>
      </nav>
      {!applied && (
        // relative：捲動容器裡有 sr-only 文字時，避免它撐出整頁的水平捲軸
        <nav aria-label="篩選機會" className="relative overflow-x-auto pb-1">
          <ul className="flex w-max gap-2">
            {OPPORTUNITY_FILTERS.map((f) => {
              const active = sameOpportunityQuery(f.query, query);
              return (
                <li key={f.label}>
                  <Link
                    href={opportunityHref(f.query)} aria-current={active ? "page" : undefined}
                    className={`inline-block h-9 whitespace-nowrap rounded-full border px-4 text-sm font-medium leading-9 ${active ? "border-ink bg-ink text-on-ink" : "border-line bg-surface text-ink"}`}
                  >
                    {f.label}
                  </Link>
                </li>
              );
            })}
            {query.tab === "category" && !OPPORTUNITY_FILTERS.some((f) => sameOpportunityQuery(f.query, query)) && (
              <li>
                <span aria-current="page" className="inline-block h-9 whitespace-nowrap rounded-full border border-ink bg-ink px-4 text-sm font-medium leading-9 text-on-ink">
                  {query.category}
                </span>
              </li>
            )}
          </ul>
        </nav>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 結果區與載入更多 `src/components/briefs/OpportunityResults.tsx`**

```tsx
"use client";

import { useState, useTransition } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { loadMoreOpportunitiesAction, type LoadMoreOpportunitiesResult } from "@/app/creator/actions";
import type { OpportunityQuery } from "@/lib/briefs/query";
import { appendUniqueById, type Opportunity, type OpportunityPage } from "@/lib/briefs/view";
import { OpportunityCard } from "./OpportunityCard";

type Props = { initial: OpportunityPage; query: OpportunityQuery; queryKey: string; today: string };

/**
 * 以 key={`${bfcacheId}|${queryKey}`} 重設結果區：換篩選或從別頁重新進來時從第一批開始，
 * 上一頁（back/forward）則保留已載入的結果（與廣場相同，見計畫 4「設計決定」2）。
 */
export function OpportunityResults(props: Props) {
  const router = useRouter();
  return <Results key={`${router.bfcacheId}|${props.queryKey}`} {...props} />;
}

async function fetchMore(queryKey: string, offset: number): Promise<LoadMoreOpportunitiesResult> {
  try {
    return (await loadMoreOpportunitiesAction(queryKey, offset)) ?? { ok: false, error: "請重新登入" };
  } catch {
    return { ok: false, error: "載入失敗，請稍後再試" };
  }
}

function Results({ initial, query, queryKey, today }: Props) {
  const [extra, setExtra] = useState<Opportunity[]>([]);
  const [fetched, setFetched] = useState(0);
  const [moreAfterLoad, setMoreAfterLoad] = useState<boolean | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [loading, startLoading] = useTransition();

  const items = appendUniqueById(initial.items, extra);
  const hasMore = moreAfterLoad ?? initial.hasMore;

  function loadMore() {
    setError(null);
    startLoading(async () => {
      const res = await fetchMore(queryKey, initial.items.length + fetched);
      startLoading(() => {
        if (!res.ok) {
          setError(res.error);
          return;
        }
        setExtra((prev) => appendUniqueById(prev, res.items));
        setFetched((n) => n + res.items.length);
        setMoreAfterLoad(res.hasMore);
      });
    });
  }

  if (items.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-card border border-line bg-surface p-8 text-center">
        {query.tab === "match" ? (
          <>
            <p className="font-semibold text-ink">目前沒有完全符合的招募，看看全部吧</p>
            <Link href="/creator?tab=all" className="mt-4 inline-flex h-10 items-center rounded-btn border border-line px-4 text-sm font-semibold text-ink hover:bg-primary-soft">
              看全部招募
            </Link>
          </>
        ) : (
          <p className="font-semibold text-ink">目前沒有開放中的招募</p>
        )}
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <ul className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3">
        {items.map((o) => <li key={o.id}><OpportunityCard o={o} today={today} /></li>)}
      </ul>
      {error && <p role="alert" className="mt-4 text-center text-sm text-danger">{error}</p>}
      {hasMore && (
        <div className="mt-6 flex justify-center">
          <button
            type="button" onClick={loadMore} disabled={loading}
            className="h-11 rounded-btn border border-line bg-surface px-6 text-sm font-semibold text-ink hover:bg-primary-soft disabled:opacity-60"
          >
            {loading ? "載入中…" : "載入更多"}
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import Link from "next/link";
import { formatDateTw } from "@/lib/briefs/dates";
import { displayStatusOf, rewardLabel, type MyApplication } from "@/lib/briefs/view";
import { ApplicationStatusBadge } from "./StatusBadges";

/** 我的報名：報名過的招募與狀態（待回覆、已錄取、未錄取、已撤回）。 */
export function MyApplicationList({ items, today }: { items: MyApplication[]; today: string }) {
  if (items.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-card border border-line bg-surface p-8 text-center">
        <p className="font-semibold text-ink">還沒有報名任何招募</p>
        <Link href="/creator" className="mt-4 inline-flex h-10 items-center rounded-btn border border-line px-4 text-sm font-semibold text-ink hover:bg-primary-soft">
          看看適合我的機會
        </Link>
      </div>
    );
  }
  return (
    <ul className="divide-y divide-line overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-surface">
      {items.map((a) => {
        const ended = displayStatusOf(a.brief.status, a.brief.deadline, today) !== "open";
        return (
          <li key={a.id}>
            <Link href={`/creator/briefs/${a.brief.id}`} className="block p-4 hover:bg-primary-soft focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-inset focus-visible:ring-ink md:p-5">
              <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                <div className="min-w-0">
                  <p className="truncate text-xs text-muted">{a.brief.brandName}</p>
                  <p className="break-words font-semibold text-ink">{a.brief.title}</p>
                </div>
                <ApplicationStatusBadge status={a.status} viewer="creator" />
              </div>
              <p className="mt-1 flex flex-wrap gap-x-4 gap-y-1 text-sm text-muted">
                <span className="font-semibold text-primary">{rewardLabel(a.brief)}</span>
                <span>報名於 {formatDateTw(a.createdAt)}</span>
                {ended && <span>招募已結束</span>}
              </p>
            </Link>
          </li>
        );
      })}
    </ul>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { Suspense } from "react";
import { MyApplicationList } from "@/components/briefs/MyApplicationList";
import { OpportunityFilters } from "@/components/briefs/OpportunityFilters";
import { OpportunityResults } from "@/components/briefs/OpportunityResults";
import { requireRole } from "@/lib/auth/current-user";
import { listOpportunities, loadMyApplications } from "@/lib/briefs/creator";
import { todayTw } from "@/lib/briefs/dates";
import { opportunityQueryString, parseOpportunityQuery } from "@/lib/briefs/query";

type Props = { searchParams: Promise<Record<string, string | string[] | undefined>> };

// 頁面殼是靜態的；searchParams 與 session 都在 <Suspense> 內讀（cacheComponents）
export default function CreatorHome({ searchParams }: Props) {
  return (
    <div>
      <h1 className="text-[32px] font-bold text-ink">機會</h1>
      <div className="mt-4">
        <Suspense fallback={<OpportunitySkeleton />}>
          <Opportunities searchParams={searchParams} />
        </Suspense>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

async function Opportunities({ searchParams }: Props) {
  // layout 的 Gate 在用戶端換頁時不會重跑，頁面自己也要檢查
  const me = await requireRole("creator");
  const query = parseOpportunityQuery(await searchParams);
  const today = todayTw();
  if (query.tab === "applied") {
    const items = await loadMyApplications(me.id);
    return (
      <div className="space-y-4">
        <OpportunityFilters query={query} />
        <MyApplicationList items={items} today={today} />
      </div>
    );
  }
  const initial = await listOpportunities(query, 0);
  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <OpportunityFilters query={query} />
      <OpportunityResults initial={initial} query={query} queryKey={opportunityQueryString(query)} today={today} />
    </div>
  );
}

function OpportunitySkeleton() {
  return (
    <div aria-busy="true" className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3">
      <p className="sr-only">載入中…</p>
      {[0, 1, 2].map((i) => <div key={i} className="h-80 rounded-card border border-line bg-surface" />)}
    </div>
  );
}
```

### Task 9: 創作者招募詳情、報名與撤回

**Files:**
- Create: `src/components/briefs/ApplyPanel.tsx`、`src/app/creator/briefs/[id]/page.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `applyToBriefAction`、`withdrawApplicationAction`、`ApplyFormState`（Task 4）；`loadOpportunity`；`BriefDetailView`、`ConfirmAction`、`ApplicationStatusBadge`；`Field`、`inputClass`；`detailDataOf`、`displayStatusOf`、`proposedPriceLabel`、`isUuid`。
- Produces: `ApplyPanel({ briefId, approved, ended, isMatch, application })`；頁面 `/creator/briefs/<id>`（草稿與看不到的 → 404）。

- [ ] **Step 1: 報名區塊 `src/components/briefs/ApplyPanel.tsx`**

手機：`sticky` 在底部導覽列上方（底部列約 4.1rem 高）；桌面：右欄、`sticky top-6`。不符合條件時先顯示「你的條件不完全符合，仍要報名嗎？」再展開表單。

```tsx
"use client";

import { useActionState, useState } from "react";
import { applyToBriefAction, withdrawApplicationAction, type ApplyFormState } from "@/app/creator/actions";
import { Field, inputClass } from "@/components/forms/Field";
import { proposedPriceLabel, type ApplicationStatus } from "@/lib/briefs/view";
import { ConfirmAction } from "./ConfirmAction";
import { ApplicationStatusBadge } from "./StatusBadges";

type Props = {
  briefId: string;
  approved: boolean;
  /** 已關閉或已截止 */
  ended: boolean;
  isMatch: boolean;
  application: { id: string; status: ApplicationStatus; message: string | null; proposedPrice: number | null } | null;
};

// 手機：固定在底部（底部導覽列上方）；桌面：右欄、捲動時固定在上方
const shell = "sticky bottom-[calc(4.75rem+env(safe-area-inset-bottom))] z-[5] max-h-[70vh] overflow-y-auto rounded-card border border-line bg-surface p-4 md:bottom-auto md:top-6 md:max-h-none md:p-5";
const primary = "h-11 w-full rounded-btn bg-ink px-6 font-semibold text-on-ink disabled:opacity-60";

export function ApplyPanel({ briefId, approved, ended, isMatch, application }: Props) {
  if (application) {
    return (
      <aside aria-label="報名" className={shell}>
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
          <p className="font-semibold text-ink">你已報名</p>
          <ApplicationStatusBadge status={application.status} viewer="creator" />
        </div>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted">報價：{proposedPriceLabel(application.proposedPrice)}</p>
        {application.message && <p className="mt-1 whitespace-pre-line break-words text-sm text-ink">{application.message}</p>}
        {application.status === "applied" && (
          <div className="mt-3">
            <ConfirmAction
              label="撤回報名" danger confirmLabel="確定撤回"
              message="撤回後不能再報名這則招募，確定要撤回嗎？"
              onConfirm={() => withdrawApplicationAction(application.id, briefId)}
            />
          </div>
        )}
      </aside>
    );
  }
  if (ended) {
    return (
      <aside aria-label="報名" className={shell}>
        <button type="button" disabled className={primary}>已結束</button>
      </aside>
    );
  }
  if (!approved) {
    return (
      <aside aria-label="報名" className={shell}>
        <button type="button" disabled aria-describedby="apply-note" className={primary}>報名</button>
        <p id="apply-note" className="mt-2 text-center text-sm text-muted">審核通過後即可報名</p>
      </aside>
    );
  }
  return <ApplyForm briefId={briefId} isMatch={isMatch} />;
}

function ApplyForm({ briefId, isMatch }: { briefId: string; isMatch: boolean }) {
  const [stage, setStage] = useState<"idle" | "warn" | "form">("idle");
  const [state, action, pending] = useActionState<ApplyFormState, FormData>(applyToBriefAction, {});
  const fe = state.fieldErrors ?? {};

  if (stage === "idle") {
    return (
      <aside aria-label="報名" className={shell}>
        <button type="button" onClick={() => setStage(isMatch ? "form" : "warn")} className={primary}>報名</button>
      </aside>
    );
  }
  if (stage === "warn") {
    return (
      <aside aria-label="報名" className={shell}>
        <p role="status" className="text-sm font-semibold text-ink">你的條件不完全符合，仍要報名嗎？</p>
        <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted">條件是給品牌的參考，不是硬性限制。</p>
        <div className="mt-3 flex gap-2">
          <button type="button" onClick={() => setStage("form")} className="h-10 flex-1 rounded-btn bg-ink text-sm font-semibold text-on-ink">仍要報名</button>
          <button type="button" onClick={() => setStage("idle")} className="h-10 flex-1 rounded-btn border border-line text-sm font-semibold text-ink">取消</button>
        </div>
      </aside>
    );
  }
  return (
    <aside aria-label="報名" className={shell}>
      <form action={action} noValidate className="space-y-3">
        <input type="hidden" name="briefId" value={briefId} />
        <Field label="給品牌的話（選填）" error={fe.message} hint="500 字以內">
          {(c) => (
            <textarea {...c} name="message" rows={4}
              className="w-full rounded-input border border-line bg-surface px-3 py-2 outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-ink" />
          )}
        </Field>
        <Field label="提出不同的報價（NT$，選填）" error={fe.proposedPrice} hint="留空代表依招募預算">
          {(c) => <input {...c} name="proposedPrice" inputMode="numeric" placeholder="例如 8000" className={inputClass} />}
        </Field>
        {state.formError && <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-danger">{state.formError}</p>}
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          <button disabled={pending} className={primary}>{pending ? "送出中…" : "送出報名"}</button>
          <button type="button" onClick={() => setStage("idle")} disabled={pending}
            className="h-11 shrink-0 rounded-btn border border-line px-4 font-semibold text-ink">取消</button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </aside>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: 頁面 `src/app/creator/briefs/[id]/page.tsx`**

```tsx
import { Suspense } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { notFound } from "next/navigation";
import { ApplyPanel } from "@/components/briefs/ApplyPanel";
import { BriefDetailView } from "@/components/briefs/BriefDetailView";
import { requireRole } from "@/lib/auth/current-user";
import { loadOpportunity } from "@/lib/briefs/creator";
import { todayTw } from "@/lib/briefs/dates";
import { isUuid } from "@/lib/briefs/query";
import { detailDataOf, displayStatusOf } from "@/lib/briefs/view";

type Props = { params: Promise<{ id: string }> };

export default function OpportunityPage({ params }: Props) {
  return (
    <div>
      <Link href="/creator" className="text-sm text-muted">← 機會</Link>
      <div className="mt-2">
        <Suspense fallback={<p className="text-muted">載入中…</p>}>
          <Opportunity params={params} />
        </Suspense>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

async function Opportunity({ params }: Props) {
  const me = await requireRole("creator");
  const { id } = await params;
  if (!isUuid(id)) notFound();
  // 草稿、別人看不到的招募（已截止且沒報名過）一律 404
  const o = await loadOpportunity(id);
  if (!o) notFound();
  const today = todayTw();
  const ended = displayStatusOf(o.status, o.deadline, today) !== "open";
  return (
    <BriefDetailView
      brief={detailDataOf(o)} today={today} match={o.match}
      aside={
        <ApplyPanel
          briefId={o.id} approved={me.creator?.status === "approved"} ended={ended} isMatch={o.isMatch}
          application={o.application}
        />
      }
    />
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: README**

在「## 正式環境（Hosted Supabase）設定」底下、`創作者頭像與作品的 Storage bucket creator-media …` 那一段**之後**加上：

```markdown
招募封面的 Storage bucket `brief-covers` 由 migration `20261124000600_brief_covers.sql` 建立（公開、5 MB、只接受 JPG／PNG／WebP，只有品牌能上傳到自己的資料夾），同樣隨 `db push --remote` 一起建好。

`20261124000500_briefs.sql` 會把 `briefs.cover_url` 改名為 `cover_path`。它開頭有保護檢查：**正式資料庫的 `briefs` 只要有任何一列，migration 就會停下來**（錯誤訊息 `briefs has rows …`）。目前沒有任何程式寫入招募，所以應該是 0 筆；如果真的停下來，先確認資料、手動搬移後再推。
```

把「**部署順序：先推資料庫，再合併 PR。**」那一段整段換成：

```markdown
**部署順序：先推資料庫，再合併 PR。** Vercel 在 `main` 合併後立刻部署；新版程式會呼叫新 migration 建立的函式（例如計畫 4 的 `search_creators`、計畫 5 的 `save_brief`、`list_opportunities`，`20261124000500_briefs.sql`、`20261124000600_brief_covers.sql`），資料庫還沒更新時 `/brand`、`/brand/briefs`、`/creator` 會出錯。所以每次 PR 有新 migration 時，先執行上面的 `npx supabase db push --remote`，確認成功後再合併。
```

把第 9 項（排程清理）換成：

```markdown
9. 排程清理沒被引用的 Storage 物件：`brand-logos`（沒被 `brand_profiles.logo_path` 引用）、`creator-media`（沒被 `creator_profiles.avatar_path`、`creator_portfolio_items.image_path` 引用）與 `brief-covers`（沒被 `briefs.cover_path` 引用），都只刪超過 24 小時的，見 `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-11-22-foundation-followups.md`。
```

- [ ] **Step 2: followups**

`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-11-22-foundation-followups.md`：

在「## 執行中做出的決定」的表格最後加：

```markdown
| 招募寫入只走函式 | `briefs`、`brief_applications` 收回 anon／authenticated／service_role 的寫入（anon 連讀取都沒有）；寫入只能經由 `save_brief`、`close_brief`、`delete_brief`、`apply_to_brief`、`withdraw_application`、`decide_application`。 |
| 台灣日期 | 「今天」一律是 `today_tw()`（`Asia/Taipei`）；TS 的 `todayTw()` 同一條規則。`today_tw(p_now)` 的參數只給測試驗證跨日邊界，policy 與函式一律不帶參數呼叫。 |
| 招募狀態 | `draft → open → closed`；「已截止」由 `deadline < today_tw()` 算出，不寫進資料庫。截止日當天可以報名；截止或關閉後品牌仍可錄取／婉拒。只有草稿能刪除；不能重新開啟、不能改回草稿。 |
| 未審核創作者 | 可以瀏覽機會與詳情，不能報名（`42501 creator not approved`）。 |
| 招募可見性 | 招募主、管理員、創作者（開放中且未截止）、報名過的人（任何狀態）。品牌看不到其他品牌的招募。判斷寫在 definer helper `can_view_brief`（避免兩張表的 policy 互相遞迴）。 |
| 錄取 | 這次只標記 `accepted`；建立對話與合作案留給計畫 6、7。錄取或婉拒後不能改；撤回後不能再報名同一則。 |
| 預算 0 | 視為沒填（存成 null）；「現金」篩選與「預算或產品至少一項」都依此判斷。 |
```

把「### 4. 創作者廣場、5. 招募與機會頁」底下這三行：

```markdown
- 報名的狀態變更需要 definer 函式（目前沒有開放 UPDATE）。錄取時，要在同一個 transaction 裡建立對話和合作案。
- 待決定：尚未審核的創作者能不能報名；超過截止日但狀態仍是 open 的招募要不要擋；品牌刪除招募時，報名紀錄會一起被刪除（cascade），這樣是否可接受。
```
```markdown
- 搜尋與篩選必須能容忍清單以外的值 → 廣場已做到（計畫 4：網址值清理後照常比對，比對不到就是沒有結果）；計畫 5 的招募篩選比照辦理。若需要嚴格清單，要在資料庫端強制。
```
分別換成：

```markdown
- 報名的狀態變更需要 definer 函式 → 已完成（計畫 5：`withdraw_application`、`decide_application`）。錄取時在同一個 transaction 建立對話和合作案 → 計畫 6、7 接上 `decide_application`。
- ~~待決定：未審核能不能報名、截止後要不要擋、刪除招募時報名一起刪~~ → 已決定（計畫 5）：未審核不能報名；截止後不能報名；只有草稿能刪除（草稿沒有報名）。
```
```markdown
- 搜尋與篩選必須能容忍清單以外的值 → 廣場已做到（計畫 4）；招募也做到（計畫 5：`?tab=`、`?cat=`、`?step=` 的垃圾值照常顯示，清單外的內容領域只是沒有結果）。若需要嚴格清單，要在資料庫端強制。
```

同一節的「索引」那一行：

在同一節最後加上：

```markdown
- 計畫 5 新增的後續事項：
  - `brief-covers` 孤兒物件（換封面、刪草稿時刪檔失敗）與其他 bucket 共用同一個清理排程（尚未實作）。草稿的封面網址若被猜到也看得到（公開 bucket，路徑含 UUID）。
  - NUL（U+0000）只在招募與報名表單擋（`hasBadChar`）；其他既有表單仍會得到 22P05 →「發生錯誤」。要全面處理時，把 `hasBidi` 的呼叫點都換成 `hasBadChar`。
  - 「我的報名」最多列 100 筆、沒有分頁；報名者列表沒有分頁；品牌「我的招募」最多 200 則。
  - 招募與機會頁不快取（每次請求以使用者 client 讀取）；截止日在台灣午夜跨日時，已開著的頁面要重新整理才會更新。
  - 404 在 `<Suspense>` 內觸發，回應碼是 200 + `noindex`（與 `/c/[handle]` 相同）。
  - 「適合我」的內容領域與地區比對是完全相等（清單外的值不會模糊比對）。
  - 錄取或婉拒沒有通知（spec 不在範圍內）；錄取後建立對話、合作案在計畫 6、7。
  - 品牌刪除帳號時，招募與報名會 cascade 刪除（帳號刪除流程見計畫 8 的 soft delete 項目）。
```

- [ ] **Step 7: 端對端操作（瀏覽器自動化，例如 chrome-devtools MCP）**

登入一律用「測試快速登入」（`http://127.0.0.1:3000/login` 的「測試品牌」／「測試創作者」按鈕，等同 `POST /auth/dev-login`，`account=brand|creator`），不要用 email 驗證碼（本機每小時只能寄 2 封）。品牌與創作者用兩個獨立的瀏覽器 context（或登出再登入）。

測試創作者（`devcreator`）的資料：粉絲 1,000、基隆市、美妝、沒填想接的合作（合作形式不檢查）。

每個標「截圖」的步驟，在寬度 390 與 1280 各截一張，存到 Step 5 印出的資料夾。每次截圖前執行 `document.documentElement.scrollWidth <= window.innerWidth`，必須是 `true`。

1. **品牌照四步驟刊登（中途存草稿、重新整理後接著填、最後發佈）**：以測試品牌登入 → 導覽列「我的招募」→ 空狀態「還沒有招募，刊登第一則吧」（截圖）→「刊登招募」。
   - 第 1 步：案名「冬季保養新品開箱」、說明兩行文字、合作形式「IG 貼文」、上傳 `cover.jpg`（預覽出現圖片）→「下一步」。網址變成 `?step=2`。
   - 第 2 步：內容領域「美妝」、最低粉絲數 `500`、地區「不限」→ 按「存成草稿」→ 網址變成 `/brand/briefs/<id>/edit?step=2&saved=1`，顯示「已存成草稿」，網址的 `saved=1` 隨即消失。
   - 重新整理 → 仍在第 2 步，內容領域「美妝」與粉絲數 500 從資料庫讀回來；按「上一步」確認案名、說明、封面都在（截圖：第 2 步）。
   - 第 3 步：直接按「下一步」→ 出現「預算和產品至少要有一項」「請選擇截止日」。填預算 `5,000`／`20,000`、截止日選 14 天後 →「下一步」。
   - 第 4 步：預覽顯示「NT$5,000–20,000」、封面、條件（截圖：預覽）→「發佈」→ 跳到 `/brand/briefs/<id>`，狀態「發佈中」、「還沒有人報名」。
   - 再刊登兩則：「限時動態互惠」（合作形式「限時動態」、只勾「提供產品」、截止日 7 天後，直接走到第 4 步發佈）；「要刪除的草稿」（只填案名，第 1 步就按「存成草稿」）。
   - 回「我的招募」：「發佈中 2」「草稿 1」，列表顯示截止日（剩幾天）、報名人數（待處理）、建立日期（截圖）。
   - 手改網址 `/brand/briefs/new?step=abc` → 停在第 1 步；`/brand/briefs/not-a-uuid` → 404 畫面。
2. **建立「已截止」的招募與報名（只能用 SQL，介面不允許過去的截止日）**：
   ```bash
   psql postgresql://postgres:postgres@127.0.0.1:54322/postgres -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 <<'SQL'
   with b as (
     insert into public.briefs (brand_id, title, description, collab_types, includes_product, deadline, status, published_at)
     select u.id, '已截止的舊招募', '昨天截止', array['IG 貼文'], true, public.today_tw() - 1, 'open', now() - interval '10 days'
       from auth.users u where u.email = 'dev-brand@kollink.test'
     returning id
   )
   insert into public.brief_applications (brief_id, creator_id, message, status)
   select b.id, '00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000e2e50012', '截止前報名的', 'applied' from b;
   SQL
   ```
   Expected：`INSERT 0 1`。
3. **未審核的創作者看得到、不能報名**：以測試創作者登入（會被自動設為已審核），再用 SQL 改成待審核：
   ```bash
   psql postgresql://postgres:postgres@127.0.0.1:54322/postgres -c "update public.creator_profiles set status = 'pending' where handle = 'devcreator';"
   ```
   重新整理 `/creator` → 上方有「審核中」橫幅、看得到「冬季保養新品開箱」→ 點進詳情 → 報名按鈕停用、下方「審核通過後即可報名」（截圖：390 時按鈕固定在底部導覽列上方）。改回已審核：
   ```bash
   psql postgresql://postgres:postgres@127.0.0.1:54322/postgres -c "update public.creator_profiles set status = 'approved' where handle = 'devcreator';"
   ```
4. **創作者在「適合我」看到招募並報名**：`/creator` → 預設「適合我」，卡片「冬季保養新品開箱」有封面、品牌名、「IG 貼文」、主色酬勞「NT$5,000–20,000」、「符合你的條件」、「粉絲 500 以上」「地區不限」「已報名 0 人」「剩 14 天」（截圖）。旅遊招募不在這裡。
   - 點「全部」→ 網址 `?tab=all`，依截止日由近到遠（第一張是「限時動態互惠」）、共 24 則、底部「載入更多」→ 按下後總共 24 張、沒有重複（`[...document.querySelectorAll('article h3')].map(e => e.textContent)` 的長度與 `new Set` 大小都是 24）、「載入更多」消失（截圖）。「已截止的舊招募」不在列表裡。
   - 點「旅遊」→ 只有 22 則旅遊招募；點「現金」→ 只有「冬季保養新品開箱」；點「互惠」→「限時動態互惠」與 22 則旅遊招募。手改網址 `/creator?cat=不存在&tab=drop` → 頁面正常、空狀態「目前沒有開放中的招募」、篩選列多出一個已選的「不存在」。
   - 回「適合我」點「冬季保養新品開箱」→ 詳情：條件四項都打勾、說明保留換行、已報名 0 人；1280 時報名區在右欄（截圖）。按「報名」→ 填「給品牌的話」`很喜歡這個系列，可以拍開箱`、報價 `8,000` →「送出報名」→ 區塊變成「你已報名」「待回覆」、報價 NT$8,000、留言；已報名人數變成 1（截圖）。
   - 回 `/creator`：卡片右上角「已報名」。
5. **創作者撤回報名**：打開「限時動態互惠」→ 條件中「合作形式」也是打勾（沒填想接的合作）→ 報名（不填留言）→「撤回報名」→ 頁內確認區塊（不是瀏覽器對話框）→「確定撤回」→ 狀態「已撤回」、沒有報名按鈕（截圖）。
6. **兩位種子創作者也報名「冬季保養新品開箱」（SQL）**：
   ```bash
   psql postgresql://postgres:postgres@127.0.0.1:54322/postgres -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 <<'SQL'
   insert into public.brief_applications (brief_id, creator_id, message, proposed_price_twd)
   select b.id, c.id, c.msg, c.price
     from public.briefs b,
          (values ('00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000e2e50011'::uuid, '我有很多保養內容', 12000),
                  ('00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000e2e50012'::uuid, null, null)) as c(id, msg, price)
    where b.title = '冬季保養新品開箱';
   SQL
   ```
   Expected：`INSERT 0 2`。
7. **品牌錄取一位、婉拒一位**：品牌打開「冬季保養新品開箱」→ 報名者 3 位（最新的在前）：名稱連到 `/c/<handle>`、粉絲、互動率、地區、留言、報價（`E2E 阿哲` 顯示「依招募預算」）、報名時間、狀態「待處理」（截圖）。
   - 對「測試創作者」按「錄取」→ 狀態「已錄取」、按鈕消失；對 `E2E 小花` 按「婉拒」→「已婉拒」。篩選「待處理 1」「已錄取 1」「已婉拒 1」各自正確（截圖：已錄取）。
   - 創作者 `/creator?tab=applied`（「我的報名」）→「冬季保養新品開箱」是「已錄取」、「限時動態互惠」是「已撤回」（截圖）。
8. **品牌關閉招募，機會頁不再顯示**：品牌打開「限時動態互惠」→「關閉招募」→ 確認區塊 →「確定關閉」→ 狀態「已關閉」、沒有編輯與關閉按鈕。創作者 `/creator?tab=all` → 沒有「限時動態互惠」（共 23 則）；「我的報名」仍有它，點進去顯示內容與「你已報名／已撤回」（報名過的人仍看得到）。
9. **截止日過了：機會頁不顯示，品牌仍可錄取**：創作者 `/creator?tab=all` 沒有「已截止的舊招募」；直接開它的網址（從 `psql -Atc "select id from public.briefs where title = '已截止的舊招募'"` 取得）→ 404 畫面（創作者沒報名過）。品牌「我的招募」→「已結束」分頁有「已截止」與「已關閉」兩則 → 打開「已截止的舊招募」→ 狀態「已截止」、沒有編輯按鈕；手改網址到 `/edit` → 被導回詳情；對 `E2E 阿哲` 按「錄取」→「已錄取」（截圖）。
10. **刪除草稿**：品牌「我的招募」→「草稿」→「要刪除的草稿」→「刪除草稿」→ 確認區塊 →「確定刪除」→ 回到 `/brand/briefs?tab=draft`、草稿數 0。

- [ ] **Step 8: 關閉 server、清理種子資料**

```bash
kill <Step 6 的 PID>
lsof -iTCP:3000 -sTCP:LISTEN || echo "已關閉"
COVERS=$(psql postgresql://postgres:postgres@127.0.0.1:54322/postgres -Atc "select coalesce(json_agg(b.cover_path), '[]') from public.briefs b join auth.users u on u.id = b.brand_id where u.email = 'dev-brand@kollink.test' and b.cover_path is not null")
set -a; source .env.local; set +a
curl -s -X DELETE "$NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL/storage/v1/object/brief-covers" -H "apikey: $SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY" -H "Authorization: Bearer $SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" -d "{\"prefixes\":$COVERS}"
psql postgresql://postgres:postgres@127.0.0.1:54322/postgres -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 <<'SQL'
delete from public.briefs b using auth.users u where u.id = b.brand_id and u.email = 'dev-brand@kollink.test';
delete from auth.users where id::text like '00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000e2e5%';
select count(*) from public.briefs;
SQL
npx supabase test db
git status --short
```
Expected：「已關閉」；DELETE 回傳刪掉的封面物件（第 1 步上傳的那一張）；`DELETE 3`（測試品牌的三則，報名一起 cascade）、`DELETE 3`、最後的 count 是 `0`；`supabase test db` 全過；`git status` 乾淨（截圖不在 repo 裡；若 `AGENTS.md` 被 `next dev` 改了就照 Step 6 commit）。把截圖資料夾路徑與每張截圖的檔名回報給 controller。
